[HUF_SRC_HOST_DATUM] Original Host and integral projective Datum, positivity and exact trace identities
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/TwoWeightEqOneAddRoot.lean#L14-L49
structure Host (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] where
  d : ℕ
  d_mod : d % 16 = 7
  d_prime : d.Prime
  rt : 𝓞 E
  rt_sq : rt ^ 2 = -(d : 𝓞 E)
  w0 : 𝓞 E
  two_weight_eq_one_add_root : 2 * w0 = 1 + rt
  bar : 𝓞 E ≃+* 𝓞 E
  bar_eq : ∀ a : 𝓞 E, ((bar a : 𝓞 E) : E) = IsCMField.complexConj E (a : E)
  unram : Algebra.Unramified (𝓞 (maximalRealSubfield E)) (𝓞 E)
  rt0 : ℂ
  sqrt_neg_disc_sq : rt0 ^ 2 = -(d : ℂ)

variable {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E]

structure Datum (H : Host E) where
  La : Type
  [acg : AddCommGroup La]
  [mod : Module (𝓞 E) La]
  fin : Module.Finite (𝓞 E) La
  proj : Module.Projective (𝓞 E) La
  EF : LinearMap.BilinForm ℤ La
  alt : ∀ x, EF x x = 0
  unimod : Function.Bijective EF
  adj : ∀ (a : 𝓞 E) x y, EF (a • x) y = EF x (H.bar a • y)
  p : ℕ
  q : ℕ
  rank : Module.finrank (𝓞 E) La = p + q
  dom : ∃ J : ℝ ⊗[ℤ] La →ₗ[ℝ] ℝ ⊗[ℤ] La, (∀ x, J (J x) = -x) ∧
    (∀ x y, EF.baseChange ℝ (J x) (J y) = EF.baseChange ℝ x y) ∧
    (∀ x, x ≠ 0 → 0 < EF.baseChange ℝ (J x) x) ∧
    ∀ (a : 𝓞 E) (A : ℝ ⊗[ℤ] La →ₗ[ℝ] ℝ ⊗[ℤ] La) (t : ℂ), (∀ x : La, A (1 ⊗ₜ x) = 1 ⊗ₜ (a • x)) →
      t = ∑ τ ∈ Finset.univ.filter (fun τ : E →+* ℂ => τ (H.rt : E) = H.rt0),
        ((p : ℂ) * τ (a : E) + (q : ℂ) * starRingEnd ℂ (τ (a : E))) →
      J ∘ₗ A = A ∘ₗ J ∧ LinearMap.trace ℝ _ A = 2 * t.re ∧ LinearMap.trace ℝ _ (J ∘ₗ A) = -2 * t.im


[HUF_SRC_LEVEL] Original odd M>=3, half-ideal product/coprimality, A>0 and N=lcm(M,A)
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/TwoWeightEqOneAddRoot.lean#L53-L66
structure Level (H : Host E) where
  M : ℕ
  three_le : 3 ≤ M
  odd : Odd M
  Mb : Ideal (𝓞 E)
  fromK : Mb = Ideal.span {a | a ∈ Mb ∧ a ∈ Algebra.adjoin ℤ {H.w0}}
  prod : Mb * Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) = Ideal.span {(M : 𝓞 E)}
  coprime : Mb ⊔ Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) = ⊤
  A : ℕ
  A_pos : 0 < A

def Level.M' {H : Host E} (l : Level H) : ℕ := Nat.lcm l.M l.A

def Level.Dvd {H : Host E} (l l₂ : Level H) : Prop := l.M ∣ l₂.M ∧ l₂.Mb ≤ l.Mb ∧ l.A ∣ l₂.A


[HUF_SRC_SAB] Actual universal group, rigidified/cubical/symmetric line, adjoint and primitive identities
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/TwoWeightEqOneAddRoot.lean#L72-L82
structure SAB (H : Host E) (S : Scheme.{0}) where
  G : Over S
  [grp : GrpObj G]
  [comm : IsCommMonObj G]
  semi : IsSemiAbelian G
  act : OAct (𝓞 E) G
  PP : RigLB G
  cub : CubicalStr G PP
  sym : PP.IsSymmetric
  adj : ∀ a, LBIso (cubeLB G ![fst G G ≫ act.act a, snd G G] PP.L) (cubeLB G ![fst G G, snd G G ≫ act.act (H.bar a)] PP.L)
  prim : LBIso PP.L (cubeLB G ![𝟙 G, act.act H.w0] PP.L)


[HUF_SRC_LIE] Original dual-number Lie eigenspaces and determinant multiplicities
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/TwoWeightEqOneAddRoot.lean#L105-L120
def epsScale (c : k) : DualNumber k →+* DualNumber k :=
  (TrivSqZeroExt.map (c • (LinearMap.id : k →ₗ[k] k))).toRingHom

def IsLieEigen {O : Type} [CommRing O] {G : Over S} [GrpObj G] (act : OAct O G) (t : Sp k ⟶ S) (τ : O →+* k)
    (v : epsPt t ⟶ G) : Prop :=
  spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom k k k).toRingHom ≫ v.left = (1 : Over.mk t ⟶ G).left ∧
    ∀ a, (v ≫ act.act a).left = spMap (epsScale (τ a)) ≫ v.left

def LieMult {O : Type} [CommRing O] {G : Over S} [GrpObj G] (act : OAct O G) (t : Sp k ⟶ S) (τ : O →+* k)
    (n : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ v : Fin n → (epsPt t ⟶ G), (∀ i, IsLieEigen act t τ (v i)) ∧ ∀ w, IsLieEigen act t τ w →
    ∃! c : Fin n → k, ∃ u : Fin n → (epsPt t ⟶ G),
      (∀ i, (u i).left = spMap (epsScale (c i)) ≫ (v i).left) ∧ w = (List.ofFn u).prod

def DetCond {H : Host E} (D : Datum H) {G : Over S} [GrpObj G] (act : OAct (𝓞 E) G) (t : Sp k ⟶ S) (ρ : k) : Prop :=
  ∀ τ : 𝓞 E →+* k, (τ H.rt = ρ → LieMult act t τ D.p) ∧ (τ H.rt ≠ ρ → LieMult act t τ D.q)


[HUF_SRC_TATE_OBJ] All original TateIso and Obj fields, geometric marking, fixed root at A
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/TwoWeightEqOneAddRoot.lean#L132-L168
    (e : ℕ → Q → Q → kˣ → Prop) where
  ζ : ℕ → kˣ
  ζ_prim : ∀ n, 0 < n → IsPrimitiveRoot (ζ n) n
  ζ_pow : ∀ n m, 0 < n → 0 < m → ζ (n * m) ^ m = ζ n
  ev : ℕ → D.La → Q
  add : ∀ n a b, ev n (a + b) = ev n a * ev n b
  lin : ∀ n (c : 𝓞 E) a, ev n (c • a) = act c (ev n a)
  ker : ∀ n, 0 < n → ∀ a, ev n a = 1 ↔ ∃ b, a = n • b
  onto : ∀ n, 0 < n → ∀ x, x ^ n = 1 ↔ ∃ a, ev n a = x
  tower : ∀ n m, 0 < n → 0 < m → ∀ a, ev (n * m) a ^ m = ev n a
  isom : ∀ n, 0 < n → ∀ a b, e n (ev n a) (ev n b) (ζ n ^ D.EF a b)

structure Obj {H : Host E} (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) {B : Type} [CommRing B] (b : Base l B) (s : S ⟶ Sp B) where
  t : SAB H S
  proper : IsProper t.G.hom
  ample : Out.RelAmple t.G t.PP.L
  ktriv : Out.KTriv t.G t.PP.L
  eta : D.La → (𝟙_ (Over S) ⟶ t.G)
  eta_add : ∀ a c, eta (a + c) = eta a * eta c
  eta_ker : ∀ a ∈ l.Mb • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), eta a = 1
  etaA : D.La → (𝟙_ (Over S) ⟶ t.G)
  etaA_add : ∀ a c, etaA (a + c) = etaA a * etaA c
  etaA_ker : ∀ a, etaA (l.A • a) = 1
  geom : ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [IsAlgClosed k] (x : Sp k ⟶ S) (β : B →+* k), x ≫ s = spMap β →
    DetCond D t.act x (β b.ρ) ∧
    ∃ I : TateIso D k (Over.mk x ⟶ t.G) (fun a g => g ≫ t.act.act a)
        (fun n g g' c => Out.WeilIs t.G t.PP.L n g g' (unitOn c)),
      (I.ζ l.A : k) = β b.ζ ∧ (∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ etaA a = I.ev l.A a) ∧
      ∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), toUnit _ ≫ eta a = I.ev l.M a

structure ObjMap {H : Host E} {D : Datum H} {l : Level H} {B B' : Type} [CommRing B] [CommRing B'] {b : Base l B}
    {b' : Base l B'} {S' : Scheme.{0}} {s' : S' ⟶ Sp B'} {s : S ⟶ Sp B} (o' : Obj D l b' s') (o : Obj D l b s)
    (f : S' ⟶ S) extends SABMap o'.t o.t f where
  eta : ∀ a, (o'.eta a).left ≫ cm.F = f ≫ (o.eta a).left
  etaA : ∀ a, (o'.etaA a).left ≫ cm.F = f ≫ (o.etaA a).left

end ReciprocityTheory.ChartRestriction.G.Geo.Tor


[HUF_SRC_MODULI] Original base field, dimension, supplied representing Moduli record and coefficient field
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/PushInvertible.lean#L12-L44
def zeta (n : ℕ) : ℂ := Complex.exp (2 * Real.pi * Complex.I / n)

def KA (l : Level H) : IntermediateField ℚ ℂ := IntermediateField.adjoin ℚ {H.rt0, zeta l.A}

def baseKA (l : Level H) : Base l (KA l) where
  unit n hn := IsUnit.mk0 _ (Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hn.ne')
  ρ := ⟨H.rt0, IntermediateField.subset_adjoin _ _ (by simp)⟩
  ρ_sq := Subtype.ext (by simpa using H.sqrt_neg_disc_sq)
  ζ := ⟨zeta l.A, IntermediateField.subset_adjoin _ _ (by simp)⟩
  ζ_prim := IsPrimitiveRoot.of_map_of_injective (f := (KA l).val.toRingHom)
    (Complex.isPrimitiveRoot_exp l.A l.A_pos.ne') Subtype.val_injective

def Datum.dim (D : Datum H) : ℕ := Module.finrank ℚ E / 2 * D.p * D.q

def constPt {k : Type} [Field k] {X : Scheme.{0}} (x : Sp (DualNumber k) ⟶ X) : Sp (DualNumber k) ⟶ X :=
  spMap (algebraMap k (DualNumber k)) ≫ spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom k k k).toRingHom ≫ x

structure Moduli (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) where
  X : Scheme.{0}
  s : X ⟶ Sp (KA l)
  qproj : IsQProjOver s
  smooth : SmoothOfRelativeDimension D.dim s
  univ : Obj D l (baseKA l) s
  rep : ∀ (S : Scheme.{0}) (s' : S ⟶ Sp (KA l)), IsReduced S → ∀ o : Obj D l (baseKA l) s',
    ∃! f : S ⟶ X, f ≫ s = s' ∧ Nonempty (ObjMap o univ f)
  unram : ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] (x : Sp (DualNumber k) ⟶ X) (o : Obj D l (baseKA l) (x ≫ s)),
    Nonempty (ObjMap o univ x) → Nonempty (ObjMap o univ (constPt x)) → x = constPt x

structure Coeff (l : Level H) where
  F : IntermediateField ℚ ℂ
  fin : FiniteDimensional ℚ F
  conj : ∀ (τ : E →+* ℂ) (a : E), τ a ∈ F
  zeta_mem : zeta l.M' ∈ F


[HUF_SRC_PERIOD] Exact ordered IsTypeFn, IsPeriod, IsHolTo and IsHolPeriods
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/IsHolPeriods.lean#L13-L28
def IsTypeFn (H : Host E) (p q : ℕ) {g : ℕ} (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)) : Prop :=
  ∀ τ : E →+* ℂ, (τ (H.rt : E) = H.rt0 → Nat.card {i // ty i = τ} = p) ∧ (τ (H.rt : E) ≠ H.rt0 → Nat.card {i // ty i = τ} = q)

def IsPeriod (D : Datum H) {g : ℕ} (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)) (P : D.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) : Prop :=
  (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) x i, P (a • x) i = ty i (a : E) * P x i) ∧ Function.Injective P ∧ DiscreteTopology P.range ∧
    Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range P) = ⊤ ∧
    ∃ Hm : Matrix (Fin g) (Fin g) ℂ, Hm.IsHermitian ∧ (∀ z : Fin g → ℂ, z ≠ 0 → 0 < (hermForm Hm z z).re) ∧
      ∀ x y, (hermForm Hm (P x) (P y)).im = D.EF x y

def IsHolTo {V : Type} [NormedAddCommGroup V] [NormedSpace ℂ V] {X : Scheme.{0}} (B : Set V) (y : V → (Sp ℂ ⟶ X)) : Prop :=
  ∀ (U : X.Opens) (f : Γ(X, U)), IsOpen {b ∈ B | ⊤ ≤ y b ⁻¹ᵁ U} ∧
    DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun b => valAt' (y b) U f) {b ∈ B | ⊤ ≤ y b ⁻¹ᵁ U}

def IsHolPeriods (D : Datum H) {g m : ℕ} (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ))
    (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → (D.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))) : Prop :=
  (∀ b ∈ B, IsPeriod D ty (P b)) ∧ ∀ x, DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun b => P b x) B


[HUF_SRC_DERIVATIVE] The original every-open/every-function dual-number derivative predicate
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/IsHolPeriods.lean#L30-L35
def IsDerivAt0 {g : ℕ} {X : Scheme.{0}} (e : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ X)) (w : Fin g → ℂ) (v : Sp (DualNumber ℂ) ⟶ X) : Prop :=
  spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v = e 0 ∧
    ∀ (U : X.Opens) (h : ⊤ ≤ v ⁻¹ᵁ U) (f : Γ(X, U)),
      TrivSqZeroExt.snd (show DualNumber ℂ from
        (Scheme.ΓSpecIso (CommRingCat.of (DualNumber ℂ))).hom.hom ((v.appLE U ⊤ h).hom f)) =
        fderiv ℂ (fun z => valAt' (e z) U f) 0 w


[HUF_SRC_THETA_FRAME] Every IsThetaFrame field, transition orientation, rigidification, and per-fiber algebraic section
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/IsHolPeriods.lean#L37-L49
def IsThetaFrame {g m : ℕ} {S : Scheme.{0}} {G : Over S} [GrpObj G] (L : RigLB G) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ))
    (y : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ S)) (e : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Fin g → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ G.left))
    (θ : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Fin g → ℂ) → ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∃ fr : L.L.ι → (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) → ℂ,
    (∀ j, DifferentiableOn ℂ (fr j) {bz | bz.1 ∈ B ∧ ⊤ ≤ e bz.1 bz.2 ⁻¹ᵁ L.L.U j}) ∧
    (∀ j b z, b ∈ B → ⊤ ≤ e b z ⁻¹ᵁ L.L.U j → fr j (b, z) ≠ 0) ∧
    (∀ j j' b z, b ∈ B → ⊤ ≤ e b z ⁻¹ᵁ (L.L.U j ⊓ L.L.U j') →
      fr j (b, z) = valAt' (e b z) (L.L.U j ⊓ L.L.U j') (L.L.g j j').val * fr j' (b, z)) ∧
    (∀ j b, b ∈ B → ⊤ ≤ e b 0 ⁻¹ᵁ L.L.U j →
      fr j (b, 0) = valAt' (y b) ((L.L.pull (eSec G)).U (fun _ => j)) (L.rig.s (fun _ => j)).val) ∧
    ∀ b ∈ B, ∃ s : (L.L.pull (pullback.fst G.hom (y b))).Sec, ∀ j z (x : Sp ℂ ⟶ pullback G.hom (y b)),
      x ≫ pullback.fst _ _ = e b z → x ≫ pullback.snd _ _ = 𝟙 _ → ⊤ ≤ e b z ⁻¹ᵁ L.L.U j →
      valAt' x ((L.L.pull (pullback.fst G.hom (y b))).U (fun _ => j)) (s.f (fun _ => j)) = θ b z * fr j (b, z)


[HUF_SRC_FAMILY] All literal FamilyUnif fields including total u/ex, finite disjoint family, holomorphy, theta and local lift
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/IsHolPeriods.lean#L51-L109
structure FamilyUnif {D : Datum H} {l : Level H} (Xo : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) {g : ℕ}
    (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)) where
  n : ℕ
  n_pos : 0 < n
  Di : Fin n → Datum H
  sig : ∀ i, (Di i).p = D.p ∧ (Di i).q = D.q

  t : ∀ i, D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] (Di i).La
  t_inj : ∀ i a, (∃ b, t i a = l.M' • b) ↔ ∃ b, a = l.M' • b
  t_surj : ∀ i b, ∃ a e, b = t i a + l.M' • e
  u : ∀ i, ((Di i).La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X)
  ex : ∀ i P, (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk (u i P) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G)
  over_σ : ∀ i P, IsPeriod (Di i) ty P → u i P ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ
  ex_add : ∀ i P, IsPeriod (Di i) ty P → ∀ z w, ex i P (z + w) = ex i P z * ex i P w
  ex_onto : ∀ i P, IsPeriod (Di i) ty P → Function.Surjective (ex i P)
  ex_ker : ∀ i P, IsPeriod (Di i) ty P → ∀ z, ex i P z = 1 ↔ z ∈ P.range
  ex_lin : ∀ i P, IsPeriod (Di i) ty P → ∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z,
    ex i P (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = ex i P z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a
  ex_weil : ∀ i P, IsPeriod (Di i) ty P → ∀ (k : ℕ) (a b : (Di i).La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k →
    (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * ((Di i).EF a b : ℂ) / k) →
    Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k (ex i P ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a)) (ex i P ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P b)) (unitOn c)
  ex_eta : ∀ i P, IsPeriod (Di i) ty P →
    ∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La),
      toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = ex i P ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t i a))
  ex_etaA : ∀ i P, IsPeriod (Di i) ty P → ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = ex i P ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t i a))
  onto : ∀ y : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X, y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ → ∃ i P, IsPeriod (Di i) ty P ∧ u i P = y
  disj : ∀ i j P P', IsPeriod (Di i) ty P → IsPeriod (Di j) ty P' → u i P = u j P' → i = j
  fibre : ∀ i P P', IsPeriod (Di i) ty P → IsPeriod (Di i) ty P' → (u i P = u i P' ↔
    ∃ (gam : (Di i).La ≃ₗ[𝓞 E] (Di i).La) (h : (Fin g → ℂ) ≃ₗ[ℂ] (Fin g → ℂ)),
      (∀ a b, (Di i).EF (gam a) (gam b) = (Di i).EF a b) ∧
      (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Di i).La), ∃ e, gam a = a + l.M • e) ∧
      (∀ a, ∃ e, gam a = a + l.A • e) ∧ ∀ a, P' (gam a) = h (P a))

  hol : ∀ i (m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → ((Di i).La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))), IsOpen B →
    IsHolPeriods (Di i) ty B P →
    IsHolTo B (fun b => u i (P b)) ∧
      IsHolTo (B ×ˢ Set.univ) (fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => (ex i (P bz.1) bz.2).left)

  ex_diff : ∀ i P, IsPeriod (Di i) ty P →
    (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt (u i P) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (ex i P z).left) w v.left) ∧
    ∀ v : epsPt (u i P) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G,
      spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = (ex i P 0).left →
      ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (ex i P z).left) w v.left

  theta : ∀ i (m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → ((Di i).La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)))
    (bas : (Fin g ⊕ Fin g → ℤ) ≃ₗ[ℤ] (Di i).La) (sgn : ℤˣ) (h τ : (Fin m → ℂ) → Matrix (Fin g) (Fin g) ℂ),
    IsOpen B → IsPreconnected B → IsHolPeriods (Di i) ty B P →
    (∀ a b, (Di i).EF (bas a) (bas b) = sgn * omegaStd a b) →
    (∀ b ∈ B, ((τ b).map Complex.im).PosDef ∧ ∀ k,
      (h b).mulVec (P b (bas (Pi.single (.inl k) 1))) = Pi.single k 1 ∧
      (h b).mulVec (P b (bas (Pi.single (.inr k) 1))) = (τ b).mulVec (Pi.single k 1)) →
    ∃ c₁ c₂ : Fin g → ℂ, (∀ k, c₁ k = 0 ∨ c₁ k = 1 / 2) ∧ (∀ k, c₂ k = 0 ∨ c₂ k = 1 / 2) ∧
      IsThetaFrame Xo.univ.t.PP B (fun b => u i (P b)) (fun b z => (ex i (P b) z).left)
        (fun b z => thetaChar (τ b) c₁ c₂ ((h b).mulVec z))

  lift : ∀ (m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (y : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X)), IsOpen B →
    (∀ b ∈ B, y b ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ) → IsHolTo B y → ∀ b₀ ∈ B,
    ∃ (B' : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (i : Fin n) (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → ((Di i).La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))),
      IsOpen B' ∧ b₀ ∈ B' ∧ B' ⊆ B ∧ IsHolPeriods (Di i) ty B' P ∧ ∀ b ∈ B', u i (P b) = y b


[HUF_SRC_N1C] Whole original N1c quantifiers and conclusion

def N1c : Prop :=
  ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (Xo : Moduli D l)
    (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) (g : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)), 1 ≤ D.p + D.q → σ (baseKA l).ρ = H.rt0 →
    IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → Nonempty (FamilyUnif Xo σ ty)

[HUF_SRC_RELAMPLE_KTRIV] Original relative-ampleness presentation and every-coefficient-ring KTriv
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/ExtensionDescent/NonzeroLocus.lean#L28-L60
  LB.LinComb L (K := Fin N) (fun _ => (A.hom ⁻¹ᵁ U).ι) (fun _ => 1)

def nonzeroLocus {Y : Scheme.{0}} {M : LB Y} (s : LB.Sec M) : Y.Opens :=
  ⨆ k, Y.basicOpen (s.f k)

def RelAmple (L : LB A.left) : Prop :=
  ∀ U : S.Opens, IsAffineOpen U →
    ∃ N : ℕ, 1 ≤ N ∧ ∃ M : ℕ, ∃ s : Fin (M + 1) → LB.Sec (powOver A L U N),
      (⨆ i, nonzeroLocus (s i) = ⊤) ∧
      ∀ i, IsAffineOpen (nonzeroLocus (s i)) ∧
        ∃ r : Fin (M + 1) → Γ((A.hom ⁻¹ᵁ U).toScheme, nonzeroLocus (s i)),
          (∀ j k, ((A.hom ⁻¹ᵁ U).toScheme.presheaf.map (homOfLE (inf_le_right :
              nonzeroLocus (s i) ⊓ (powOver A L U N).U k ≤ (powOver A L U N).U k)).op).hom
                ((s j).f k) =
            ((A.hom ⁻¹ᵁ U).toScheme.presheaf.map (homOfLE (inf_le_left :
              nonzeroLocus (s i) ⊓ (powOver A L U N).U k ≤ nonzeroLocus (s i))).op).hom (r j) *
            ((A.hom ⁻¹ᵁ U).toScheme.presheaf.map (homOfLE (inf_le_right :
              nonzeroLocus (s i) ⊓ (powOver A L U N).U k ≤ (powOver A L U N).U k)).op).hom
                ((s i).f k)) ∧
          Function.Surjective (MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom
            ((A.hom ∣_ U).appLE ⊤ (nonzeroLocus (s i)) le_top).hom r)

def translDiff (L : LB A.left) {T : Over S} (x : T ⟶ A) : LB (T ⊗ A).left :=
  LB.LinComb L (K := Fin 2) ![(lift (fst T A ≫ x) (snd T A) ≫ μ[A]).left, (snd T A).left] ![1, -1]

def KTriv (L : LB A.left) : Prop :=
  ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] (t : Spec (CommRingCat.of R) ⟶ S) (x : Over.mk t ⟶ A),
    (∀ p : Spec (CommRingCat.of R), ∃ V : (Spec (CommRingCat.of R)).Opens, p ∈ V ∧
      ∃ c : LB.Gen ((translDiff A L x).pull ((fst (Over.mk t) A).left ⁻¹ᵁ V).ι), c.IsGen) →
    x = toUnit _ ≫ η[A]

structure PolAV (S : Scheme.{0}) where



[HUF_SRC_WEIL] Actual theta-line quotient and universally tested commutator/Weil relation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/ExtensionDescent/ConstGen.lean#L17-L58

def translPt {T : Over S} (x : T ⟶ A) : T ⊗ A ⟶ A := lift (fst T A ≫ x) (snd T A) ≫ μ[A]

def translT {T : Over S} (x : T ⟶ A) : T ⊗ A ⟶ T ⊗ A := lift (fst T A) (translPt A x)

def thetaLB (L : LB A.left) (k : ℤ) {T : Over S} (x : T ⟶ A) : LB (T ⊗ A).left :=
  LB.LinComb L (K := Fin 2) ![(snd T A).left, (translPt A x).left] ![k, -k]

structure ThetaGrp (L : LB A.left) (k : ℤ) (T : Over S) where

  pt : T ⟶ A

  φ : LB.Gen (thetaLB A L k pt)

  isGen : φ.IsGen

def constGen (L : LB A.left) {T : Over S} (c : Γ(T.left, ⊤)ˣ) :
    LB.Gen (LB.LinComb L (Y := (T ⊗ A).left) (K := PEmpty) PEmpty.elim PEmpty.elim) where
  s := fun _ => res le_top (Units.map (fst T A).left.appTop.hom.toMonoidHom c)

def CommIsOf (L : LB A.left) (k : ℤ) {T : Over S} (θx θy : ThetaGrp A L k T)
    (c : Γ(T.left, ⊤)ˣ) : Prop :=
  LB.Gen.Corr L _ _ _ _
    (LB.mulGen L _ _ _ _ θx.φ (LB.pullGen L _ _ θy.φ (translT A θx.pt).left))
    (LB.mulGen L _ _ _ _
      (LB.mulGen L _ _ _ _ θy.φ (LB.pullGen L _ _ θx.φ (translT A θy.pt).left))
      (constGen A L c))

abbrev overRes {T : Over S} (V : T.left.Opens) : Over S := Over.mk (V.ι ≫ T.hom)

def overResι {T : Over S} (V : T.left.Opens) : overRes V ⟶ T := Over.homMk V.ι rfl

def unitRes {T : Over S} (V : T.left.Opens) (c : Γ(T.left, ⊤)ˣ) : Γ((overRes V).left, ⊤)ˣ :=
  Units.map V.ι.appTop.hom.toMonoidHom c

def WeilIs (L : LB A.left) (k : ℤ) {T : Over S} (x y : T ⟶ A) (c : Γ(T.left, ⊤)ˣ) : Prop :=
  ∀ p : T.left, ∃ V : T.left.Opens, p ∈ V ∧
    (∃ θx θy : ThetaGrp A L k (overRes V), θx.pt = overResι V ≫ x ∧ θy.pt = overResι V ≫ y) ∧
    ∀ θx θy : ThetaGrp A L k (overRes V), θx.pt = overResι V ≫ x → θy.pt = overResι V ≫ y →
      CommIsOf A L k θx θy (unitRes V c)

end ReciprocityTheory.ChartRestriction.G.Out


[HUF_SRC_INGENUS_UNIF] Exact InGenus and pointwise Unif predicates
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/FamilyUnifOfParts.lean#L18-L40
def InGenus (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) (D' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) : Prop :=
  D'.p = D.p ∧ D'.q = D.q ∧ ∃ (f : ℕ → D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (ν : ℕ → ℤ),
    (∀ n m : ℕ, 0 < n → 0 < m → ∀ a, ∃ e, f (n * m) a = f n a + n • e) ∧
    (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → (∀ a, (∃ e, f n a = n • e) ↔ ∃ e, a = n • e) ∧ ∀ a', ∃ a e, a' = f n a + n • e) ∧
    (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∀ a a', (n : ℤ) ∣ D'.EF (f n a) (f n a') - ν n * D.EF a a') ∧
    σ (baseKA l).ζ = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (ν l.A : ℂ) / l.A) ∧ t = f l.M'

def Unif {D : Datum H} {l : Level H} (Xo : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) {g : ℕ} (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ))
    (D' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (P : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) (y : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X)
    (e : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G)) : Prop :=
  y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ IsPeriod D' ty P ∧ ((∀ z w, e (z + w) = e z * e w) ∧ Function.Surjective e ∧
    IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => (e z).left) ∧
    (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e z).left) w v.left) ∧
    (∀ v : epsPt y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = (e 0).left →
      ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e z).left) w v.left)) ∧
    (∀ z, e z = 1 ↔ z ∈ (P).range) ∧
    (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, e (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = e z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a) ∧
    (∀ (k : ℕ) (a a₂ : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k →
      (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a a₂ : ℂ) / k) →
      Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k (e ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a)) (e ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a₂)) (unitOn c)) ∧
    (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La),
      toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = e ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t a))) ∧
    ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = e ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t a))


[HUF_SRC_CLASS_TRANSPORT] Period transport, marking congruence transfer, classes, and equality
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/FamilyUnifOfParts.lean#L42-L163
theorem isPeriod_transport {D' D'' : Datum H} {g : ℕ} {ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)} (gam : D'.La ≃ₗ[𝓞 E] D''.La)
    (hgam : ∀ a b, D''.EF (gam a) (gam b) = D'.EF a b) {P : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)} (hP : IsPeriod D' ty P) :
    IsPeriod D'' ty (P.comp gam.symm.toLinearMap.toAddMonoidHom) := by
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4, Hm, h5, h6, h7⟩ := hP
  have hr : (P.comp gam.symm.toLinearMap.toAddMonoidHom).range = P.range := by
    ext z
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨a, rfl⟩; exact ⟨gam.symm a, rfl⟩
    · rintro ⟨a, rfl⟩; exact ⟨gam a, by simp⟩
  refine ⟨fun a x i => ?_, h2.comp gam.symm.injective, by rw [hr]; exact h3, ?_, Hm, h5, h6, fun x y => ?_⟩
  · simpa using h1 a (gam.symm x) i
  · rw [← h4, ← AddMonoidHom.coe_range, hr, AddMonoidHom.coe_range]
  · have := hgam (gam.symm x) (gam.symm y)
    simp only [LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply] at this
    rw [this, ← h7]; rfl

theorem level_pos (l : Level H) : 0 < l.M' :=
  Nat.lcm_pos (by have := l.three_le; omega) l.A_pos

theorem exists_inGenus (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ)
    (hs : ∀ (l : Level H) (N : ℕ) (ν : ℤ), 0 < N → IsCoprime ν (N : ℤ) → (l.A : ℤ) ∣ ν - 1 →
      ∃ g : Sim D, (N : ℤ) ∣ g.ν N - ν) : ∃ t, InGenus D l σ D t := by
  have hprim : IsPrimitiveRoot (σ (baseKA l).ζ) l.A := (baseKA l).ζ_prim.map_of_injective σ.injective
  obtain ⟨i, -, hi, hζ⟩ := (Complex.isPrimitiveRoot_iff _ _ l.A_pos.ne').mp hprim
  obtain ⟨s, c, hc⟩ := hs { l with A := 1, A_pos := one_pos } l.A (-(i : ℤ)) l.A_pos
    (Nat.isCoprime_iff_coprime.mpr hi).neg_left (by simp)
  refine ⟨s.f l.M', rfl, rfl, s.f, s.ν, fun n m hn hm a => ?_, fun n hn => ?_, s.mult, ?_, rfl⟩
  · obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := s.tower n m hn hm a
    exact ⟨b, by rw [← hb]; abel⟩
  · obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := s.inv n hn
    refine ⟨fun a => ⟨?_, ?_⟩, fun a' => ?_⟩
    · rintro ⟨e, he⟩
      obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := (hg a).1
      exact ⟨g e - b, by rw [smul_sub, ← hb, he, map_nsmul]; abel⟩
    · rintro ⟨e, rfl⟩
      exact ⟨s.f n e, map_nsmul _ _ _⟩
    · obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := (hg a').2
      exact ⟨g a', -b, by rw [smul_neg, ← hb]; abel⟩
  · have hA : (l.A : ℂ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr l.A_pos.ne'
    have h1 : (s.ν l.A : ℂ) = -(i : ℂ) + l.A * c := by
      have : s.ν l.A = -(i : ℤ) + l.A * c := by linarith
      rw [this]; push_cast; ring
    rw [← hζ, h1, Complex.exp_eq_exp_iff_exists_int]
    exact ⟨c, by field_simp; ring⟩

theorem triv_iff {D D' : Datum H} (l : Level H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La)
    (ht : ∀ b, ∃ a e, b = t a + l.M' • e) (gam : D'.La ≃ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (I : Ideal (𝓞 E)) (n : ℕ) (hn : n ∣ l.M') :
    (∀ a ∈ I • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), ∃ x, gam (t a) = t a + n • x) ↔
      ∀ a ∈ I • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D'.La), ∃ x, gam a = a + n • x := by
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := hn
  constructor
  · intro h b hb
    refine Submodule.smul_induction_on hb (fun c hc b' _ => ?_) ?_
    · obtain ⟨a, e, rfl⟩ := ht b'
      obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := h (c • a) (Submodule.smul_mem_smul hc trivial)
      refine ⟨x + k • (gam (c • e) - c • e), ?_⟩
      rw [smul_add, ← t.map_smul, map_add, hx, smul_comm c, map_nsmul, smul_add, ← mul_smul, hk, smul_sub]
      abel
    · rintro x y ⟨u, hu⟩ ⟨v, hv⟩
      exact ⟨u + v, by rw [map_add, hu, hv, smul_add]; abel⟩
  · intro h a ha
    refine h _ (Submodule.smul_induction_on ha (fun c hc a' _ => ?_) fun x y hx hy => ?_)
    · rw [t.map_smul]; exact Submodule.smul_mem_smul hc trivial
    · rw [map_add]; exact add_mem hx hy

theorem triv_iff_top {D D' : Datum H} (l : Level H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La)
    (ht : ∀ b, ∃ a e, b = t a + l.M' • e) (gam : D'.La ≃ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (n : ℕ) (hn : n ∣ l.M') :
    (∀ a, ∃ x, gam (t a) = t a + n • x) ↔ ∀ a, ∃ x, gam a = a + n • x := by
  simpa using triv_iff l t ht gam ⊤ n hn

theorem quasiCompact_str {D : Datum H} {l : Level H} (Xo : Moduli D l) : QuasiCompact Xo.s := by
  obtain ⟨N, i, -, hi, h⟩ := Xo.qproj
  have := isProper_str (KA l) N
  rw [← h]
  infer_instance

def cls {D : Datum H} {l : Level H} (Xo : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) {g : ℕ} (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ))
    (y : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X) : Set (Σ D' : Datum H, D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) :=
  {x | InGenus D l σ x.1 x.2 ∧ ∃ P e, Unif Xo σ ty x.1 x.2 P y e}

section Fixed

variable {D : Datum H} {l : Level H} {Xo : Moduli D l} {σ : KA l →+* ℂ} {g : ℕ} {ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)}
  (perObj : ∀ (D' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (P : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)), InGenus D l σ D' t →
    IsPeriod D' ty P → ∃ y e, Unif Xo σ ty D' t P y e)
  (upUniq : ∀ (D' D'' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (t' : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D''.La) (P : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))
    (P' : D''.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) (y y' : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X) e e', Unif Xo σ ty D' t P y e → Unif Xo σ ty D'' t' P' y' e' →
    (y = y' ↔ ∃ (gam : D'.La ≃ₗ[𝓞 E] D''.La) (h : (Fin g → ℂ) ≃ₗ[ℂ] (Fin g → ℂ)),
      (∀ a b, D''.EF (gam a) (gam b) = D'.EF a b) ∧
      (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), ∃ x, gam (t a) = t' a + l.M • x) ∧
      (∀ a, ∃ x, gam (t a) = t' a + l.A • x) ∧ ∀ a, P' (gam a) = h (P a)))

include upUniq in

theorem point_eq {D' : Datum H} {t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La} {P : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)} {y y' : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X} {e e'}
    (h : Unif Xo σ ty D' t P y e) (h' : Unif Xo σ ty D' t P y' e') : y = y' :=
  (upUniq _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ h h').mpr ⟨LinearEquiv.refl _ _, LinearEquiv.refl _ _, fun _ _ => rfl,
    fun _ _ => ⟨0, by simp⟩, fun _ => ⟨0, by simp⟩, fun _ => rfl⟩

include perObj upUniq in
theorem cls_subset {y y' : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X} {x} (hx : x ∈ cls Xo σ ty y) (hx' : x ∈ cls Xo σ ty y') :
    cls Xo σ ty y ⊆ cls Xo σ ty y' := by
  rintro x' ⟨hg', P', e', hu'⟩
  obtain ⟨-, P, e, hu⟩ := hx
  obtain ⟨-, P₂, e₂, hu₂⟩ := hx'
  obtain ⟨gam, h, h1, h2, h3, -⟩ := (upUniq _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ hu hu').mp rfl
  obtain ⟨y'', e'', hu''⟩ := perObj _ _ _ hg' (isPeriod_transport gam h1 hu₂.2.1)
  have : y' = y'' := (upUniq _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ hu₂ hu'').mpr
    ⟨gam, LinearEquiv.refl _ _, h1, h2, h3, fun a => by simp⟩
  subst this
  exact ⟨hg', _, _, hu''⟩

include perObj upUniq in

theorem cls_eq {y y' : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X} {x} (hx : x ∈ cls Xo σ ty y) (hx' : x ∈ cls Xo σ ty y') :
    cls Xo σ ty y = cls Xo σ ty y' :=
  (cls_subset perObj upUniq hx hx').antisymm (cls_subset perObj upUniq hx' hx)

end Fixed

end GlobalTheory.UnifParts



[HUF_SRC_FAMILY_ASSEMBLY] Original familyUnif_of_parts and its complete finite-family installation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/FamilyUnifOfParts.lean#L168-L422
theorem familyUnif_of_parts : (∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D
    : Datum H) (g : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+*
    ℂ)), IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → ∃ P, IsPeriod D ty P) → (∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField
    E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (Xo : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) (g
    : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)), 1 ≤ D.p + D.q → σ (baseKA
    l).ρ = H.rt0 → IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → ∀ y : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X, y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ → ∃ (D'
    : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (P : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) (e : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk y ⟶
    Xo.univ.t.G)), (D'.p = D.p ∧ D'.q = D.q ∧ ∃ (f : ℕ → D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (ν

    b z).left) (fun b z => thetaChar (τ b) c₁ c₂ ((h b).mulVec z))) → (∀ (E : Type) [Field
    E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (N : ℕ) (ν
    : ℤ), 0 < N → IsCoprime ν (N : ℤ) → (l.A : ℤ) ∣ ν - 1 → ∃ g : Sim D, (N : ℤ) ∣ g.ν N - ν ∧ (l.A
    : ℤ) ∣ g.ν l.A - 1 ∧ (∀ a, g.f l.M a - a ∈ l.Mb • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞
    E) D.La)) ∧ ∀ a, D.ModN l.A (g.f l.A a - a)) → N1c := by
  intro perExist ptUnif perObj upUniq locLift holU compFin thetaFam simMult E _ _ _ H D l Xo σ g ty h1 h2 h3
  replace ptUnif : ∀ y : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X, y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ → ∃ x, x ∈ cls Xo σ ty y := fun y hy => by
    obtain ⟨D', t, P, e, hg, hu⟩ := ptUnif E H D l Xo σ g ty h1 h2 h3 y hy
    exact ⟨⟨D', t⟩, hg, P, e, hu⟩
  replace perObj : ∀ (D' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (P : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)), InGenus D l σ D' t →
      IsPeriod D' ty P → ∃ y e, Unif Xo σ ty D' t P y e := perObj E H D l Xo σ g ty h1 h2 h3
  replace upUniq := upUniq E H D l Xo σ g ty h1 h2 h3
  replace locLift := locLift E H D l Xo σ g ty h1 h2 h3
  replace holU := holU E H D l Xo σ g ty h1 h2 h3
  replace thetaFam := thetaFam E H D l Xo σ g ty h1 h2 h3
  have hfin : (cls Xo σ ty '' {x | x ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ}).Finite := by
    refine compFin (KA l) σ Xo.X Xo.s D.dim _ (cls Xo σ ty) Xo.smooth (quasiCompact_str Xo)
      fun B y hB hy hh b₀ hb₀ => ?_
    obtain ⟨x, hg, P₀, e₀, hu⟩ := ptUnif _ (hy b₀ hb₀)
    obtain ⟨B', P, e, hB', hb', -, -, -, -, hub⟩ := locLift x.1 x.2 _ B y b₀ P₀ e₀ hB hb₀ hy hh hu
    filter_upwards [hB'.mem_nhds hb'] with b hb
    exact cls_eq perObj upUniq (x := x) ⟨hg, _, _, hub b hb⟩ ⟨hg, _, _, hu⟩
  have hne : (cls Xo σ ty '' {x | x ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ}).Nonempty := by
    obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := exists_inGenus D l σ fun l' N ν a b c =>
      (simMult E H D l' N ν a b c).imp fun _ h => h.1
    obtain ⟨P, hP⟩ := perExist E H D g ty h3
    obtain ⟨y, e, hu⟩ := perObj D t P ht hP
    exact ⟨_, y, hu.1, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨n, ⟨φ⟩⟩ := Finite.exists_equiv_fin _ (h := hfin.to_subtype)
  have hn : 0 < n := Fin.pos (φ ⟨_, hne.some_mem⟩)
  have hY : ∀ i, ∃ y : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X, y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ cls Xo σ ty y = φ.symm i := fun i => (φ.symm i).2
  choose Y hYσ hY using hY
  choose x hx using fun i => ptUnif _ (hYσ i)
  have hue : ∀ i (P : (x i).1.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)), ∃ (y : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X)
      (e : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G)),
      IsPeriod (x i).1 ty P → Unif Xo σ ty (x i).1 (x i).2 P y e := fun i P => by
    by_cases hP : IsPeriod (x i).1 ty P
    · obtain ⟨y, e, h⟩ := perObj _ _ P (hx i).1 hP
      exact ⟨y, e, fun _ => h⟩
    · exact ⟨Y i, fun _ => 1, fun h => absurd h hP⟩
  choose u ex hu using hue
  have hcls : ∀ y : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X, y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ → ∃ i, x i ∈ cls Xo σ ty y := fun y hy => by
    refine ⟨φ ⟨_, y, hy, rfl⟩, ?_⟩
    have := hx (φ ⟨_, y, hy, rfl⟩)
    rwa [hY, Equiv.symm_apply_apply] at this
  have hts : ∀ i, (∀ a, (∃ b, (x i).2 a = l.M' • b) ↔ ∃ b, a = l.M' • b) ∧
      ∀ b, ∃ a e, b = (x i).2 a + l.M' • e := fun i => by
    obtain ⟨-, -, f, ν, -, hf, -, -, ht⟩ := (hx i).1
    rw [ht]
    exact hf _ (level_pos l)
  have hhol : ∀ i (m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → ((x i).1.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))), IsOpen B →
      IsHolPeriods (x i).1 ty B P → IsHolTo B (fun b => u i (P b)) ∧
      IsHolTo (B ×ˢ Set.univ) (fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => (ex i (P bz.1) bz.2).left) :=
    fun i m B P hB hP => holU _ _ m B P (fun b => u i (P b)) (fun b => ex i (P b)) (hx i).1 hB hP
      fun b hb => hu i _ (hP.1 b hb)
  refine ⟨{ n := n
            n_pos := hn
            Di := fun i => (x i).1
            sig := fun i => ⟨(hx i).1.1, (hx i).1.2.1⟩
            t := fun i => (x i).2
            t_inj := fun i => (hts i).1
            t_surj := fun i => (hts i).2
            u := u
            ex := ex
            over_σ := fun i P hP => (hu i P hP).1
            ex_add := fun i P hP => (hu i P hP).2.2.1.1
            ex_onto := fun i P hP => (hu i P hP).2.2.1.2.1
            ex_ker := fun i P hP => (hu i P hP).2.2.2.1
            ex_lin := fun i P hP => (hu i P hP).2.2.2.2.1
            ex_weil := fun i P hP => (hu i P hP).2.2.2.2.2.1
            ex_eta := fun i P hP => (hu i P hP).2.2.2.2.2.2.1
            ex_etaA := fun i P hP => (hu i P hP).2.2.2.2.2.2.2
            onto := fun y hy => ?_
            disj := fun i j P P' hP hP' h => ?_
            fibre := fun i P P' hP hP' => ?_
            hol := hhol
            ex_diff := fun i P hP => (hu i P hP).2.2.1.2.2.2
            theta := fun i m B P bas sgn h τ hB hc hP hb hτ =>
              thetaFam _ _ m B P (fun b => u i (P b)) (fun b => ex i (P b)) bas sgn h τ
                (fun b hb => hu i _ (hP.1 b hb)) hb hτ hB hc hP (hhol i m B P hB hP).1 (hhol i m B P hB hP).2
            lift := fun m B y hB hy hh b₀ hb₀ => ?_ }⟩
  · obtain ⟨i, -, P, e, h⟩ := hcls y hy
    exact ⟨i, P, h.2.1, point_eq upUniq (hu i P h.2.1) h⟩
  · have hi : cls Xo σ ty (Y i) = cls Xo σ ty (u i P) :=
      cls_eq perObj upUniq (hx i) ⟨(hx i).1, _, _, hu i P hP⟩
    have hj : cls Xo σ ty (Y j) = cls Xo σ ty (u j P') :=
      cls_eq perObj upUniq (hx j) ⟨(hx j).1, _, _, hu j P' hP'⟩
    apply φ.symm.injective
    apply Subtype.ext
    rw [← hY, ← hY, hi, hj, h]
  · rw [upUniq _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ (hu i P hP) (hu i P' hP')]
    refine exists_congr fun gam => exists_congr fun h => ?_
    rw [triv_iff l _ (hts i).2 gam _ l.M (Nat.dvd_lcm_left _ _),
      triv_iff_top l _ (hts i).2 gam l.A (Nat.dvd_lcm_right _ _)]
  · obtain ⟨i, -, P₀, e₀, h⟩ := hcls _ (hy b₀ hb₀)
    obtain ⟨B', P, e, hB', hb', hsub, -, hP, -, hub⟩ := locLift _ _ m B y b₀ P₀ e₀ hB hb₀ hy hh h
    exact ⟨B', i, P, hB', hb', hsub, hP, fun b hb => point_eq upUniq (hu i _ (hP.1 b hb)) (hub b hb)⟩

end GlobalTheory


[HUF_SRC_OBJ_DIMENSION] Actual abelian-scheme, dimension, rigidification and symmetry supplier
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/ObjIsAbelianSchemeDimRigidifiedSym.lean#L207-L299
theorem dim_eq_of_detCond {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] {H : Host E} (D : Datum H)
    {S : Scheme.{0}} {G : Over S} [GrpObj G] (act : OAct (𝓞 E) G) {k : Type} [Field k] [IsAlgClosed k] [CharZero k]
    (x : Sp k ⟶ S) {r : k} (hr : r ^ 2 = -(H.d : k)) (hD : DetCond D act x r) {n : ℕ}
    (hn : SmoothOfRelativeDimension n (pullback.snd G.hom x)) : n = Module.finrank ℚ E / 2 * (D.p + D.q) := by
  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := univChart_of_relativeDimension ((Over.pullback x).obj G) hn
  have : ∀ a, IsMonHom (act.act a) := act.hom
  let ρ := tangentAction hc (fun a => (Over.pullback x).map (act.act a)) (by rw [act.one, CategoryTheory.Functor.map_id])
    (fun a b => by rw [act.mul, Functor.map_comp]) (fun a b => by rw [act.add, Functor.map_mul])
  have key : ∀ (τ : 𝓞 E →+* k) (N : ℕ), LieMult act x τ N → Module.finrank k (EigenSplit.eigenSub ρ τ) = N := by
    intro τ N hN
    have e : EigenSplit.eigenSub ρ τ =
        Chart.eigLat hc (fun a => (Over.pullback x).map (act.act a)) (fun a => τ a) := by
      ext v
      rw [EigenSplit.mem_eigenSub, Chart.mem_eigLat_iff]
      rfl
    rw [e]
    have : IsMonHom (Iso.refl G).hom := inferInstanceAs (IsMonHom (𝟙 G))
    exact Chart.finrank_of_lieMult (isGrpPullback_of_iso (Iso.refl G)) (Category.comp_id x) hc act act.act
      (fun a => by simp) _ (fun a => rfl) τ hN
  have := finrank_eq_of_eigenSub H hr ρ fun τ => ⟨fun h => key τ _ ((hD τ).1 h), fun h => key τ _ ((hD τ).2 h)⟩
  rwa [Module.finrank_fin_fun] at this

variable {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] {H : Host E} {D : Datum H} {l : Level H} {B : Type}
  [CommRing B] {b : Base l B} {S : Scheme.{0}} {s : S ⟶ Sp B}

theorem isAbelianScheme (o : Obj D l b s) : IsAbelianScheme o.t.G :=
  ⟨o.t.semi.comm, o.t.semi.smooth, o.proper, o.t.semi.geomConnected⟩

theorem rigidified {t : SAB H S} : Out.Rigidified t.G t.PP.L := ⟨t.PP.rig, t.PP.rigGen⟩

theorem sym {t : SAB H S} : Out.Sym t.G t.PP.L := by
  obtain ⟨-, w, hw, -⟩ := t.sym
  exact fun _ => ⟨⊤, trivial, gen_of_iso ((isGen_iff w).1 hw) _⟩

theorem smoothOfRelativeDimension (o : Obj D l b s) :
    SmoothOfRelativeDimension (Module.finrank ℚ E / 2 * (D.p + D.q)) o.t.G.hom := by
  have := o.t.semi.smooth
  have := o.t.semi.geomConnected
  choose n W hW hd using H1.exists_dim o.t.G.hom
  have : IsZariskiLocalAtSource (@SmoothOfRelativeDimension (Module.finrank ℚ E / 2 * (D.p + D.q))) :=
    HasRingHomProperty.instIsZariskiLocalAtSource
  refine IsZariskiLocalAtSource.of_iSup_eq_top
    (P := @SmoothOfRelativeDimension (Module.finrank ℚ E / 2 * (D.p + D.q))) W
    (top_le_iff.1 fun y _ => TopologicalSpace.Opens.mem_iSup.2 ⟨y, hW y⟩) fun y => ?_
  let k := AlgebraicClosure (o.t.G.left.residueField y)
  let z : Sp k ⟶ o.t.G.left :=
    Spec.map (CommRingCat.ofHom (algebraMap (o.t.G.left.residueField y) k)) ≫ o.t.G.left.fromSpecResidueField y
  have hz : ∀ u, z u = y := fun u => Scheme.fromSpecResidueField_apply y _
  let x : Sp k ⟶ S := z ≫ o.t.G.hom
  let β : B →+* k := (Spec.preimage (x ≫ s)).hom
  have hβ : x ≫ s = spMap β := (Spec.map_preimage (x ≫ s)).symm
  have : CharZero k := charZero_of_inj_zero fun m hm => by
    by_contra h0
    have := (b.unit m (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero h0)).map β
    rw [map_natCast, hm] at this
    exact not_isUnit_zero this
  have hr : β b.ρ ^ 2 = -(H.d : k) := by rw [← map_pow, b.ρ_sq, map_neg, map_natCast]
  have : ConnectedSpace ↥(pullback o.t.G.hom x) :=
    GeometricallyConnected.geometrically_connectedSpace (f := o.t.G.hom) x _ _ (IsPullback.of_hasPullback _ _)
  let z' : Sp k ⟶ pullback o.t.G.hom x := pullback.lift z (𝟙 _) (Category.id_comp _).symm
  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := exists_dim_of_preconnected (pullback.snd o.t.G.hom x) (z' (default : PrimeSpectrum k))
  have e₁ : n y = m := H1.dim_base (IsPullback.of_hasPullback _ _) (hd y) hm (z' (default : PrimeSpectrum k)) (by
    have e : z' ≫ pullback.fst o.t.G.hom x = z := pullback.lift_fst _ _ _
    have e' := (congrArg (fun q : Sp k ⟶ o.t.G.left => q (default : PrimeSpectrum k)) e).trans (hz _)
    exact (congrArg (fun u => u ∈ W y) e').mpr (hW y))
  rw [← dim_eq_of_detCond D o.t.act x hr (o.geom k x β hβ).1 hm, ← e₁]
  exact hd y

end GlobalTheory.ObjDim

namespace GlobalTheory

open ReciprocityTheory.ChartRestriction.G ReciprocityTheory.ChartRestriction.G.Semi ReciprocityTheory.ChartRestriction.G.Geo.Tor

theorem obj_isAbelianScheme_dim_rigidified_sym :
    ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (B : Type) [CommRing B] (b : Base l B) (S : Scheme.{0}) (s : S ⟶ Sp B) (o : Obj D l b s), IsAbelianScheme o.t.G ∧ SmoothOfRelativeDimension (Module.finrank ℚ E / 2 * (D.p + D.q)) o.t.G.hom ∧ Out.Rigidified o.t.G o.t.PP.L ∧ Out.Sym o.t.G o.t.PP.L :=
  fun _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ o =>
    ⟨ObjDim.isAbelianScheme o, ObjDim.smoothOfRelativeDimension o, ObjDim.rigidified, ObjDim.sym⟩

def Obj.toPolAV {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] {H : Host E} {D : Datum H} {l : Level H}
    {B : Type} [CommRing B] {b : Base l B} {S : Scheme.{0}} {s : S ⟶ Sp B} (o : Obj D l b s) : Out.PolAV S where
  A := o.t.G
  isAb := ObjDim.isAbelianScheme o
  g := Module.finrank ℚ E / 2 * (D.p + D.q)
  smoothDim := ObjDim.smoothOfRelativeDimension o
  L := o.t.PP.L
  isLB := o.t.PP.isLB
  rigid := ObjDim.rigidified
  sym := ObjDim.sym
  ample := o.ample
  ktriv := o.ktriv

end GlobalTheory


[HUF_SRC_SMOOTH_CHARTS] Holomorphic chart for a smooth scheme at a prescribed complex base embedding
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/ExistsHolomorphicChart.lean#L71-L172
theorem exists_chart_algebra (d : ℕ) [Algebra.IsStandardSmoothOfRelativeDimension d k A]
    (e : A →ₐ[k] ℂ) :
    ∃ (W : Set (Fin d → ℂ)) (p : (Fin d → ℂ) → (A →ₐ[k] ℂ)) (n : ℕ) (r : Fin n → A) (ε : ℝ),
      IsOpen W ∧ 0 ∈ W ∧ p 0 = e ∧ 0 < ε ∧ (∀ a, DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun w => p w a) W) ∧
      Set.InjOn p W ∧ ∀ x : A →ₐ[k] ℂ, (∀ i, ‖x (r i) - e (r i)‖ < ε) → ∃ w ∈ W, x = p w := by
  have hc : (algebraMap ℂ (ℂ ⊗[k] A)).IsStandardSmoothOfRelativeDimension d :=
    (RingHom.isStandardSmoothOfRelativeDimension_algebraMap d).2 inferInstance
  obtain ⟨φ, hφc, hφ, hφ0⟩ := HolChart.exists_etale_coords_vanishing _ hc (extPt e).toRingHom
    (fun a => (extPt e).commutes a)
  let _ : Algebra (MvPolynomial (Fin d) ℂ) (ℂ ⊗[k] A) := φ.toAlgebra
  have _ : IsScalarTower ℂ (MvPolynomial (Fin d) ℂ) (ℂ ⊗[k] A) :=
    IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq' hφc.symm
  have _ : Algebra.Etale (MvPolynomial (Fin d) ℂ) (ℂ ⊗[k] A) := hφ
  obtain ⟨W, σ, n, f, ε, hW, hW0, hσ0, hε, hσd, hσt, hσs⟩ :=
    exists_holomorphic_section_of_etale d (ℂ ⊗[k] A) (extPt e) hφ0
  choose F hF hF' using fun j => exists_continuous_extPt (f j)
  have hN : {u : A → ℂ | ∀ j, ‖F j u - extPt e (f j)‖ < ε} ∈ nhds (e : A → ℂ) := by
    refine IsOpen.mem_nhds ?_ fun j => ?_
    · rw [Set.setOf_forall]
      exact isOpen_iInter_of_finite fun j =>
        isOpen_lt (((hF j).sub continuous_const).norm) continuous_const
    · rw [hF', sub_self, norm_zero]; exact hε
  obtain ⟨m, r, δ, hδ, hr⟩ := exists_finite_test hN
  refine ⟨W, fun w => resPt (σ w), m, r, δ, hW, hW0, ?_, hδ, fun a => hσd (1 ⊗ₜ a), ?_, ?_⟩
  · show resPt (σ 0) = e
    rw [hσ0, resPt_extPt]
  · intro w hw w' hw' h
    have h1 : σ w = σ w' := by
      rw [← extPt_resPt (σ w), ← extPt_resPt (σ w')]
      exact congrArg extPt h
    funext i
    rw [← hσt w hw i, h1, hσt w' hw' i]
  · intro x hx
    obtain ⟨w, hw, h⟩ := hσs (extPt x) fun j => by
      rw [← hF']; exact hr x hx j
    exact ⟨w, hw, (resPt_extPt x).symm.trans (congrArg resPt h)⟩

end Ring

theorem exists_holomorphic_chart :
    ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] (σ : k →+* ℂ) (X : Scheme.{0}) (s : X ⟶ Sp k) (d : ℕ) (y₀ : Sp ℂ ⟶ X), SmoothOfRelativeDimension d s → y₀ ≫ s = spMap σ → ∃ (B : Set (Fin d → ℂ)) (y : (Fin d → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ X)) (U : X.Opens) (f : Fin d → Γ(X, U)) (n : ℕ) (r : Fin n → Γ(X, U)), IsOpen B ∧ (0 : Fin d → ℂ) ∈ B ∧ y 0 = y₀ ∧ (∀ b ∈ B, y b ≫ s = spMap σ) ∧ IsHolTo B y ∧ Set.InjOn y B ∧ ⊤ ≤ y₀ ⁻¹ᵁ U ∧ ∃ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε ∧ ∀ x : Sp ℂ ⟶ X, x ≫ s = spMap σ → ⊤ ≤ x ⁻¹ᵁ U → (∀ i, ‖valAt' x U (r i) - valAt' y₀ U (r i)‖ < ε) → x ∈ y '' B := by
  intro k _ σ X s d y₀ hX hy₀
  obtain ⟨U₀, -, U, hU, hp, hle, hsm⟩ :=
    hX.exists_isStandardSmoothOfRelativeDimension (y₀ (default : PrimeSpectrum ℂ))
  have hU₀ : U₀ = ⊤ := by
    refine eq_top_iff.2 fun y _ => ?_
    have h1 : s (y₀ (default : PrimeSpectrum ℂ)) ∈ U₀ := hle hp
    have h2 : y = s (y₀ (default : PrimeSpectrum ℂ)) :=
      Subsingleton.elim (α := PrimeSpectrum k) _ _
    rw [h2]; exact h1
  subst hU₀
  let c : k →+* Γ(X, U) :=
    (s.appLE ⊤ U hle).hom.comp (Scheme.ΓSpecIso (CommRingCat.of k)).inv.hom
  have hc : c.IsStandardSmoothOfRelativeDimension d :=
    RingHom.isStandardSmoothOfRelativeDimension_respectsIso.2 (s.appLE ⊤ U hle).hom
      (Scheme.ΓSpecIso (CommRingCat.of k)).symm.commRingCatIsoToRingEquiv hsm
  let _ : Algebra k ℂ := σ.toAlgebra
  let _ : Algebra k Γ(X, U) := c.toAlgebra
  have _ : Algebra.IsStandardSmoothOfRelativeDimension d k Γ(X, U) := hc
  let pt : (Γ(X, U) →+* ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ X) := fun e =>
    Spec.map (CommRingCat.ofHom e : Γ(X, U) ⟶ CommRingCat.of ℂ) ≫ hU.fromSpec
  have hptU : ∀ e, ⊤ ≤ pt e ⁻¹ᵁ U := fun e p _ => hU.range_fromSpec.le ⟨_, rfl⟩
  have hptv : ∀ e f, valAt' (pt e) U f = e f := fun e f => by
    rw [valAt', dif_pos (hptU e)]
    exact congrArg (fun ψ => ψ.hom f)
      (AffinePointValues.appLE_specMap_fromSpec hU (CommRingCat.ofHom e) (hptU e))
  have hpts : ∀ e, pt e ≫ s = spMap (e.comp c) := fun e => by
    simp only [pt]
    rw [Category.assoc, AffinePointValues.fromSpec_comp hU s hle, ← Spec.map_comp]
    rfl
  have hsurj : ∀ x : Sp ℂ ⟶ X, x ≫ s = spMap σ → ⊤ ≤ x ⁻¹ᵁ U →
      ∃ e : Γ(X, U) →ₐ[k] ℂ, pt e.toRingHom = x := by
    intro x hxs hx
    let e : Γ(X, U) →+* ℂ := (x.appLE U ⊤ hx ≫ (Scheme.ΓSpecIso (CommRingCat.of ℂ)).hom).hom
    have he : pt e = x := AffinePointValues.specMap_appLE_fromSpec hU (R := CommRingCat.of ℂ) x hx
    have h1 : spMap (e.comp c) = spMap σ := by rw [← hpts, he, hxs]
    have h2 : e.comp c = σ := congrArg CommRingCat.Hom.hom (Spec.map_injective h1)
    exact ⟨{ e with commutes' := fun a => RingHom.congr_fun h2 a }, he⟩
  have hyU : ⊤ ≤ y₀ ⁻¹ᵁ U := ((valAt'_rules X y₀).1 U).2 hp
  obtain ⟨e, rfl⟩ := hsurj y₀ hy₀ hyU
  obtain ⟨W, p, n, r, ε, hW, hW0, hp0, hε, hpd, hpi, hps⟩ := exists_chart_algebra d e
  refine ⟨W, fun w => pt (p w).toRingHom, U, fun _ => 0, n, r, hW, hW0, by simp only [hp0],
    fun b _ => ?_, fun V f => ?_, fun w hw w' hw' h => hpi hw hw' ?_, hyU, ε, hε,
    fun x hxs hx hxe => ?_⟩
  · rw [hpts]
    congr 2
    exact RingHom.ext fun a => (p b).commutes a
  · exact HolChart.hol_of_affine valAt'_rules hU hW (fun w => pt (p w).toRingHom)
      (fun w _ => hptU _) (fun a => (hpd a).congr fun w _ => hptv _ a) V f
  · refine AlgHom.ext fun a => ?_
    have h1 := hptv (p w).toRingHom a
    have h' : pt (p w).toRingHom = pt (p w').toRingHom := h
    rw [h', hptv] at h1
    exact h1.symm
  · obtain ⟨e', rfl⟩ := hsurj x hxs hx
    obtain ⟨w, hw, h⟩ := hps e' fun i => by
      have := hxe i
      rw [hptv, hptv] at this
      exact this
    exact ⟨w, hw, by rw [h]⟩

end GlobalTheory.SubfieldChart


[HUF_SRC_REVERSE_POINT] Literal reverse point uniformization at every given original moduli point
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/ExistsUniformisationAtPointClosed.lean#L18-L22
theorem exists_uniformisation_at_point_closed : ThetaUnifAV → Out.Laws → ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (Xo : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) (g : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)), 1 ≤ D.p + D.q → σ (baseKA l).ρ = H.rt0 → IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → ∀ y : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X, y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ → ∃ (D' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (P : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) (e : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G)), (D'.p = D.p ∧ D'.q = D.q ∧ ∃ (f : ℕ → D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (ν : ℕ → ℤ), (∀ n m : ℕ, 0 < n → 0 < m → ∀ a, ∃ e, f (n * m) a = f n a + n • e) ∧ (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → (∀ a, (∃ e, f n a = n • e) ↔ ∃ e, a = n • e) ∧ ∀ a', ∃ a e, a' = f n a + n • e) ∧ (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∀ a a', (n : ℤ) ∣ D'.EF (f n a) (f n a') - ν n * D.EF a a') ∧ σ (baseKA l).ζ = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (ν l.A : ℂ) / l.A) ∧ t = f l.M') ∧ (y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ IsPeriod D' ty P ∧ ((∀ z w, e (z + w) = e z * e w) ∧ Function.Surjective e ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => (e z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = (e 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e z).left) w v.left)) ∧ (∀ z, e z = 1 ↔ z ∈ (P).range) ∧ (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, e (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = e z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a) ∧ (∀ (k : ℕ) (a a₂ : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k → (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a a₂ : ℂ) / k) → Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k (e ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a)) (e ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a₂)) (unitOn c)) ∧ (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = e ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t a))) ∧ ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = e ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t a))) :=
  fun hT hL => exists_uniformisation_at_point hT hL GlobalTheory.LiePull.lieMult_pull GlobalTheory.obj_pull
    GlobalTheory.obj_isAbelianScheme_dim_rigidified_sym GlobalTheory.PeriodDatum.exists_datum

end GlobalTheory.PointUnif


[HUF_SRC_REVERSE_POINT_CORE] Original point extraction, integral period datum and Tate-marking assembly
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/ExistsUniformisationAtPoint.lean#L16-L120

theorem exists_uniformisation_at_point : ThetaUnifAV → Out.Laws → (∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] (S S' : Scheme.{0}) (G : Over S) (G' : Over S') [GrpObj G] [GrpObj G'] (f : S' ⟶ S) (φ : G'.left ⟶ G.left) (a : OAct O G) (a' : OAct O G'), IsGrpPullback G' G f φ → (∀ c, (a'.act c).left ≫ φ = φ ≫ (a.act c).left) → ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] (x : Sp k ⟶ S') (τ : O →+* k) (n : ℕ), LieMult a' x τ n ↔ LieMult a (x ≫ f) τ n) → (∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (B B' : Type) [CommRing B] [CommRing B'] (b : Base l B) (b' : Base l B') (j : B →+* B') (S S' : Scheme.{0}) (s : S ⟶ Sp B) (s' : S' ⟶ Sp B') (f : S' ⟶ S), j b.ρ = b'.ρ → j b.ζ = b'.ζ → f ≫ s = s' ≫ spMap j → ∀ o : Obj D l b s, ∃ o' : Obj D l b' s', Nonempty (ObjMap o' o f)) → (∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (B : Type) [CommRing B] (b : Base l B) (S : Scheme.{0}) (s : S ⟶ Sp B) (o : Obj D l b s), IsAbelianScheme o.t.G ∧ SmoothOfRelativeDimension (Module.finrank ℚ E / 2 * (D.p + D.q)) o.t.G.hom ∧ Out.Rigidified o.t.G o.t.PP.L ∧ Out.Sym o.t.G o.t.PP.L) → (∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (La : Type) [AddCommGroup La] [Module (𝓞 E) La] (EF : LinearMap.BilinForm ℤ La) (p q g : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)) (P : La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) (Hm : Matrix (Fin g) (Fin g) ℂ), IsTypeFn H p q ty → Function.Bijective EF → (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) x y, EF (a • x) y = EF x (H.bar a • y)) → (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) x i, P (a • x) i = ty i (a : E) * P x i) → Function.Injective P → DiscreteTopology P.range → Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range P) = ⊤ → Hm.IsHermitian → (∀ z : Fin g → ℂ, z ≠ 0 → 0 < (hermForm Hm z z).re) → (∀ x y, (hermForm Hm (P x) (P y)).im = EF x y) → ∃ (D' : Datum H) (e : D'.La ≃ₗ[𝓞 E] La), D'.p = p ∧ D'.q = q ∧ ∀ x y, D'.EF x y = EF (e x) (e y)) → ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (Xo : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) (g : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)), 1 ≤ D.p + D.q → σ (baseKA l).ρ = H.rt0 → IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → ∀ y : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X, y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ → ∃ (D' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (P : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) (e : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G)), (D'.p = D.p ∧ D'.q = D.q ∧ ∃ (f : ℕ → D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (ν : ℕ → ℤ), (∀ n m : ℕ, 0 < n → 0 < m → ∀ a, ∃ e, f (n * m) a = f n a + n • e) ∧ (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → (∀ a, (∃ e, f n a = n • e) ↔ ∃ e, a = n • e) ∧ ∀ a', ∃ a e, a' = f n a + n • e) ∧ (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∀ a a', (n : ℤ) ∣ D'.EF (f n a) (f n a') - ν n * D.EF a a') ∧ σ (baseKA l).ζ = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (ν l.A : ℂ) / l.A) ∧ t = f l.M') ∧ (y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ IsPeriod D' ty P ∧ ((∀ z w, e (z + w) = e z * e w) ∧ Function.Surjective e ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => (e z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = (e 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e z).left) w v.left)) ∧ (∀ z, e z = 1 ↔ z ∈ (P).range) ∧ (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, e (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = e z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a) ∧ (∀ (k : ℕ) (a a₂ : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k → (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a a₂ : ℂ) / k) → Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k (e ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a)) (e ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a₂)) (unitOn c)) ∧ (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = e ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t a))) ∧ ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = e ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t a))) := by
  intro hT _ _ hPull hPol hDat E _ _ _ H D l Xo σ g ty _ hσ hty y hy
  let b' : Base l ℂ :=
    { unit := fun n hn => IsUnit.mk0 _ (Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 hn.ne')
      ρ := σ (baseKA l).ρ
      ρ_sq := by rw [← map_pow, (baseKA l).ρ_sq, map_neg, map_natCast]
      ζ := σ (baseKA l).ζ
      ζ_prim := (baseKA l).ζ_prim.map_of_injective σ.injective }
  obtain ⟨o, ⟨m⟩⟩ := hPull E H D l (KA l) ℂ (baseKA l) b' σ Xo.X (Sp ℂ) Xo.s (𝟙 _) y rfl rfl
    (hy.trans (Category.id_comp _).symm) Xo.univ
  obtain ⟨hab, hsm, hrig, hsym⟩ := hPol E H D l ℂ b' (Sp ℂ) (𝟙 _) o
  obtain ⟨rig, hr⟩ := id hrig
  obtain ⟨u, Hm, χ, Ef, hAH, -, h1, h2, hF, -, hE, hbij, hweil⟩ :=
    hT { A := o.t.G, isAb := hab, g := _, smoothDim := hsm, L := o.t.PP.L, isLB := o.t.PP.isLB, rigid := hrig,
         sym := hsym, ample := o.ample, ktriv := o.ktriv } rig hr
  have hLiso : LBIso o.t.PP.L (Xo.univ.t.PP.L.pull m.cm.F) := by
    obtain ⟨w, hw, -⟩ := m.lin
    exact ⟨w, hw⟩
  obtain ⟨Φ, hΦ, hΦW⟩ := exists_push m.cart (𝟙_ _)
    (weilIs_grpPullback_of_comm _ _ _ _ y m.cm.F _ _ Xo.univ.t.PP.isLB o.t.PP.isLB m.cart hLiso _) y (Category.id_comp y)
  have he₀ := isExp_push m.cart u h1 h2 Φ hΦ
  have hker₀ : ∀ z, Φ (u.ex z) = 1 ↔ z ∈ u.Λ := fun z => (map_eq_one_iff Φ Φ.injective).trans (u.ker z)
  obtain ⟨ρ, hρ⟩ := he₀.exists_rep Xo.univ.t.act u.Λ u.discrete hker₀ fun a => by
    obtain ⟨F, hF⟩ := hF (o.t.act.act a) (o.t.act.hom a)
    refine ⟨F, fun z => Over.OverMorphism.ext ?_⟩
    rw [hΦ, hF, Over.comp_left, Over.comp_left, hΦ, Category.assoc, Category.assoc]
    exact congrArg _ (m.act a)
  obtain ⟨hdet, I, hζ, hA, hM⟩ := Xo.univ.geom ℂ y σ hy
  obtain ⟨h, hh⟩ := exists_equiv_of_finrank_eq (isSplitting_embeddings (E := E)) (diagRep ty) ρ fun φ => by
    rw [finrank_eigenSub_diagRep]
    by_cases hφ : φ (H.rt : E) = H.rt0
    · rw [(hty φ).1 hφ, he₀.finrank_eigenSub _ ρ hρ ((hdet _).1 (hφ.trans hσ.symm))]
    · rw [(hty φ).2 hφ, he₀.finrank_eigenSub _ ρ hρ ((hdet _).2 fun h => hφ (h.trans hσ))]
  have he := he₀.comp_equiv h
  have hlin : ∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, Φ (u.ex (h fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k)) = Φ (u.ex (h z)) ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a :=
    fun a z => by rw [← hρ, ← hh, diagRep_apply]
  have hst : ∀ a, ∀ x ∈ u.Λ, ρ a x ∈ u.Λ := fun a x hx => by
    have := Xo.univ.t.act.hom a
    rw [← hker₀, hρ, (hker₀ x).2 hx, MonObj.one_comp]
  letI : SMul (𝓞 E) u.Λ := ⟨fun a x => ⟨ρ a x, hst a x x.2⟩⟩
  have hsm : ∀ (a : 𝓞 E) (x : u.Λ), ((a • x : u.Λ) : _ → ℂ) = ρ a x := fun _ _ => rfl
  letI : Module (𝓞 E) u.Λ := Module.ofMinimalAxioms
    (fun a x y => Subtype.ext (by simp [hsm])) (fun a b x => Subtype.ext (by simp [hsm]))
    (fun a b x => Subtype.ext (by simp [hsm])) (fun x => Subtype.ext (by simp [hsm]))
  let P : u.Λ →+ (Fin g → ℂ) := h.symm.toLinearMap.toAddMonoidHom.comp u.Λ.subtype.toAddMonoidHom
  have hP : ∀ x, h (P x) = x := fun x => h.apply_symm_apply _
  have hrange : ∀ z, z ∈ P.range ↔ h z ∈ u.Λ := fun z =>
    ⟨by rintro ⟨x, rfl⟩; rw [hP]; exact x.2, fun hz => ⟨⟨_, hz⟩, h.symm_apply_apply z⟩⟩
  have hker : ∀ z, Φ (u.ex (h z)) = 1 ↔ z ∈ P.range := fun z => (hker₀ _).trans (hrange z).symm
  have hPs : ∀ (a : 𝓞 E) x i, P (a • x) i = ty i (a : E) * P x i := fun a x => congrFun (h.injective (by
    rw [hP, hsm, ← diagRep_apply, hh, hP]))
  have hPi : Function.Injective P := h.symm.injective.comp Subtype.val_injective
  have hPd : DiscreteTopology P.range := by
    have := u.discrete
    refine DiscreteTopology.of_continuous_injective (f := fun z : P.range => (⟨h z, (hrange _).1 z.2⟩ : u.Λ))
      ((h.toLinearMap.continuous_of_finiteDimensional.comp continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _) fun z w hzw => ?_
    exact Subtype.ext (h.injective (congrArg Subtype.val hzw))
  have hPf : Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range P) = ⊤ := by
    have e : Set.range P = (h.symm.toLinearMap.restrictScalars ℝ) '' u.Λ := by
      ext z
      exact ⟨by rintro ⟨x, rfl⟩; exact ⟨x, x.2, rfl⟩, by rintro ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩; exact ⟨⟨x, hx⟩, rfl⟩⟩
    rw [e, ← Submodule.map_span, u.full, Submodule.map_top, LinearMap.range_eq_top]
    exact h.symm.surjective
  obtain ⟨Hm', hH', hH''⟩ := exists_hermForm_comp Hm hAH.1 h.toLinearMap
  have hpos : ∀ z : Fin g → ℂ, z ≠ 0 → 0 < (hermForm Hm' z z).re := fun z hz => by
    rw [hH'']
    exact hAH.2.1 _ (h.map_ne_zero_iff.2 hz)
  have him : ∀ x x', (hermForm Hm' (P x) (P x')).im = Ef x x' := fun x x' => by
    rw [hH'', hE]
    exact congrArg₂ (fun a b => (hermForm Hm a b).im) (hP x) (hP x')
  have hweil' : ∀ (k : ℕ) (a b : u.Λ) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k →
      (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (Ef a b : ℂ) / k) →
      Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k (Φ (u.ex (h ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a)))) (Φ (u.ex (h ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P b))))
        (unitOn c) := fun k a b c hk hc => by
    rw [map_smul, map_smul, hP, hP]
    exact hΦW _ _ _ _ (hweil k a b c hk hc)
  obtain ⟨D', ε, hp, hq, hε⟩ := hDat E H u.Λ Ef D.p D.q g ty P Hm' hty hbij
    (adj_of_tateIso Xo.univ.t.PP.isLB he hker hPs hlin hweil' I) hPs hPi hPd hPf hH' hpos him
  let P' : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ) := P.comp ε.toLinearMap.toAddMonoidHom
  have hr' : P'.range = P.range := by
    ext z
    exact ⟨by rintro ⟨x, rfl⟩; exact ⟨ε x, rfl⟩,
      by rintro ⟨x, rfl⟩; exact ⟨ε.symm x, congrArg P (ε.apply_symm_apply x)⟩⟩
  have hper : IsPeriod D' ty P' := by
    refine ⟨fun a x i => ?_, hPi.comp ε.injective, hr' ▸ hPd, ?_, Hm', hH', hpos, fun x x' => ?_⟩
    · exact (congrArg (fun x => P x i) (map_smul ε a x)).trans (hPs a _ i)
    · rw [← AddMonoidHom.coe_range, hr', AddMonoidHom.coe_range, hPf]
    · rw [hε]; exact him _ _
  have hker' : ∀ z, Φ (u.ex (h z)) = 1 ↔ z ∈ P'.range := hr' ▸ hker
  have hweil'' : ∀ (k : ℕ) (a b : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k →
      (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a b : ℂ) / k) →
      Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k (Φ (u.ex (h ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P' a)))) (Φ (u.ex (h ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P' b))))
        (unitOn c) := fun k a b c hk hc => hweil' k (ε a) (ε b) c hk (by rw [hc, hε])
  obtain ⟨f, ν, f1, f2, f3, f4, f5⟩ := similar_with_multiplier
    (fun n x y c c' => weilIs_unique_fieldPoint _ _ _ Xo.univ.t.PP.isLB ℂ _ n x y c c') I
    (expTateIso he hper hker' hlin hweil'')
  have hM' : 0 < l.M' := Nat.lcm_pos (lt_of_lt_of_le (by norm_num) l.three_le) l.A_pos
  refine ⟨D', f l.M', P', fun z => Φ (u.ex (h z)), ⟨hp, hq, f, ν, f1, f2, f3, ?_, rfl⟩, hy, hper, he, hker', hlin,
    hweil'', fun a ha => (hM a ha).trans (f5 _ _ hM' (Nat.dvd_lcm_left _ _) a),
    fun a => (hA a).trans (f5 _ _ hM' (Nat.dvd_lcm_right _ _) a)⟩
  rw [← hζ, f4 _ l.A_pos]
  exact expNegUnit_zpow_val _ _

end GlobalTheory.PointUnif


[HUF_SRC_TATE_LIFT] Exact projective lifting and genus_of_tateIso, with positive-level guards
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/PeriodLatticeInGenus.lean#L19-L91
theorem ev_eq_iff (I : TateIso D k Q act e) {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (a b : D.La) :
    I.ev n a = I.ev n b ↔ ∃ y, a = b + n • y := by
  rw [tateIso_ev_eq_iff I hn]
  unfold Datum.ModN
  exact exists_congr fun y => sub_eq_iff_eq_add'

theorem exists_lift (I' : TateIso D' k Q act e) (I : TateIso D k Q act e) {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) :
    ∃ f : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La, ∀ a, I.ev n a = I'.ev n (f a) := by
  have hs : ∀ a, ∃ b, I'.ev n b = I.ev n a := fun a => (I'.onto n hn _).1 (tateIso_ev_pow I hn a)
  choose s hs using hs
  let N : Submodule (𝓞 E) D'.La := LinearMap.range (n • (LinearMap.id : D'.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La))
  have hN : ∀ a b : D'.La, N.mkQ a = N.mkQ b ↔ I'.ev n a = I'.ev n b := by
    intro a b
    rw [tateIso_ev_eq_iff I' hn, Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.Quotient.eq]
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨c, hc⟩
      exact ⟨c, hc.symm⟩
    · rintro ⟨c, hc⟩
      exact ⟨c, hc.symm⟩
  let φ : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La ⧸ N :=
    { toFun := fun a => N.mkQ (s a)
      map_add' := fun a b => by
        rw [← map_add, hN, I'.add, hs, hs, hs, I.add]
      map_smul' := fun c a => by
        rw [RingHom.id_apply, ← map_smul, hN, I'.lin, hs, hs, I.lin] }
  have := D.proj
  obtain ⟨f, hf⟩ := Module.projective_lifting_property N.mkQ φ N.mkQ_surjective
  refine ⟨f, fun a => ?_⟩
  have h : N.mkQ (f a) = N.mkQ (s a) := LinearMap.congr_fun hf a
  rw [(hN _ _).1 h, hs]

theorem genus_of_tateIso
    (he : ∀ n x y c c', 0 < n → x ^ n = 1 → y ^ n = 1 → e n x y c → e n x y c' → c = c')
    (I' : TateIso D' k Q act e) (I : TateIso D k Q act e) :
    ∃ (f : ℕ → D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (ν : ℕ → ℤ),
      ((∀ n m : ℕ, 0 < n → 0 < m → ∀ x, ∃ y, f (n * m) x = f n x + n • y) ∧
      (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → (∀ x, (∃ y, f n x = n • y) ↔ ∃ y, x = n • y) ∧ ∀ x', ∃ x y, x' = f n x + n • y) ∧
      ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∀ x x', (n : ℤ) ∣ D'.EF (f n x) (f n x') - ν n * D.EF x x') ∧
      ∀ n, 0 < n → I.ζ n = I'.ζ n ^ ν n ∧ ∀ a, I.ev n a = I'.ev n (f n a) := by
  have h1 : ∀ n : ℕ, ∃ f : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La, 0 < n → ∀ a, I.ev n a = I'.ev n (f a) := by
    intro n
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos n with h | h
    · exact ⟨0, fun h' => absurd h h'.ne'⟩
    · obtain ⟨f, hf⟩ := exists_lift I' I h
      exact ⟨f, fun _ => hf⟩
  have h2 : ∀ n : ℕ, ∃ ν : ℤ, 0 < n → I.ζ n = I'.ζ n ^ ν := by
    intro n
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos n with h | h
    · exact ⟨1, fun h' => absurd h h'.ne'⟩
    · have : NeZero n := ⟨h.ne'⟩
      obtain ⟨i, -, hi⟩ := (I'.ζ_prim n h).eq_pow_of_mem_rootsOfUnity
        ((mem_rootsOfUnity _ _).2 (I.ζ_prim n h).pow_eq_one)
      exact ⟨i, fun _ => by rw [zpow_natCast, hi]⟩
  choose f hf using h1
  choose ν hν using h2
  refine ⟨f, ν, ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩, fun n hn => ⟨hν n hn, hf n hn⟩⟩
  · intro n m hn hm a
    rw [← ev_eq_iff I' hn, ← hf n hn, ← I'.tower n m hn hm, ← hf (n * m) (Nat.mul_pos hn hm),
      I.tower n m hn hm]
  · intro n hn
    refine ⟨fun x => ?_, fun x' => ?_⟩
    · rw [← I'.ker n hn, ← I.ker n hn, hf n hn]
    · obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := (I.onto n hn _).1 (tateIso_ev_pow I' hn x')
      refine ⟨x, (ev_eq_iff I' hn _ _).1 ?_⟩
      rw [← hf n hn, hx]
  · intro n hn a b
    have e1 := I'.isom n hn (f n a) (f n b)
    have e2 := I.isom n hn a b
    rw [hf n hn a, hf n hn b] at e2
    have h := he n _ _ _ _ hn (tateIso_ev_pow I' hn _) (tateIso_ev_pow I' hn _) e1 e2
    rw [hν n hn, ← zpow_mul] at h
    rw [← (I'.ζ_prim n hn).zpow_eq_one_iff_dvd, zpow_sub, h, mul_inv_cancel]



[HUF_SRC_PERIOD_TATE] Original exponential Tate description and period_lattice_in_genus
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/PeriodLatticeInGenus.lean#L94-L205
theorem unitOn_injective {R : Type} [CommRing R] : Function.Injective (unitOn (R := R)) := fun a b h => by
  have h1 := congrArg (fun x : (Γ(Sp R, ⊤))ˣ => (Scheme.ΓSpecIso (CommRingCat.of R)).hom.hom x.val) h
  apply Units.ext
  simpa [unitOn] using h1

noncomputable def expRoot (n : ℕ) : ℂˣ :=
  Units.mk0 (Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) / n)) (Complex.exp_ne_zero _)

theorem expRoot_zpow (n : ℕ) (m : ℤ) :
    ((expRoot n ^ m : ℂˣ) : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (m : ℂ) / n) := by
  rw [Units.val_zpow_eq_zpow_val, expRoot, Units.val_mk0, ← Complex.exp_int_mul]
  congr 1
  ring

theorem expRoot_prim {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) : IsPrimitiveRoot (expRoot n) n := by
  rw [← IsPrimitiveRoot.coe_units_iff]
  have h := (Complex.isPrimitiveRoot_exp n hn.ne').inv
  rw [← Complex.exp_neg] at h
  rw [expRoot, Units.val_mk0, neg_div]
  exact h

theorem expRoot_pow {n m : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (hm : 0 < m) : expRoot (n * m) ^ m = expRoot n := by
  apply Units.ext
  rw [Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, expRoot, expRoot, Units.val_mk0, Units.val_mk0, ← Complex.exp_nat_mul]
  congr 1
  have : (n : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
  have : (m : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hm.ne'
  push_cast
  field_simp

section

variable {A : Over (Sp ℂ)} [GrpObj A] {L : LB A.left} {g : ℕ} (u : HolUnif A L g)

theorem ex_zero : u.ex 0 = 1 := (u.ker 0).2 u.Λ.zero_mem

theorem ex_nsmul (n : ℕ) (z : Fin g → ℂ) : u.ex ((n : ℂ) • z) = u.ex z ^ n := by
  induction n with
  | zero => simp [ex_zero]
  | succ n ih => rw [Nat.cast_succ, add_smul, one_smul, u.ex_add, ih, pow_succ]

end

noncomputable def periodTateIso {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] {H : Host E} (D' : Datum H)
    {A : Over (Sp ℂ)} [GrpObj A] {L : LB A.left} {g : ℕ} (u : HolUnif A L g) (act : OAct (𝓞 E) A)
    (Q : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) (hQ : Function.Injective Q) (hΛ : ∀ z, z ∈ u.Λ ↔ z ∈ Q.range)
    (hact : ∀ (a : 𝓞 E) (n : ℕ) x, u.ex ((n : ℂ)⁻¹ • Q (a • x)) = u.ex ((n : ℂ)⁻¹ • Q x) ≫ act.act a)
    (hW : ∀ (n : ℕ) (a b : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < n →
      (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a b : ℂ) / n) →
      Out.WeilIs A L n (u.ex ((n : ℂ)⁻¹ • Q a)) (u.ex ((n : ℂ)⁻¹ • Q b)) (unitOn c)) :
    TateIso D' ℂ (𝟙_ (Over (Sp ℂ)) ⟶ A) (fun c y => y ≫ act.act c)
      (fun n y y' c => Out.WeilIs A L n y y' (unitOn c)) where
  ζ := expRoot
  ζ_prim _ hn := expRoot_prim hn
  ζ_pow _ _ hn hm := expRoot_pow hn hm
  ev n x := u.ex ((n : ℂ)⁻¹ • Q x)
  add n a b := by rw [map_add, smul_add, u.ex_add]
  lin n c a := hact c n a
  ker n hn a := by
    have hn' : (n : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
    rw [u.ker, hΛ]
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨b, hb⟩
      refine ⟨b, hQ ?_⟩
      rw [map_nsmul, hb, ← Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul ℂ, smul_smul, mul_inv_cancel₀ hn', one_smul]
    · rintro ⟨b, rfl⟩
      refine ⟨b, ?_⟩
      rw [map_nsmul, ← Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul ℂ, smul_smul, inv_mul_cancel₀ hn', one_smul]
  onto n hn x := by
    have hn' : (n : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
    constructor
    · intro hx
      obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := u.ex_onto x
      rw [← ex_nsmul, u.ker, hΛ] at hx
      obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := hx
      exact ⟨a, by rw [ha, smul_smul, inv_mul_cancel₀ hn', one_smul]⟩
    · rintro ⟨a, rfl⟩
      rw [← ex_nsmul, u.ker, hΛ, smul_smul, mul_inv_cancel₀ hn', one_smul]
      exact ⟨a, rfl⟩
  tower n m hn hm a := by
    have hn' : (n : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
    have hm' : (m : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hm.ne'
    rw [← ex_nsmul, smul_smul]
    congr 2
    push_cast
    field_simp
  isom n hn a b := hW n a b _ hn (expRoot_zpow n _)

theorem period_lattice_in_genus :
    Out.Laws → Out.Laws2 → (∀ (S : Scheme.{0}) (P : Out.PolAV S) (M : LB P.A.left), M.IsLB → ∃ t : LB.Gen (Theta P.A M), t.IsGen) → ThetaUnifAV → ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (D' : Datum H) (P : Out.PolAV (Sp ℂ)) (u : HolUnif P.A P.L P.g) (Hm : Matrix (Fin P.g) (Fin P.g) ℂ) (χ : u.Λ → ℂ) (act : OAct (𝓞 E) P.A) (Q : D'.La →+ (Fin P.g → ℂ)), D'.p = D.p → D'.q = D.q → u.IsAH Hm χ → Function.Injective Q → (∀ z, z ∈ u.Λ ↔ z ∈ Q.range) → (∀ x y, (hermForm Hm (Q x) (Q y)).im = D'.EF x y) → (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) (n : ℕ) x, u.ex ((n : ℂ)⁻¹ • Q (a • x)) = u.ex ((n : ℂ)⁻¹ • Q x) ≫ act.act a) → ∀ I : TateIso D ℂ (Over.mk (𝟙 (Sp ℂ)) ⟶ P.A) (fun c y => y ≫ act.act c) (fun n y y' c => Out.WeilIs P.A P.L n y y' (unitOn c)), ∃ (f : ℕ → D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (ν : ℕ → ℤ), (D'.p = D.p ∧ D'.q = D.q ∧ (∀ n m : ℕ, 0 < n → 0 < m → ∀ x, ∃ y, f (n * m) x = f n x + n • y) ∧ (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → (∀ x, (∃ y, f n x = n • y) ↔ ∃ y, x = n • y) ∧ ∀ x', ∃ x y, x' = f n x + n • y) ∧ ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∀ x x', (n : ℤ) ∣ D'.EF (f n x) (f n x') - ν n * D.EF x x') ∧ ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → (I.ζ n : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (ν n : ℂ) / n) ∧ ∀ x, (I.ev n x).left = (u.ex ((n : ℂ)⁻¹ • Q (f n x))).left := by
  intro hL hL2 hT hU E _ _ _ H D D' P u Hm χ act Q hp hq hAH hQ hΛ hE hact I
  obtain ⟨Ef, hEf, -, -, -, -, -, -, hW⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.appellHumbert_uniformisation_riemannForm hL hL2 hT hU P u Hm χ hAH
  have hW' : ∀ (n : ℕ) (a b : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < n →
      (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a b : ℂ) / n) →
      Out.WeilIs P.A P.L n (u.ex ((n : ℂ)⁻¹ • Q a)) (u.ex ((n : ℂ)⁻¹ • Q b)) (unitOn c) := by
    intro n a b c hn hc
    refine hW n ⟨Q a, (hΛ _).2 ⟨a, rfl⟩⟩ ⟨Q b, (hΛ _).2 ⟨b, rfl⟩⟩ c hn ?_
    have h := hEf ⟨Q a, (hΛ _).2 ⟨a, rfl⟩⟩ ⟨Q b, (hΛ _).2 ⟨b, rfl⟩⟩
    rw [hE] at h
    rw [Int.cast_injective h]
    exact hc
  obtain ⟨f, ν, ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩, h4⟩ := genus_of_tateIso (D := D) (D' := D')
    (fun n x y c c' _ hx hy ha hb =>
      unitOn_injective (((hL.weil _ P n (Over.mk (𝟙 (Sp ℂ)))).1 x y hx hy).unique ha hb))
    (periodTateIso D' u act Q hQ hΛ hact hW') I
  refine ⟨f, ν, ⟨hp, hq, h1, h2, h3⟩, fun n hn => ⟨?_, fun x => ?_⟩⟩
  · rw [(h4 n hn).1]
    exact expRoot_zpow n _
  · rw [(h4 n hn).2]
    rfl

end GlobalTheory.TorsionGenus


[HUF_SRC_RELATIVE_EXP] Original local jointly holomorphic relative exponential interface
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/ExistsRelativeExpOfParts.lean#L53-L160
theorem exists_relative_exp_of_parts
    (hchart : ∀ (m g : ℕ) (Z X : Scheme.{0}) (p : Z ⟶ X) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (y : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ X)) (b₀ : Fin m → ℂ) (z₀ : Sp ℂ ⟶ Z), SmoothOfRelativeDimension g p → IsOpen B → b₀ ∈ B → IsHolTo B y → z₀ ≫ p = y b₀ → ∃ (U : Z.Opens) (t : Fin g → Γ(Z, U)) (n : ℕ) (f : Fin n → Γ(Z, U)) (N : Set ((Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ))) (O : Set ((Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin n → ℂ))) (σ : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ Z)), IsOpen N ∧ IsOpen O ∧ ⊤ ≤ z₀ ⁻¹ᵁ U ∧ (b₀, fun j => valAt' z₀ U (f j)) ∈ O ∧ IsHolTo N σ ∧ (∀ q ∈ N, σ q ≫ p = y q.1 ∧ ⊤ ≤ σ q ⁻¹ᵁ U ∧ ∀ i, valAt' (σ q) U (t i) = q.2 i) ∧ (∀ (b : Fin m → ℂ) (x : Sp ℂ ⟶ Z), x ≫ p = y b → ⊤ ≤ x ⁻¹ᵁ U → (b, fun j => valAt' x U (f j)) ∈ O → (b, fun i => valAt' x U (t i)) ∈ N ∧ σ (b, fun i => valAt' x U (t i)) = x) ∧ ∀ (v v' : Sp (DualNumber ℂ) ⟶ Z) (h : ⊤ ≤ v ⁻¹ᵁ U) (h' : ⊤ ≤ v' ⁻¹ᵁ U), v ≫ p = v' ≫ p → spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v = spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v' → (∀ i, GlobalTheory.TangentOrigin.evalPt v U h (t i) = GlobalTheory.TangentOrigin.evalPt v' U h' (t i)) → v = v')
    (htan : ∀ (X : Scheme.{0}) (G : Over X) (x : Sp ℂ ⟶ X) (g : ℕ) (e : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk x ⟶ G)), IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => (e z).left) → (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt x ⟶ G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e z).left) w v.left) ∧ ∀ (U : G.left.Opens) (t : Fin g → Γ(G.left, U)), ⊤ ≤ (show Sp ℂ ⟶ G.left from (e 0).left) ⁻¹ᵁ U → (∀ (v v' : Sp (DualNumber ℂ) ⟶ G.left) (h : ⊤ ≤ v ⁻¹ᵁ U) (h' : ⊤ ≤ v' ⁻¹ᵁ U), v ≫ G.hom = v' ≫ G.hom → spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v = spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v' → (∀ i, GlobalTheory.TangentOrigin.evalPt v U h (t i) = GlobalTheory.TangentOrigin.evalPt v' U h' (t i)) → v = v') → ((∀ v : epsPt x ⟶ G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = (e 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e z).left) w v.left) ↔ Function.Injective (fderiv ℂ (fun z i => valAt' (e z).left U (t i)) 0)))
    (hexp : ∀ (m g : ℕ) (b₀ : Fin m → ℂ) (M : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) → (Fin g → ℂ)), AnalyticAt ℂ M (b₀, 0, 0) → (∀ᶠ q : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) in nhds (b₀, 0), M (q.1, q.2, 0) = q.2) → (∀ᶠ q : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) in nhds (b₀, 0, 0), M (q.1, q.2.1, q.2.2) = M (q.1, q.2.2, q.2.1)) → (∀ᶠ q : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) in nhds (b₀, 0, 0, 0), M (q.1, M (q.1, q.2.1, q.2.2.1), q.2.2.2) = M (q.1, q.2.1, M (q.1, q.2.2.1, q.2.2.2))) → ∃ E L : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) → (Fin g → ℂ), AnalyticAt ℂ E (b₀, 0) ∧ AnalyticAt ℂ L (b₀, 0) ∧ (∀ᶠ b in nhds b₀, E (b, 0) = 0 ∧ HasFDerivAt (fun v => E (b, v)) (ContinuousLinearMap.id ℂ (Fin g → ℂ)) 0) ∧ (∀ᶠ q : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) in nhds (b₀, 0), L (q.1, E q) = q.2 ∧ E (q.1, L q) = q.2) ∧ ∀ᶠ q : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) in nhds (b₀, 0, 0), E (q.1, q.2.1 + q.2.2) = M (q.1, E (q.1, q.2.1), E (q.1, q.2.2)))
    (hsub : ∀ (X : Scheme.{0}) (G : Over X) [GrpObj G] (x : Sp ℂ ⟶ X) (H : Subgroup (Over.mk x ⟶ G)) (U : G.left.Opens) (κ : Type) [Fintype κ] (f : κ → Γ(G.left, U)) (O : Set (κ → ℂ)), Smooth G.hom → GeometricallyConnected G.hom → IsOpen O → ⊤ ≤ (show Sp ℂ ⟶ G.left from (1 : Over.mk x ⟶ G).left) ⁻¹ᵁ U → (fun j => valAt' (1 : Over.mk x ⟶ G).left U (f j)) ∈ O → (∀ a : Over.mk x ⟶ G, ⊤ ≤ (show Sp ℂ ⟶ G.left from a.left) ⁻¹ᵁ U → (fun j => valAt' a.left U (f j)) ∈ O → a ∈ H) → H = ⊤) :
    ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] (σ : k →+* ℂ) (X : Scheme.{0}) (s : X ⟶ Sp k) (G : Over X) [GrpObj G] (g m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (y : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ X)) (b₀ : Fin m → ℂ) (e₀ : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk (y b₀) ⟶ G)), LocallyOfFiniteType s → IsAbelianScheme G → SmoothOfRelativeDimension g G.hom → IsOpen B → b₀ ∈ B → (∀ b ∈ B, y b ≫ s = spMap σ) → IsHolTo B y → ((∀ z w, e₀ (z + w) = e₀ z * e₀ w) ∧ Function.Surjective e₀ ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => (e₀ z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt (y b₀) ⟶ G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e₀ z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt (y b₀) ⟶ G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = (e₀ 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e₀ z).left) w v.left)) → ∃ (B' : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (e : ∀ b, (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk (y b) ⟶ G)) (r : ℝ), IsOpen B' ∧ b₀ ∈ B' ∧ B' ⊆ B ∧ e b₀ = e₀ ∧ 0 < r ∧ IsHolTo (B' ×ˢ Set.univ) (fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => (e bz.1 bz.2).left) ∧ (∀ b ∈ B', ((∀ z w, (e b) (z + w) = (e b) z * (e b) w) ∧ Function.Surjective (e b) ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => ((e b) z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = ((e b) 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left)) ∧ ∀ z, ‖z‖ < r → e b z = 1 → z = 0) ∧ ∀ (n : ℕ) (W : Set (Fin n → ℂ)) (β : (Fin n → ℂ) → (Fin m → ℂ)) (x : ∀ w, Over.mk (y (β w)) ⟶ G) (w₀ : Fin n → ℂ) (z₀ : Fin g → ℂ), IsOpen W → w₀ ∈ W → DifferentiableOn ℂ β W → Set.MapsTo β W B' → IsHolTo W (fun w => (x w).left) → e (β w₀) z₀ = x w₀ → ∃ ζ : (Fin n → ℂ) → (Fin g → ℂ), ζ w₀ = z₀ ∧ AnalyticAt ℂ ζ w₀ ∧ ∀ᶠ w in nhds w₀, e (β w) (ζ w) = x w := by
  intro k _ σ₀ X s₀ G _ g m B y b₀ e₀ _ hab hsm hB hb₀ _ hy ⟨he₀add, _, he₀hol, _, he₀tan⟩
  have := hab.comm
  have hy₀ : HolAt y b₀ := ⟨B, hB, hb₀, hy⟩
  obtain ⟨U, t, n, f, N, O, σ, hN, hO, hU, hO₀, hσ, hσN, hσinv, hunr⟩ :=
    hchart m g G.left X G.hom B y b₀ (pt (1 : Over.mk (y b₀) ⟶ G)) hsm hB hb₀ hy (Over.w _)
  obtain ⟨s, hs1, hs2, hs3⟩ := exists_centred_chart hy₀ hN hU hO₀ hσ hσN hσinv
  obtain ⟨B', e, r, Q, L, hB', hb', hB'B, hr, hQ, hadd, hfib, hinv⟩ :=
    exists_global_exp hexp (hB.mem_nhds hb₀) hy₀ hO hU hO₀ hs1 hs2 hs3
  have hzero : ∀ {x : Sp ℂ ⟶ X} {u : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk x ⟶ G)}, (∀ z w, u (z + w) = u z * u w) → u 0 = 1 :=
    fun {x u} h => by
      have := h 0 0
      rw [add_zero] at this
      exact mul_eq_left.1 this.symm
  have he₀0 : e₀ 0 = 1 := hzero he₀add
  have he₀U : ⊤ ≤ pt (e₀ 0) ⁻¹ᵁ U := by rw [he₀0]; exact hU
  have hinj := ((htan X G (y b₀) g e₀ he₀hol).2 U t he₀U hunr).1 he₀tan
  set T₀ : (Fin g → ℂ) →L[ℂ] (Fin g → ℂ) := fderiv ℂ (fun z => crd U t (pt (e₀ z))) 0 with hT₀
  have hsurj : Function.Surjective T₀ :=
    (LinearMap.injective_iff_surjective (f := (T₀ : (Fin g → ℂ) →ₗ[ℂ] (Fin g → ℂ)))).1 hinj
  let T : (Fin g → ℂ) ≃L[ℂ] (Fin g → ℂ) := ContinuousLinearEquiv.ofBijective T₀ (LinearMap.ker_eq_bot.mpr hinj)
    (LinearMap.range_eq_top.mpr hsurj)
  have hT : ∀ z, T z = T₀ z := fun _ => rfl
  have hhol : ∀ b ∈ B', ∀ z, HolAt (fun q : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => pt (e q.1 (T₀ q.2))) (b, z) :=
    fun b hb z => by
      have := HolAt.comp (φ := fun q : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => (q.1, T₀ q.2)) (v₀ := (b, z))
        ((hfib b hb).1 (T₀ z)) (analyticAt_fst.prod ((T₀.analyticAt _).comp analyticAt_snd))
      exact this
  refine ⟨B', fun b z => e b (T₀ z), r / (‖T₀‖ + 1), hB', hb', hB'B, ?_, by positivity,
    isHolTo_of_holAt (hB'.prod isOpen_univ) fun q hq => hhol q.1 hq.1 q.2, fun b hb => ?_, ?_⟩
  ·
    obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7, h8⟩ := hfib b₀ hb'
    obtain ⟨ζ, hζ0, hζ, hζe⟩ := exists_lift hO hQ hadd hinv (β := fun _ : Fin g → ℂ => b₀) (x := e₀) (w₀ := 0)
      (z₀ := 0) analyticAt_const ⟨Set.univ, isOpen_univ, trivial, he₀hol⟩ ((hzero (hadd b₀)).trans he₀0.symm)
      (h1 0) h2 h3 h4 h6 h7
    have hd : HasFDerivAt ζ T₀ 0 := by
      have k1 := HasFDerivAt.comp (f := ζ) (0 : Fin g → ℂ) (hζ0.symm ▸ h5) hζ.differentiableAt.hasFDerivAt
      have k2 : HasFDerivAt (fun z => crd U t (pt (e₀ z))) _ 0 := k1.congr_of_eventuallyEq
        (hζe.mono fun z hz => by simp only [Function.comp, hz])
      rw [hT₀, k2.fderiv, ContinuousLinearMap.id_comp]
      exact hζ.differentiableAt.hasFDerivAt
    have tζ : Tendsto ζ (𝓝 0) (𝓝 0) := by simpa only [ContinuousAt, hζ0] using hζ.continuousAt
    have t1 : Tendsto (fun p : (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => p.1) (𝓝 0) (𝓝 0) := continuous_fst.tendsto 0
    have t2 : Tendsto (fun p : (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => p.2) (𝓝 0) (𝓝 0) := continuous_snd.tendsto 0
    have t3 : Tendsto (fun p : (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => p.1 + p.2) (𝓝 0) (𝓝 0) := by simpa using t1.add t2
    have t4 : Tendsto (fun p : (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => ζ (p.1 + p.2) - (ζ p.1 + ζ p.2)) (𝓝 0) (𝓝 0) := by
      simpa using (tζ.comp t3).sub ((tζ.comp t1).add (tζ.comp t2))
    have hζT := eventuallyEq_of_additive_of_hasFDerivAt hd (by
      filter_upwards [t1.eventually hζe, t2.eventually hζe, t3.eventually hζe,
        t4.eventually (Metric.ball_mem_nhds 0 hr)] with p k1 k2 k3 k4
      refine sub_eq_zero.1 (h8 _ (mem_ball_zero_iff.1 k4) ?_)
      have k5 := hadd b₀ (ζ (p.1 + p.2) - (ζ p.1 + ζ p.2)) (ζ p.1 + ζ p.2)
      rw [sub_add_cancel, hadd b₀ (ζ p.1), k1, k2, k3, he₀add] at k5
      exact mul_eq_right.1 k5.symm)
    have key := addMonoidHom_eq_of_eventuallyEq
      (e := AddMonoidHom.mk' (fun z => Additive.ofMul (e b₀ (T₀ z))) fun z w => by rw [map_add, hadd]; rfl)
      (e' := AddMonoidHom.mk' (fun z => Additive.ofMul (e₀ z)) fun z w => by rw [he₀add]; rfl) (by
        filter_upwards [hζT, hζe] with z k1 k2
        exact congrArg Additive.ofMul (by rw [← k1]; exact k2))
    exact funext fun z => congrArg Additive.toMul (DFunLike.congr_fun key z)
  · obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7, h8⟩ := hfib b hb
    have hH : IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => pt (e b (T₀ z))) := isHolTo_of_holAt isOpen_univ fun z _ => by
      have := HolAt.comp (φ := fun z : Fin g → ℂ => (b, z)) (v₀ := z) (hhol b hb z)
        (analyticAt_const.prod analyticAt_id)
      exact this
    have hadd' : ∀ z w, e b (T₀ (z + w)) = e b (T₀ z) * e b (T₀ w) := fun z w => by rw [map_add, hadd]
    obtain ⟨k1, k2⟩ := htan X G (y b) g (fun z => e b (T₀ z)) hH
    refine ⟨⟨hadd', ?_, hH, k1, (k2 U t ?_ hunr).2 ?_⟩, fun z hz h => ?_⟩
    · refine Function.Surjective.comp (g := e b) ?_ hsurj
      let ε : (Fin g → ℂ) →+ Additive (Over.mk (y b) ⟶ G) :=
