        AddMonoidHom.mk' (fun z => Additive.ofMul (e b z)) fun z w => by rw [hadd]; rfl
      have hc : Continuous fun u : Fin n ⊕ Fin g → ℂ => (b, fun j => u (Sum.inl j)) := by fun_prop
      have hc' : Continuous fun u : Fin n ⊕ Fin g → ℂ =>
        (b, (fun j => u (Sum.inr j)) - crd U t (pt (1 : Over.mk (y b) ⟶ G))) := by fun_prop
      have := hsub X G (y b) (AddSubgroup.toSubgroup' ε.range) U (Fin n ⊕ Fin g) (Sum.elim f t) _ hab.smooth
        hab.geomConnected ((hO.preimage hc).inter (hQ.preimage hc')) h2
        ⟨h3, by simpa only [Set.mem_preimage, Sum.elim_inr, sub_self] using h4⟩
        fun a ha hb => ⟨_, congrArg Additive.ofMul (hinv b a ha hb.1 hb.2)⟩
      intro a
      obtain ⟨z, hz⟩ : a ∈ AddSubgroup.toSubgroup' ε.range := this ▸ Subgroup.mem_top a
      exact ⟨z, congrArg Additive.toMul hz⟩
    · rw [hzero (u := fun z => e b (T₀ z)) hadd']; exact h2
    · have := HasFDerivAt.comp (f := T₀) (0 : Fin g → ℂ) (by rw [map_zero]; exact h5) T₀.hasFDerivAt
      have k3 : fderiv ℂ (fun z => crd U t (pt (e b (T₀ z)))) 0 = _ := this.fderiv
      rw [ContinuousLinearMap.id_comp] at k3
      exact k3.symm ▸ hinj
    · refine hinj ((h8 _ ((T₀.le_opNorm z).trans_lt ?_) h).trans (map_zero T₀).symm)
      rw [lt_div_iff₀ (by positivity)] at hz
      nlinarith [norm_nonneg z]
  · intro j W β x w₀ z₀ hW hw₀ hβ hβW hx h0
    obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7, h8⟩ := hfib _ (hβW hw₀)
    obtain ⟨ζ, hζ0, hζ, hζe⟩ := exists_lift hO hQ hadd hinv (x := x)
      (SCV.analyticAt_of_differentiableOn hβ (hW.mem_nhds hw₀)) ⟨W, hW, hw₀, hx⟩ h0 (h1 _) h2 h3 h4 h6 h7
    refine ⟨fun w => T.symm (ζ w), by simp only [hζ0, ← hT, T.symm_apply_apply], (T.symm.analyticAt _).comp hζ, ?_⟩
    filter_upwards [hζe] with w hw
    rw [← hT, T.apply_symm_apply, hw]

theorem exists_relative_exp :
    ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] (σ : k →+* ℂ) (X : Scheme.{0}) (s : X ⟶ Sp k) (G : Over X) [GrpObj G] (g m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (y : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ X)) (b₀ : Fin m → ℂ) (e₀ : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk (y b₀) ⟶ G)), LocallyOfFiniteType s → IsAbelianScheme G → SmoothOfRelativeDimension g G.hom → IsOpen B → b₀ ∈ B → (∀ b ∈ B, y b ≫ s = spMap σ) → IsHolTo B y → ((∀ z w, e₀ (z + w) = e₀ z * e₀ w) ∧ Function.Surjective e₀ ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => (e₀ z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt (y b₀) ⟶ G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e₀ z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt (y b₀) ⟶ G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = (e₀ 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e₀ z).left) w v.left)) → ∃ (B' : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (e : ∀ b, (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk (y b) ⟶ G)) (r : ℝ), IsOpen B' ∧ b₀ ∈ B' ∧ B' ⊆ B ∧ e b₀ = e₀ ∧ 0 < r ∧ IsHolTo (B' ×ˢ Set.univ) (fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => (e bz.1 bz.2).left) ∧ (∀ b ∈ B', ((∀ z w, (e b) (z + w) = (e b) z * (e b) w) ∧ Function.Surjective (e b) ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => ((e b) z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = ((e b) 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left)) ∧ ∀ z, ‖z‖ < r → e b z = 1 → z = 0) ∧ ∀ (n : ℕ) (W : Set (Fin n → ℂ)) (β : (Fin n → ℂ) → (Fin m → ℂ)) (x : ∀ w, Over.mk (y (β w)) ⟶ G) (w₀ : Fin n → ℂ) (z₀ : Fin g → ℂ), IsOpen W → w₀ ∈ W → DifferentiableOn ℂ β W → Set.MapsTo β W B' → IsHolTo W (fun w => (x w).left) → e (β w₀) z₀ = x w₀ → ∃ ζ : (Fin n → ℂ) → (Fin g → ℂ), ζ w₀ = z₀ ∧ AnalyticAt ℂ ζ w₀ ∧ ∀ᶠ w in nhds w₀, e (β w) (ζ w) = x w :=
  exists_relative_exp_of_parts (RelChart.exists_chart_with_parameters EtaleFamily.exists_holomorphic_sections)
    vertical_derivative_in_coordinates exists_param_exp_log_pi OpenSubgroupPts.subgroup_eq_top_of_nhds_one

end GlobalTheory.RelExp


[HUF_SRC_LATTICE_CONTINUATION] Original full-kernel lattice continuation conclusion
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/LatContMapContinuous.lean#L60-L169
theorem latContinuation : ∀ (La : Type) [AddCommGroup La] [Module.Free ℤ La] [Module.Finite ℤ La] (g m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (b₀ : Fin m → ℂ) (K : (Fin m → ℂ) → AddSubgroup (Fin g → ℂ)) (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → (La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))) (r : ℝ), IsOpen B → b₀ ∈ B → 0 < r → (∀ b ∈ B, ∀ z ∈ K b, ‖z‖ < r → z = 0) → (∀ (b : ℕ → (Fin m → ℂ)) (z : ℕ → (Fin g → ℂ)) (z₀ : Fin g → ℂ), Filter.Tendsto b Filter.atTop (nhds b₀) → Filter.Tendsto z Filter.atTop (nhds z₀) → (∀ i, z i ∈ K (b i)) → z₀ ∈ K b₀) → (∀ x, ContinuousAt (fun b => P b x) b₀) → (∀ b ∈ B, (P b).range ≤ K b) → (P b₀).range = K b₀ → Function.Injective (P b₀) → Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (P b₀)) = ⊤ → ∀ᶠ b in nhds b₀, (P b).range = K b ∧ Function.Injective (P b) ∧ Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (P b)) = ⊤ ∧ DiscreteTopology (P b).range := by
  intro La _ _ _ g m B b₀ K P r hB hb₀ hr hdisc hclosed hcont hle heq hinj hspan
  obtain ⟨J, _, ⟨e⟩⟩ : ∃ (J : Type) (_ : Fintype J), Nonempty (Module.Basis J ℤ La) :=
    ⟨_, inferInstance, ⟨Module.Free.chooseBasis ℤ La⟩⟩
  have hrepr : ∀ b (k : J → ℤ), P b (e.equivFun.symm k) = ∑ j, ((k j : ℤ) : ℝ) • P b (e j) := by
    intro b k
    rw [← latContMap_repr e (P b) k, latContMap_apply]
  have hcoord : ∀ b x, P b x = ∑ j, ((e.equivFun x j : ℤ) : ℝ) • P b (e j) := by
    intro b x
    rw [← hrepr]
    simp
  have hdiscR : ∀ b ∈ B, DiscreteTopology (P b).range := fun b hb =>
    latCont_discrete _ r hr (fun z hz hzr => hdisc b hb z (hle b hb hz) hzr)
  have hcard : Fintype.card J = Module.finrank ℝ (Fin g → ℂ) := by
    rw [← Module.finrank_fintype_fun_eq_card ℝ]
    exact latCont_card e (P b₀) (hdiscR b₀ hb₀) hinj hspan
  have hspan0 : ⊤ ≤ Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun j => P b₀ (e j))) := by
    rw [← hspan]
    apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨x, rfl⟩
    rw [hcoord]
    exact Submodule.sum_mem _ (fun j _ => Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (Submodule.subset_span ⟨j, rfl⟩))
  have hli0 : LinearIndependent ℝ (fun j => P b₀ (e j)) :=
    linearIndependent_of_top_le_span_of_card_eq_finrank hspan0 hcard
  have hvc : ContinuousAt (fun b => fun j => P b (e j)) b₀ :=
    continuousAt_pi.mpr (fun j => hcont (e j))
  have hev_li : ∀ᶠ b in nhds b₀, LinearIndependent ℝ (fun j => P b (e j)) :=
    hvc.tendsto.eventually hli0.eventually
  have hev_B : ∀ᶠ b in nhds b₀, b ∈ B := hB.mem_nhds hb₀
  have hspanv : ∀ b, LinearIndependent ℝ (fun j => P b (e j)) →
      Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun j => P b (e j))) = ⊤ :=
    fun b hb => hb.span_eq_top_of_card_eq_finrank' hcard
  have hsurj : ∀ b, LinearIndependent ℝ (fun j => P b (e j)) →
      ∀ z, ∃ c : J → ℝ, ∑ j, c j • P b (e j) = z := by
    intro b hb z
    have hz : z ∈ Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun j => P b (e j))) := by
      rw [hspanv b hb]
      trivial
    exact (Submodule.mem_span_range_iff_exists_fun ℝ).mp hz
  have hev_eq : ∀ᶠ b in nhds b₀, ∀ z ∈ K b, z ∈ (P b).range := by
    by_contra hne
    rw [Filter.not_eventually] at hne
    have hfr : ∃ᶠ b in nhds b₀, b ∈ B ∧ LinearIndependent ℝ (fun j => P b (e j)) ∧
        ∃ z ∈ K b, z ∉ (P b).range := by
      refine (hne.and_eventually (hev_B.and hev_li)).mono ?_
      rintro b ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩
      push Not at h1
      exact ⟨h2, h3, h1⟩
    obtain ⟨bs, hbs, hprop⟩ := Filter.exists_seq_forall_of_frequently hfr
    choose hbsB hbsli z hzK hzR using hprop
    choose c hc using fun i => hsurj (bs i) (hbsli i) (z i)
    have hshift : ∀ i (k : J → ℤ),
        (∑ j, (Int.fract (c i j) - (k j : ℝ)) • P (bs i) (e j)) ∈ K (bs i) ∧
        (∑ j, (Int.fract (c i j) - (k j : ℝ)) • P (bs i) (e j)) ∉ (P (bs i)).range := by
      intro i k
      have e1 : ∑ j, (Int.fract (c i j) - (k j : ℝ)) • P (bs i) (e j)
          = z i - P (bs i) (e.equivFun.symm (fun j => ⌊c i j⌋ + k j)) := by
        rw [hrepr, ← hc i, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
        refine Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun j _ => ?_)
        rw [← sub_smul]
        congr 1
        push_cast
        rw [← Int.self_sub_floor]
        ring
      rw [e1]
      have hmem : P (bs i) (e.equivFun.symm (fun j => ⌊c i j⌋ + k j)) ∈ (P (bs i)).range :=
        ⟨_, rfl⟩
      refine ⟨sub_mem (hzK i) (hle _ (hbsB i) hmem), fun h => hzR i ?_⟩
      have := add_mem h hmem
      rwa [sub_add_cancel] at this
    have hIcc : ∀ i, (fun j => Int.fract (c i j)) ∈ Set.Icc (0 : J → ℝ) 1 := fun i =>
      ⟨fun j => Int.fract_nonneg _, fun j => (Int.fract_lt_one _).le⟩
    obtain ⟨a, -, φ, hφ, ha⟩ := (isCompact_Icc (a := (0 : J → ℝ)) (b := 1)).tendsto_subseq hIcc
    have hbφ : Filter.Tendsto (bs ∘ φ) Filter.atTop (nhds b₀) := hbs.comp hφ.tendsto_atTop
    have hvφ : ∀ j, Filter.Tendsto (fun i => P (bs (φ i)) (e j)) Filter.atTop
        (nhds (P b₀ (e j))) := fun j => (hcont (e j)).tendsto.comp hbφ
    have haj : ∀ j, Filter.Tendsto (fun i => Int.fract (c (φ i) j)) Filter.atTop (nhds (a j)) :=
      fun j => ((continuous_apply j).tendsto a).comp ha
    have hlim : ∀ k : J → ℤ, Filter.Tendsto
        (fun i => ∑ j, (Int.fract (c (φ i) j) - (k j : ℝ)) • P (bs (φ i)) (e j)) Filter.atTop
        (nhds (∑ j, (a j - (k j : ℝ)) • P b₀ (e j))) := fun k =>
      tendsto_finsetSum _ (fun j _ => ((haj j).sub_const _).smul (hvφ j))
    have hz0 : (∑ j, (a j - ((0 : J → ℤ) j : ℝ)) • P b₀ (e j)) ∈ K b₀ :=
      hclosed (bs ∘ φ) _ _ hbφ (hlim 0) (fun i => (hshift (φ i) 0).1)
    simp only [Pi.zero_apply, Int.cast_zero, sub_zero] at hz0
    rw [← heq] at hz0
    obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := AddMonoidHom.mem_range.mp hz0
    have hzero : ∑ j, (a j - ((e.equivFun x j : ℤ) : ℝ)) • P b₀ (e j) = 0 := by
      simp only [sub_smul, Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
      rw [← hcoord b₀ x, ← hx, sub_self]
    have hlim0 := hlim (e.equivFun x)
    rw [hzero] at hlim0
    obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := (hlim0.eventually (Metric.ball_mem_nhds (0 : Fin g → ℂ) hr)).exists
    rw [dist_zero_right] at hi
    obtain ⟨hK', hR'⟩ := hshift (φ i) (e.equivFun x)
    exact hR' (by rw [hdisc _ (hbsB (φ i)) _ hK' hi]; exact zero_mem _)
  filter_upwards [hev_B, hev_li, hev_eq] with b hb hli hrange
  refine ⟨le_antisymm (hle b hb) (fun z hz => hrange z hz), ?_, ?_, hdiscR b hb⟩
  · rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
    intro x hx
    rw [hcoord] at hx
    have h0 := (Fintype.linearIndependent_iff.mp hli) _ hx
    have h1 : e.equivFun x = 0 := funext fun j => by simpa using h0 j
    simpa using congrArg e.equivFun.symm h1
  · rw [eq_top_iff, ← hspanv b hli]
    apply Submodule.span_mono
    rintro _ ⟨j, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨e j, rfl⟩

end GlobalTheory


[HUF_SRC_LOCAL_LIFT] Full prescribed-initial-period local lift with one open neighborhood
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/ExistsLocalPeriodLift.lean#L21-L27
theorem exists_local_period_lift :
    ThetaUnifAV → Out.Laws → ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (Xo : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) (g : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)), 1 ≤ D.p + D.q → σ (baseKA l).ρ = H.rt0 → IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → ∀ (D' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (y : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X)) (b₀ : Fin m → ℂ) (P₀ : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) (e₀ : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk (y b₀) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G)), IsOpen B → b₀ ∈ B → (∀ b ∈ B, y b ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ) → IsHolTo B y → ((y b₀) ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ IsPeriod D' ty P₀ ∧ ((∀ z w, e₀ (z + w) = e₀ z * e₀ w) ∧ Function.Surjective e₀ ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => (e₀ z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt (y b₀) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e₀ z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt (y b₀) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = (e₀ 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e₀ z).left) w v.left)) ∧ (∀ z, e₀ z = 1 ↔ z ∈ (P₀).range) ∧ (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, e₀ (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = e₀ z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a) ∧ (∀ (k : ℕ) (a a₂ : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k → (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a a₂ : ℂ) / k) → Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k (e₀ ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P₀ a)) (e₀ ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P₀ a₂)) (unitOn c)) ∧ (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = e₀ ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • P₀ (t a))) ∧ ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = e₀ ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • P₀ (t a))) → ∃ (B' : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → (D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))) (e : ∀ b, (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G)), IsOpen B' ∧ b₀ ∈ B' ∧ B' ⊆ B ∧ P b₀ = P₀ ∧ IsHolPeriods D' ty B' P ∧ IsHolTo (B' ×ˢ Set.univ) (fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => (e bz.1 bz.2).left) ∧ ∀ b ∈ B', ((y b) ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ IsPeriod D' ty (P b) ∧ ((∀ z w, (e b) (z + w) = (e b) z * (e b) w) ∧ Function.Surjective (e b) ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => ((e b) z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = ((e b) 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left)) ∧ (∀ z, (e b) z = 1 ↔ z ∈ ((P b)).range) ∧ (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, (e b) (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = (e b) z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a) ∧ (∀ (k : ℕ) (a a₂ : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k → (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a a₂ : ℂ) / k) → Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k ((e b) ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) a)) ((e b) ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) a₂)) (unitOn c)) ∧ (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = (e b) ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) (t a))) ∧ ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = (e b) ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) (t a))) :=
  fun hT hL => GlobalTheory.T.holomorphic_map_moduli_lifts_locally_to_periods hT hL isReduced_isNormalScheme_isLocallyNoetherian_of_smooth_over_field
    obj_isAbelianScheme_dim_rigidified_sym obj_pull RelExp.exists_relative_exp latContinuation
    holomorphic_diagonalisation (torsion_eq_and_weilIs_of_isHolTo hL)

end GlobalTheory


[HUF_SRC_PERIOD_COORDINATES] Exact PeriodCoord.exists_period_coordinates with finite period set, radius and dimension
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/ExistsFinsetOfMemNhds.lean#L325-L398
theorem exists_period_coordinates : ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (g : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)) (P₀ : D.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)), IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → IsPeriod D ty P₀ → ∃ (Z : (D.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) → (Fin D.dim → ℂ)) (s : Finset D.La) (ε : ℝ), 0 < ε ∧ (∀ (m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → (D.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))), IsOpen B → IsHolPeriods D ty B P → (∀ b ∈ B, ∀ x ∈ s, ‖P b x - P₀ x‖ < ε) → DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun b => Z (P b)) B) ∧ ∀ P P' : D.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ), IsPeriod D ty P → IsPeriod D ty P' → (∀ x ∈ s, ‖P x - P₀ x‖ < ε) → (∀ x ∈ s, ‖P' x - P₀ x‖ < ε) → Z P = Z P' → ∃ h : (Fin g → ℂ) ≃ₗ[ℂ] (Fin g → ℂ), (∀ a, P' a = h (P a)) ∧ ∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, h (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = fun k => ty k (a : E) * h z k := by
  intro E _ _ _ H D g ty P₀ hty hP₀
  obtain ⟨α, β, hαβ⟩ := exists_colMat hP₀
  obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := exists_index hty
  obtain ⟨M₀, -, hpos₀, hM₀⟩ := hP₀.2.2.2.2
  have hA₀ : colMat α P₀ = 1 := by
    refine (realMap_inj (B := colMat β P₀) (B' := 0) (ext_period hP₀ fun x => ?_)).1
    rw [← hαβ P₀ hP₀]
    simp [realMap_apply]
  have hcont : Continuous fun f : D.La → Fin g → ℂ => (colMat α f).det := by
    refine Continuous.matrix_det (continuous_matrix fun i j => ?_)
    simp only [colMat, of_apply]
    fun_prop
  obtain ⟨s, ε, hε, hs⟩ := exists_finset_of_mem_nhds
    ((hcont.continuousAt (x := ⇑P₀)).eventually_ne (show (colMat α P₀).det ≠ 0 by simp [hA₀]))
  refine ⟨fun P k => (((colMat α P)⁻¹ * colMat β P)ᵀ * M₀) (e k).1 (e k).2, s, ε, hε, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro m B P _ hP hnear
    have hc : ∀ (γ : Fin g → (D.La →₀ ℂ)) i j, DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun b => colMat γ (P b) i j) B :=
      fun γ i j => by
        simp only [colMat, of_apply]
        exact DifferentiableOn.fun_sum fun x _ => (differentiableOn_pi.1 (hP.2 x) i).const_mul _
    have hinv := RiemannRel.differentiableOn_left_inverse (fun b => colMat α (P b)) (fun b => (colMat α (P b))⁻¹)
      (hc α) (fun b hb => nonsing_inv_mul _ (isUnit_iff_ne_zero.2 (hs _ (hnear b hb))))
    refine differentiableOn_pi.2 fun k => ?_
    simp only [mul_apply, transpose_apply]
    exact DifferentiableOn.fun_sum fun l _ =>
      (DifferentiableOn.fun_sum fun n _ => (hinv _ _).mul (hc β _ _)).mul_const _
  · intro P P' hP hP' hn hn' hZ
    have hA : (colMat α P).det ≠ 0 := hs _ hn
    have hA' : (colMat α P').det ≠ 0 := hs _ hn'
    have h1 := hαβ P hP
    have h1' := hαβ P' hP'
    have h2 := symm_and_support hP₀ hP hM₀ h1 hA
    have h2' := symm_and_support hP₀ hP' hM₀ h1' hA'
    beta_reduce at hZ
    clear hs hαβ
    generalize colMat α P = A, colMat β P = B, colMat α P' = A', colMat β P' = B' at *
    have hu := isUnit_iff_ne_zero.2 hA
    have hu' := isUnit_iff_ne_zero.2 hA'
    have h3 : ∀ i j, ty i (H.rt : E) = H.rt0 → ((A⁻¹ * B)ᵀ * M₀) i j = ((A'⁻¹ * B')ᵀ * M₀) i j := by
      intro i j hi
      by_cases hj : ty j = ComplexEmbedding.conjugate (ty i)
      · obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := he i j hi hj
        simpa only [hk] using congrFun hZ k
      · rw [(h2 i j).2 hj, (h2' i j).2 hj]
    have h4 : (A⁻¹ * B)ᵀ * M₀ = (A'⁻¹ * B')ᵀ * M₀ := by
      ext i j
      by_cases hi : ty i (H.rt : E) = H.rt0
      · exact h3 i j hi
      by_cases hj : ty j = ComplexEmbedding.conjugate (ty i)
      · rw [(h2 i j).1, (h2' i j).1]
        exact h3 j i (by rw [hj, conjugate_mem_iff]; exact hi)
      · rw [(h2 i j).2 hj, (h2' i j).2 hj]
    have h5 : A⁻¹ * B = A'⁻¹ * B' := by
      have hM := isUnit_iff_ne_zero.2 (det_ne_zero_of_pos hpos₀)
      have := congrArg (· * M₀⁻¹) h4
      simp only [mul_nonsing_inv_cancel_right _ _ hM] at this
      exact transpose_injective this
    have hN : IsUnit (A' * A⁻¹).det := by
      rw [det_mul]; exact hu'.mul (isUnit_nonsing_inv_det _ hu)
    have h6 : ∀ x, P' x = (A' * A⁻¹) *ᵥ P x := fun x => by
      rw [h1, h1', realMap_apply, realMap_apply, mulVec_add, mulVec_mulVec, mulVec_mulVec,
        nonsing_inv_mul_cancel_right _ _ hu, Matrix.mul_assoc, h5, mul_nonsing_inv_cancel_left _ _ hu']
    refine ⟨Matrix.toLinearEquiv' _ (invertibleOfIsUnitDet _ hN), h6, fun a z => ?_⟩
    have h7 := (comm_of_realMap hP hP' (A := A' * A⁻¹) (B := 0) (fun x => by simp [realMap_apply, h6]) a).1
    have h8 : ∀ z : Fin g → ℂ, (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = actMat ty a *ᵥ z := fun z => by
      funext k; rw [actMat, mulVec_diagonal]
    rw [h8, h8]
    change (A' * A⁻¹) *ᵥ _ = _ *ᵥ ((A' * A⁻¹) *ᵥ z)
    rw [mulVec_mulVec, mulVec_mulVec, h7]

end

end GlobalTheory.PeriodCoord


[HUF_SRC_EXPONENTIAL_UNIQUE] Actual half-level isometry rigidity and exp_eq_of_periods_of_marking
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/ExpEqOfPeriodsOfMarking.lean#L185-L321
theorem eq_self_of_isometry (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) {g : ℕ} {ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)}
    {P : D.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)} (hP : IsPeriod D ty P) (h : (Fin g → ℂ) →L[ℝ] (Fin g → ℂ))
    (hI : ∀ z, h (Complex.I • z) = Complex.I • h z) (φ : D.La → D.La) (hφ : ∀ a, P (φ a) = h (P a))
    (hEF : ∀ a b, D.EF (φ a) (φ b) = D.EF a b) (hO : ∀ (c : 𝓞 E) a, φ (c • a) = c • φ a)
    (hM : ∀ b ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), ∃ z, φ b = b + l.M • z)
    (z : Fin g → ℂ) : h z = z := by
  obtain ⟨-, hinj, hd, hspan, Hm, -, hpos, hIm⟩ := hP
  let σ : Module.End ℤ D.La := (AddMonoidHom.mk' φ fun a b => hinj (by simp only [hφ, map_add])).toIntLinearMap
  have hσ : ∀ a, σ a = φ a := fun _ => rfl
  have := D.fin
  have : Module.Finite ℤ D.La := Module.Finite.trans (𝓞 E) D.La
  have : Module.IsTorsionFree ℤ D.La :=
    Function.Injective.moduleIsTorsionFree P hinj fun r m => map_zsmul P r m
  have : Module.Free ℤ D.La := Module.free_of_finite_type_torsion_free'
  let B : (Fin g → ℂ) →ₗ[ℝ] (Fin g → ℂ) →ₗ[ℝ] ℝ := LinearMap.mk₂ ℝ (fun u v => (hermForm Hm u v).im)
    (fun a b c => by simp [hermForm]) (fun r a b => by simp [hermForm])
    (fun a b c => by simp [hermForm, Matrix.mulVec_add]) (fun r a b => by simp [hermForm, Matrix.mulVec_smul])
  have hB : B.compl₁₂ h.toLinearMap h.toLinearMap = B := by
    refine LinearMap.ext_on_range hspan fun a => LinearMap.ext_on_range hspan fun b => ?_
    show (hermForm Hm (h (P a)) (h (P b))).im = (hermForm Hm (P a) (P b)).im
    rw [← hφ, ← hφ, hIm, hIm, hEF]
  have hq : ∀ u, (hermForm Hm u u).re = B (Complex.I • u) u := fun u => by
    show _ = (hermForm Hm (Complex.I • u) u).im
    simp [hermForm]
  have hbd := isBounded_sublevel (q := fun u => (hermForm Hm u u).re)
    (by unfold hermForm; fun_prop) (fun r u => by
      simp only [hq]
      rw [smul_comm, map_smul, map_smul, LinearMap.smul_apply, smul_smul, sq, smul_eq_mul]) hpos
  have key : σ = 1 := by
    refine end_eq_one_of_invariant D.La σ ((injective_iff_map_eq_zero σ).2 fun a ha => D.unimod.1 ?_)
      (fun a => (hermForm Hm (P a) (P a)).re) (fun c => ?_) (fun a => ?_) l.M l.three_le fun a => ?_
    · ext b
      rw [← hEF, ← hσ, ha, map_zero]
      rfl
    · refine Set.Finite.of_finite_image (f := P) ((Metric.finite_isBounded_inter_isClosed
        (s := P.range) DiscreteTopology.isDiscrete (hbd c) AddSubgroup.isClosed_of_discrete).subset ?_) hinj.injOn
      rintro _ ⟨a, ha, rfl⟩
      exact ⟨ha, a, rfl⟩
    · simp only [hq, hσ, hφ, ← hI]
      exact LinearMap.congr_fun₂ hB _ _
    · obtain ⟨i, hi, j, hj, hij⟩ := Submodule.mem_sup.mp ((Ideal.eq_top_iff_one _).mp l.coprime)
      obtain ⟨z₁, hz₁⟩ := hM (j • a) (Submodule.smul_mem_smul hj trivial)
      obtain ⟨z₂, hz₂⟩ := ObjRigid.exists_eq_nsmul D l.M (i • (φ a - a)) fun b => by
        obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ := hM (H.bar i • b) (Submodule.smul_mem_smul (Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ hi) trivial)
        have h1 := hEF a (H.bar i • b)
        rw [hw, map_add, map_nsmul, nsmul_eq_mul] at h1
        rw [D.adj, map_sub, LinearMap.sub_apply]
        exact ⟨-D.EF (φ a) w, by linarith⟩
      refine ⟨z₂ + z₁, ?_⟩
      rw [hO] at hz₁
      rw [hσ, smul_add, ← hz₂, ← sub_eq_iff_eq_add'.2 hz₁, ← smul_sub, ← add_smul, hij, one_smul, add_sub_cancel]
  have : h.toLinearMap = LinearMap.id := LinearMap.ext_on_range hspan fun a => by
    show h (P a) = P a
    rw [← hφ, ← hσ, key]
    rfl
  exact LinearMap.congr_fun this z

end GlobalTheory.ExpUnique

namespace GlobalTheory

open AlgebraicGeometry CategoryTheory Limits MonoidalCategory CartesianMonoidalCategory MonObj NumberField
open ReciprocityTheory.ChartRestriction.G ReciprocityTheory.ChartRestriction.G.Semi ReciprocityTheory.ChartRestriction.G.Geo.Tor
open scoped CategoryTheory.Obj TensorProduct
open ExpUnique

theorem exp_eq_of_periods_of_marking : ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (Xo : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) (g : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)), 1 ≤ D.p + D.q → σ (baseKA l).ρ = H.rt0 → IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → ∀ (D' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (P : D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ)) (y : Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X) (e e' : (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G)), (D'.p = D.p ∧ D'.q = D.q ∧ ∃ (f : ℕ → D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (ν : ℕ → ℤ), (∀ n m : ℕ, 0 < n → 0 < m → ∀ a, ∃ e, f (n * m) a = f n a + n • e) ∧ (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → (∀ a, (∃ e, f n a = n • e) ↔ ∃ e, a = n • e) ∧ ∀ a', ∃ a e, a' = f n a + n • e) ∧ (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∀ a a', (n : ℤ) ∣ D'.EF (f n a) (f n a') - ν n * D.EF a a') ∧ σ (baseKA l).ζ = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (ν l.A : ℂ) / l.A) ∧ t = f l.M') → (y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ IsPeriod D' ty P ∧ ((∀ z w, e (z + w) = e z * e w) ∧ Function.Surjective e ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => (e z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = (e 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e z).left) w v.left)) ∧ (∀ z, e z = 1 ↔ z ∈ (P).range) ∧ (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, e (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = e z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a) ∧ (∀ (k : ℕ) (a a₂ : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k → (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a a₂ : ℂ) / k) → Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k (e ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a)) (e ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a₂)) (unitOn c)) ∧ (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = e ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t a))) ∧ ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = e ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t a))) → (y ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ IsPeriod D' ty P ∧ ((∀ z w, e' (z + w) = e' z * e' w) ∧ Function.Surjective e' ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => (e' z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e' z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt y ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = (e' 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (e' z).left) w v.left)) ∧ (∀ z, e' z = 1 ↔ z ∈ (P).range) ∧ (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, e' (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = e' z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a) ∧ (∀ (k : ℕ) (a a₂ : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k → (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a a₂ : ℂ) / k) → Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k (e' ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a)) (e' ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a₂)) (unitOn c)) ∧ (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = e' ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t a))) ∧ ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = e' ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (t a))) → e = e' := by
  rintro E _ _ _ H D l Xo σ g ty - - - D' t P y e e' ⟨-, -, f, ν, -, hf, -, -, rfl⟩
    ⟨-, hP, ⟨add, onto, hol, dv, du⟩, ker, lin, weil, eta, -⟩ ⟨-, -, ⟨add', -, hol', -, -⟩, ker', lin', weil', eta', -⟩
  have hM : 0 < l.M := lt_of_lt_of_le (by norm_num) l.three_le
  have hd : DiscreteTopology P.range := hP.2.2.1
  have : DiscreteTopology P.range.toIntSubmodule := hd
  have : IsZLattice ℝ P.range.toIntSubmodule := ⟨hP.2.2.2.1⟩
  obtain ⟨h, hh⟩ := exists_realLinear_lift P.range.toIntSubmodule add add' onto ker <| by
    filter_upwards [eventually_eq_of_mem_closure hol hol' (by rw [hom_zero add, hom_zero add'])] with p hp hc
    refine Over.OverMorphism.ext (hp (closure_mono (fun r hr => ?_) hc))
    exact congrArg (·.left) (show e' r.1 = e r.2 from hr)
  obtain ⟨v, hv, -⟩ := dv 0
  have hI := map_I_smul hol hol' hv (fun w hw => (du v hv.1).unique hw hv) h fun z => congrArg (·.left) (hh z)
  have hn : ∀ (k : ℕ) z, h ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • z) = (k : ℂ)⁻¹ • h z := fun k z => by
    have : ∀ u : Fin g → ℂ, (k : ℂ)⁻¹ • u = (k : ℝ)⁻¹ • u := fun u => by
      rw [← Complex.coe_smul, Complex.ofReal_inv, Complex.ofReal_natCast]
    rw [this, this, map_smul]
  choose φ hφ using fun a => (ker (h (P a))).1 ((hh _).trans ((ker' _).2 ⟨a, rfl⟩))
  have he' : ∀ (k : ℕ) a, e' ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P a) = e ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • P (φ a)) := fun k a => by rw [← hh, hn, hφ]
  have key := eq_self_of_isometry D' l hP h hI φ hφ (fun a b => ?_) (fun c a => hP.2.1 ?_) fun b hb => ?_
  · exact funext fun z => by rw [← hh, key]
  ·
    rw [← sub_eq_zero]
    refine Int.eq_zero_of_dvd_of_natAbs_lt_natAbs (d := ((D'.EF (φ a) (φ b) - D'.EF a b).natAbs + 1 : ℕ)) ?_
      (by rw [Int.natAbs_natCast]; exact Nat.lt_succ_self _)
    generalize hk : (D'.EF (φ a) (φ b) - D'.EF a b).natAbs + 1 = k
    have hk0 : 0 < k := hk ▸ Nat.succ_pos _
    have w1 := weil k (φ a) (φ b) (Units.mk0 _ (Complex.exp_ne_zero _)) hk0 rfl
    have w2 := weil' k a b (Units.mk0 _ (Complex.exp_ne_zero _)) hk0 rfl
    rw [he', he'] at w2
    have w3 := congrArg Units.val (weilIs_unique_fieldPoint _ _ _ Xo.univ.t.PP.isLB ℂ y _ _ _ _ _ w1 w2)
    obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := Complex.exp_eq_exp_iff_exists_int.1 w3
    refine ⟨-n, Int.cast_injective (α := ℂ) ?_⟩
    have hk1 : (k : ℂ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 hk0.ne'
    have h2 := Complex.two_pi_I_ne_zero
    generalize 2 * (Real.pi : ℂ) * Complex.I = c at hn h2
    field_simp at hn
    push_cast
    linear_combination -hn
  ·
    let m : (Fin g → ℂ) →ₗ[ℝ] (Fin g → ℂ) :=
      LinearMap.pi fun k => (LinearMap.mulLeft ℝ (ty k (c : E))).comp (LinearMap.proj k)
    have hm : ∀ z, m z = fun k => ty k (c : E) * z k := fun _ => rfl
    have hc : h.toLinearMap ∘ₗ m = m ∘ₗ h.toLinearMap :=
      realLinear_eq_of_sub_mem_discrete (Γ := P.range) DiscreteTopology.isDiscrete fun z =>
        (hom_eq_iff add (Λ := P.range) ker _ _).1 (by
          show e (h (m z)) = e (m (h z))
          rw [hh, hm, hm, lin, lin', hh])
    have hPm : ∀ x, P (c • x) = m (P x) := fun x => funext (hP.1 c x)
    rw [hφ, hPm, hPm, hφ]
    exact LinearMap.congr_fun hc _
  ·
    have hM1 : (l.M : ℂ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 hM.ne'
    have hadd : ∀ a b, φ (a + b) = φ a + φ b := fun a b => hP.2.1 (by simp only [hφ, map_add])
    have hns : ∀ (n : ℕ) a, φ (n • a) = n • φ a := fun n a => hP.2.1 (by simp only [hφ, map_nsmul])
    refine Submodule.smul_induction_on hb (fun j hj x _ => ?_) ?_
    · obtain ⟨a, w, rfl⟩ := (hf l.M' (Nat.lcm_pos hM l.A_pos)).2 x
      obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ : l.M ∣ l.M' := Nat.dvd_lcm_left _ _
      have ha := Submodule.smul_mem_smul hj (Submodule.mem_top (x := a))
      obtain ⟨z, hz⟩ := (hom_eq_iff add (Λ := P.range) ker _ _).1
        ((he' _ _).symm.trans ((eta' _ ha).symm.trans (eta _ ha)))
      have h1 : φ (f l.M' (j • a)) = f l.M' (j • a) + l.M • z := hP.2.1 (by
        rw [map_add, map_nsmul, hz, ← Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul ℂ, ← smul_sub, smul_inv_smul₀ hM1, add_sub_cancel])
      have h2 : j • l.M' • w = l.M • r • j • w := by rw [hr, mul_smul, smul_comm j, smul_comm j]
      refine ⟨z + φ (r • j • w) - r • j • w, ?_⟩
      rw [smul_add, ← map_smul, h2, hadd, h1, hns, smul_sub, smul_add]
      abel
    · rintro x x' ⟨z, hz⟩ ⟨z', hz'⟩
      exact ⟨z + z', by rw [hadd, hz, hz', smul_add, add_add_add_comm]⟩

end GlobalTheory


[HUF_SRC_INJECTIVE_HOLOMORPHIC] Original injective holomorphic map/invertible derivative interface
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/NotInjOnOfFderivEqZero.lean#L1-L378
import Mathlib
import ReciprocityTheory.AnalyticForms.AnalyticCharts.Osgood
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.ModuliProofs.AnalyticAtEntry

open Set Filter Metric Topology
open scoped ContDiff

namespace GlobalTheory.InjHol

theorem exists_zero_of_norm_lt {φ : ℂ → ℂ} {c : ℂ} {r : ℝ} (hr : 0 < r)
    (hφ : DifferentiableOn ℂ φ (closedBall c r)) (h : ∀ t ∈ sphere c r, ‖φ c‖ < ‖φ t‖) :
    ∃ t ∈ closedBall c r, φ t = 0 := by
  by_contra hne
  push Not at hne
  have hd : DiffContOnCl ℂ (fun t => (φ t)⁻¹) (ball c r) := by
    apply DifferentiableOn.diffContOnCl
    rw [closure_ball c hr.ne']
    exact hφ.inv hne
  obtain ⟨t, ht, hmax⟩ := Complex.exists_mem_frontier_isMaxOn_norm isBounded_ball
    (nonempty_ball.mpr hr) hd
  rw [frontier_ball c hr.ne'] at ht
  have h1 : ‖(φ c)⁻¹‖ ≤ ‖(φ t)⁻¹‖ := hmax (subset_closure (mem_ball_self hr))
  rw [norm_inv, norm_inv] at h1
  have h2 : 0 < ‖φ c‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr (hne c (mem_closedBall_self hr.le))
  exact absurd (inv_strictAnti₀ h2 (h t ht)) (not_lt.mpr h1)

theorem not_injOn_of_fderiv_eq_zero {E F : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℂ E]
    [NormedAddCommGroup F] [NormedSpace ℂ F] [CompleteSpace F] [Nontrivial E] {f : E → F} {a : E}
    (hf : AnalyticAt ℂ f a) (h0 : ∀ᶠ x in 𝓝 a, fderiv ℂ f x = 0) {s : Set E} (hs : s ∈ 𝓝 a) :
    ¬ InjOn f s := by
  intro hinj
  obtain ⟨r, hr, hb⟩ := Metric.eventually_nhds_iff_ball.mp
    (hf.eventually_analyticAt.and (h0.and hs))
  have hd : DifferentiableOn ℂ f (ball a r) := fun x hx =>
    (hb x hx).1.differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt
  have hnb : (𝓝[≠] a).NeBot := Module.punctured_nhds_neBot ℂ E a
  obtain ⟨x, hx, hxa⟩ := ((show ∀ᶠ x in 𝓝[≠] a, x ∈ ball a r from
    mem_nhdsWithin_of_mem_nhds (ball_mem_nhds a hr)).and self_mem_nhdsWithin).exists
  exact hxa (hinj (hb x hx).2.2 (mem_of_mem_nhds hs)
    (isOpen_ball.is_const_of_fderiv_eq_zero (𝕜 := ℂ) (convex_ball a r).isPreconnected hd
      (fun y hy => (hb y hy).2.1) hx (mem_ball_self hr)))

theorem deriv_ne_zero_of_injOn {f : ℂ → ℂ} {a : ℂ} (hf : AnalyticAt ℂ f a) {s : Set ℂ}
    (hs : s ∈ 𝓝 a) (hinj : InjOn f s) : deriv f a ≠ 0 := by
  intro h0
  obtain ⟨r, hr, hb⟩ := nhds_basis_closedBall.eventually_iff.mp (hf.eventually_analyticAt.and hs)
  have hd : DifferentiableOn ℂ f (closedBall a r) := fun x hx =>
    (hb hx).1.differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt
  have hsym : ∀ w, dslope f w a = dslope f a w := by
    intro w
    by_cases hw : w = a
    · rw [hw]
    · rw [dslope_of_ne _ hw, dslope_of_ne _ (Ne.symm hw), slope_comm]
  have h1 : ∀ᶠ w in 𝓝 a, ∀ z ∈ sphere a r, ‖dslope f w a‖ < ‖dslope f w z‖ := by
    refine (isCompact_sphere a r).eventually_forall_of_forall_eventually fun z hz => ?_
    have hza : z ≠ a := ne_of_mem_sphere hz hr.ne'
    have hzc := (hb (sphere_subset_closedBall hz))
    have c1 : ContinuousAt (fun p : ℂ × ℂ => ‖dslope f a p.1‖) (a, z) :=
      (ContinuousAt.comp (f := Prod.fst) (x := (a, z))
        (continuousAt_dslope_same.mpr hf.differentiableAt) continuousAt_fst).norm
    have c2 : ContinuousAt (fun p : ℂ × ℂ => ‖(p.2 - p.1)⁻¹ * (f p.2 - f p.1)‖) (a, z) := by
      refine ContinuousAt.norm (ContinuousAt.mul (ContinuousAt.inv₀ (by fun_prop)
        (sub_ne_zero.mpr hza)) (ContinuousAt.sub ?_ ?_))
      · exact ContinuousAt.comp (f := Prod.snd) (x := (a, z)) hzc.1.continuousAt continuousAt_snd
      · exact ContinuousAt.comp (f := Prod.fst) (x := (a, z)) hf.continuousAt continuousAt_fst
    have hlt : ‖dslope f a a‖ < ‖(z - a)⁻¹ * (f z - f a)‖ := by
      rw [dslope_same, h0, norm_zero, norm_pos_iff]
      exact mul_ne_zero (inv_ne_zero (sub_ne_zero.mpr hza))
        (sub_ne_zero.mpr fun h => hza (hinj hzc.2 (mem_of_mem_nhds hs) h))
    have hne : ∀ᶠ p : ℂ × ℂ in 𝓝 (a, z), p.2 ≠ p.1 :=
      (isOpen_ne_fun continuous_snd continuous_fst).mem_nhds hza
    filter_upwards [c1.eventually_lt c2 hlt, hne] with p hp hp'
    rw [hsym, dslope_of_ne _ hp', slope_def_module, smul_eq_mul]
    exact hp
  have h2 : ∀ᶠ w in 𝓝 a, fderiv ℂ f w = 0 := by
    filter_upwards [h1, ball_mem_nhds a hr] with w hw hwb
    obtain ⟨z, hz, hz0⟩ := exists_zero_of_norm_lt hr
      ((Complex.differentiableOn_dslope (mem_of_superset (isOpen_ball.mem_nhds hwb)
        ball_subset_closedBall)).mpr hd) hw
    by_cases hzw : z = w
    · rw [hzw, dslope_same] at hz0
      ext
      simpa using hz0
    · rw [dslope_of_ne _ hzw, slope_def_module, smul_eq_zero] at hz0
      rcases hz0 with h | h
      · exact absurd (sub_eq_zero.mp (inv_eq_zero.mp h)) hzw
      · exact absurd (hinj (hb hz).2 (hb (ball_subset_closedBall hwb)).2 (sub_eq_zero.mp h)) hzw
  exact not_injOn_of_fderiv_eq_zero hf h2 hs hinj

theorem exists_apply_eq_one {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℂ E]
    {L : E →L[ℂ] ℂ} (h : L ≠ 0) : ∃ v, L v = 1 := by
  obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ : ∃ w, L w ≠ 0 := by
    by_contra hc
    push Not at hc
    exact h (ContinuousLinearMap.ext hc)
  exact ⟨(L w)⁻¹ • w, by simp [hw]⟩

theorem exists_slice {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℂ E] [CompleteSpace E]
    {g : E → ℂ} {b : E} (hg : AnalyticAt ℂ g b) {L : E →L[ℂ] ℂ} (hL : HasFDerivAt g L b)
    (h0 : L ≠ 0) :
    ∃ ψ : L.ker → E, AnalyticAt ℂ ψ 0 ∧ ψ 0 = b ∧ HasFDerivAt ψ L.ker.subtypeL 0 ∧
      (∀ᶠ x in 𝓝 0, g (ψ x) = g b) ∧ ∃ s ∈ 𝓝 (0 : L.ker), InjOn ψ s := by
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := exists_apply_eq_one h0
  let R : ℂ →L[ℂ] E := ContinuousLinearMap.toSpanSingleton ℂ v
  have hR : Function.RightInverse R L := fun t => by simp [R, hv]
  let p := L.projKerOfRightInverse R hR
  let e : E ≃L[ℂ] ℂ × L.ker := ContinuousLinearEquiv.equivOfRightInverse L R hR
  let G : E → ℂ × L.ker := fun x => (g x, p (x - b))
  have hG : ContDiffAt ℂ ω G b :=
    (hg.prod ((p.analyticAt _).comp (analyticAt_id.sub analyticAt_const))).contDiffAt
  have hG' : HasFDerivAt G (e : E →L[ℂ] ℂ × L.ker) b := by
    have := hL.prodMk (p.hasFDerivAt.comp b ((hasFDerivAt_id b).sub_const b))
    exact this
  have hω : (ω : WithTop ℕ∞) ≠ 0 := by simp
  have hGb : G b = (g b, 0) := by simp [G]
  let I := hG.localInverse hG' hω
  have hI : AnalyticAt ℂ I (g b, 0) := hGb ▸ (hG.to_localInverse hG' hω).analyticAt
  have hI0 : I (g b, 0) = b := hGb ▸ hG.localInverse_apply_image hG' hω
  have hI' : HasFDerivAt I (e.symm : ℂ × L.ker →L[ℂ] E) (g b, 0) :=
    hGb ▸ (hG.hasStrictFDerivAt' hG' hω).to_localInverse.hasFDerivAt
  have hri : ∀ᶠ y in 𝓝 (g b, (0 : L.ker)), G (I y) = y :=
    hGb ▸ (hG.hasStrictFDerivAt' hG' hω).eventually_right_inverse
  have hc : ContinuousAt (fun x : L.ker => (g b, x)) 0 := by fun_prop
  have hri' := hc.eventually hri
  refine ⟨fun x => I (g b, x), ?_, hI0, ?_, ?_, _, hri', ?_⟩
  · exact hI.comp_of_eq (analyticAt_const.prod analyticAt_id) rfl
  · have := HasFDerivAt.comp (0 : L.ker) (f := fun x : L.ker => (g b, x)) hI'
      (hasFDerivAt_prodMk_right (𝕜 := ℂ) (g b) (0 : L.ker))
    convert! this
    ext x
    simp [e]
  · filter_upwards [hri'] with x hx
    exact congrArg Prod.fst hx
  · intro x hx y hy hxy
    have h1 : G (I (g b, x)) = (g b, x) := hx
    have h2 : G (I (g b, y)) = (g b, y) := hy
    have h3 : I (g b, x) = I (g b, y) := hxy
    rw [h3, h2] at h1
    exact (congrArg Prod.snd h1).symm

theorem eventually_zero_of_subset {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℂ E]
    [CompleteSpace E] {g h : E → ℂ} {b : E} (hg : AnalyticAt ℂ g b) (hh : AnalyticAt ℂ h b)
    (h0 : fderiv ℂ g b ≠ 0) (hb : h b = 0) (hsub : ∀ᶠ z in 𝓝 b, h z = 0 → g z = 0) :
    ∀ᶠ z in 𝓝 b, g z = 0 → h z = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := exists_apply_eq_one h0
  have hgb : g b = 0 := hsub.self_of_nhds hb
  have hT : Tendsto (fun q : E × ℂ => q.1 + q.2 • v) (𝓝 (b, 0)) (𝓝 b) := by
    have : ContinuousAt (fun q : E × ℂ => q.1 + q.2 • v) (b, 0) := by fun_prop
    simpa using this.tendsto
  have hT1 : Tendsto (fun q : E × ℂ => q.1) (𝓝 (b, 0)) (𝓝 b) := continuous_fst.tendsto _
  have hc : 0 < (2 * (‖v‖ + 1))⁻¹ := by positivity
  have hst := (hg.hasStrictFDerivAt.isLittleO.def hc)
  rw [nhds_prod_eq] at hst
  have hQ : ∀ᶠ q : E × ℂ in 𝓝 (b, 0), AnalyticAt ℂ h (q.1 + q.2 • v) ∧
      (g q.1 = 0 → h (q.1 + q.2 • v) = 0 → q.2 = 0) := by
    filter_upwards [hT.eventually hh.eventually_analyticAt, hT.eventually hsub,
      (hT.prodMk hT1).eventually hst] with q h1 h2 h3
    refine ⟨h1, fun h4 h5 => ?_⟩
    simp only [h2 h5, h4, add_sub_cancel_left, map_smul, hv, smul_eq_mul, mul_one, sub_self,
      zero_sub, norm_neg, norm_smul] at h3
    have h6 : (2 * (‖v‖ + 1))⁻¹ * ‖v‖ ≤ 1 / 2 := by
      rw [inv_mul_le_iff₀ (by positivity)]
      linarith
    have h7 : ‖q.2‖ ≤ 0 := by nlinarith [norm_nonneg q.2]
    exact norm_le_zero_iff.mp h7
  have hc0 : ContinuousAt (fun t : ℂ => (b, t)) 0 := by fun_prop
  obtain ⟨r, hr, hball⟩ :=
    nhds_basis_closedBall.eventually_iff.mp (hc0.eventually hQ.eventually_nhds)
  have h1 := (isCompact_closedBall (0 : ℂ) r).eventually_forall_of_forall_eventually
    (x₀ := b) (P := fun z t => AnalyticAt ℂ h (z + t • v) ∧ (g z = 0 → h (z + t • v) = 0 → t = 0))
    fun t ht => hball ht
  have h2 : ∀ᶠ z in 𝓝 b, ∀ t ∈ sphere (0 : ℂ) r, ‖h z‖ < ‖h (z + t • v)‖ := by
    refine (isCompact_sphere (0 : ℂ) r).eventually_forall_of_forall_eventually fun t ht => ?_
    have ht' := (hball (sphere_subset_closedBall ht)).self_of_nhds
    have c1 : ContinuousAt (fun q : E × ℂ => ‖h q.1‖) (b, t) :=
      (ContinuousAt.comp (f := Prod.fst) (x := (b, t)) hh.continuousAt continuousAt_fst).norm
    have c2 : ContinuousAt (fun q : E × ℂ => ‖h (q.1 + q.2 • v)‖) (b, t) :=
      (ContinuousAt.comp (f := fun q : E × ℂ => q.1 + q.2 • v) (x := (b, t))
        ht'.1.continuousAt (by fun_prop)).norm
    refine c1.eventually_lt c2 ?_
    simp only [hb, norm_zero, norm_pos_iff]
    exact fun h5 => ne_of_mem_sphere ht hr.ne' (ht'.2 hgb h5)
  filter_upwards [h1, h2] with z hz1 hz2 hgz
  obtain ⟨t, ht, ht0⟩ := exists_zero_of_norm_lt (φ := fun t => h (z + t • v)) hr
    (fun t ht => (DifferentiableAt.comp t (f := fun t : ℂ => z + t • v)
      (hz1 t ht).1.differentiableAt (by fun_prop)).differentiableWithinAt)
    (by simpa using hz2)
  have := (hz1 t ht).2 hgz ht0
  simpa [this] using ht0

theorem exists_regular_of_zero {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℂ E]
    {h : E → ℂ} {a : E} (hh : AnalyticAt ℂ h a) (ha : h a = 0) (hne : ¬ ∀ᶠ z in 𝓝 a, h z = 0)
    {W : Set E} (hW : W ∈ 𝓝 a) :
    ∃ b ∈ W, h b = 0 ∧ AnalyticAt ℂ h b ∧ ∃ g : E → ℂ, AnalyticAt ℂ g b ∧ fderiv ℂ g b ≠ 0 ∧
      ∀ᶠ z in 𝓝 b, h z = 0 → g z = 0 := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨U, hU, hUo, haU⟩ := _root_.eventually_nhds_iff.mp (hh.eventually_analyticAt.and hW)
  have hUa : AnalyticOnNhd ℂ h U := fun z hz => (hU z hz).1
  have hex : ∃ k, ∃ z ∈ U, h z = 0 ∧ iteratedFDeriv ℂ k h z ≠ 0 := by
    by_contra hc
    push Not at hc
    obtain ⟨p, r, hp⟩ := hh
    apply hne
    filter_upwards [eball_mem_nhds a hp.r_pos] with z hz
    have hz' : z - a ∈ Metric.eball (0 : E) r := by
      simpa [Metric.mem_eball, edist_eq_enorm_sub] using hz
    have := hp.hasSum_iteratedFDeriv hz'
    simp only [hc _ a haU ha, ContinuousMultilinearMap.zero_apply, smul_zero,
      add_sub_cancel] at this
    exact this.unique hasSum_zero
  obtain ⟨b, hbU, hb, hk⟩ := Nat.find_spec hex
  have hmin := fun j => Nat.find_min hex (m := j)
  generalize Nat.find hex = k at hk hmin
  cases k with
  | zero => exact absurd (by ext; simp [hb]) hk
  | succ j =>
    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ : ∃ m, iteratedFDeriv ℂ (j + 1) h b m ≠ 0 := by
      by_contra hc
      push Not at hc
      exact hk (ContinuousMultilinearMap.ext hc)
    let A := ContinuousMultilinearMap.apply ℂ (fun _ : Fin j => E) ℂ (Fin.tail m)
    have hj := hUa.iteratedFDeriv j b hbU
    refine ⟨b, (hU b hbU).2, hb, hUa b hbU, fun z => A (iteratedFDeriv ℂ j h z),
      (A.analyticAt _).comp hj, fun h0 => hm ?_, ?_⟩
    · have := (A.hasFDerivAt.comp b hj.differentiableAt.hasFDerivAt).fderiv
      have h2 : A.comp (fderiv ℂ (iteratedFDeriv ℂ j h) b) = 0 := this.symm.trans h0
      rw [iteratedFDeriv_succ_apply_left]
      exact congrArg (fun T => T (m 0)) h2
    · filter_upwards [hUo.mem_nhds hbU] with z hz hz0
      have := hmin j (Nat.lt_succ_self j)
      push Not at this
      simp [this z hz hz0]

theorem analyticAt_det {X E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup X] [NormedSpace ℂ X]
    [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℂ E] [FiniteDimensional ℂ E] {A : X → E →L[ℂ] E} {a : X}
    (hA : AnalyticAt ℂ A a) :
    AnalyticAt ℂ (fun z => LinearMap.det (A z : E →ₗ[ℂ] E)) a := by
  first
  | exact @_root_.GlobalTheory.AnalyticMatrixEntries.analyticAt_det
  | exact _root_.GlobalTheory.AnalyticMatrixEntries.analyticAt_det
  | (apply _root_.GlobalTheory.AnalyticMatrixEntries.analyticAt_det <;> assumption)
  | (intros; apply _root_.GlobalTheory.AnalyticMatrixEntries.analyticAt_det <;> assumption)
  | (intros; apply @_root_.GlobalTheory.AnalyticMatrixEntries.analyticAt_det <;> first | assumption | infer_instance)
  | (intros; exact _root_.GlobalTheory.AnalyticMatrixEntries.analyticAt_det ..)

def LocalStatement (n : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (E F : Type) [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℂ E] [FiniteDimensional ℂ E]
    [NormedAddCommGroup F] [NormedSpace ℂ F] [FiniteDimensional ℂ F],
    Module.finrank ℂ E = n → Module.finrank ℂ F = n → ∀ (f : E → F) (a : E), AnalyticAt ℂ f a →
      (∃ s ∈ 𝓝 a, InjOn f s) → Function.Injective (fderiv ℂ f a)

theorem injective_of_fderiv_ne_zero {n : ℕ} (ih : LocalStatement n) {E F : Type}
    [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℂ E] [FiniteDimensional ℂ E]
    [NormedAddCommGroup F] [NormedSpace ℂ F] [FiniteDimensional ℂ F]
    (hE : Module.finrank ℂ E = n + 1) (hF : Module.finrank ℂ F = n + 1) {f : E → F} {a : E}
    (hf : AnalyticAt ℂ f a) {s : Set E} (hs : s ∈ 𝓝 a) (hinj : InjOn f s)
    (h0 : fderiv ℂ f a ≠ 0) : Function.Injective (fderiv ℂ f a) := by
  haveI : CompleteSpace E := FiniteDimensional.complete ℂ E
  obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ : ∃ w, fderiv ℂ f a w ≠ 0 := by
    by_contra hc
    push Not at hc
    exact h0 (ContinuousLinearMap.ext hc)
  obtain ⟨l₀, hl₀⟩ := Module.Projective.exists_dual_ne_zero ℂ hw
  let l : F →L[ℂ] ℂ := LinearMap.toContinuousLinearMap l₀
  have hL0 : l.comp (fderiv ℂ f a) ≠ 0 := fun h => hl₀ (congrArg (fun T => T w) h)
  have hl0 : l ≠ 0 := fun h => hL0 (by simp [h])
  obtain ⟨ψ, hψ, hψ0, hψ', hψg, t, ht, hψi⟩ := exists_slice (g := fun x => l (f x))
    ((l.analyticAt _).comp hf) (l.hasFDerivAt.comp a hf.differentiableAt.hasFDerivAt) hL0
  obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := exists_apply_eq_one hl0
  let R : ℂ →L[ℂ] F := ContinuousLinearMap.toSpanSingleton ℂ u
  have hR : Function.RightInverse R l := fun t => by simp [R, hu]
  let p := l.projKerOfRightInverse R hR
  have hK : Module.finrank ℂ (l.comp (fderiv ℂ f a)).ker = n := by
    have := Module.Dual.finrank_ker_add_one_of_ne_zero
      (f := (l.comp (fderiv ℂ f a) : E →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ)) (fun h => hL0 (ContinuousLinearMap.coe_injective h))
    omega
  have hK' : Module.finrank ℂ l.ker = n := by
    have := Module.Dual.finrank_ker_add_one_of_ne_zero
      (f := (l : F →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ)) (fun h => hl0 (ContinuousLinearMap.coe_injective h))
    omega
  have hs' : ∀ᶠ x in 𝓝 0, ψ x ∈ s := hψ.continuousAt.eventually (by rw [hψ0]; exact hs)
  have hfa : HasFDerivAt f (fderiv ℂ f a) (ψ 0) := by
    rw [hψ0]
    exact hf.differentiableAt.hasFDerivAt
  have key := ih _ _ hK hK' (fun x => p (f (ψ x))) 0
    ((p.analyticAt _).comp (hf.comp_of_eq hψ hψ0))
    ⟨_, inter_mem ht (hs'.and hψg), fun x hx y hy hxy => by
      refine hψi hx.1 hy.1 (hinj hx.2.1 hy.2.1 ?_)
      have h1 : f (ψ x) - R (l (f (ψ x))) = f (ψ y) - R (l (f (ψ y))) := congrArg Subtype.val hxy
      rw [hx.2.2, hy.2.2] at h1
      exact sub_left_injective h1⟩
  have hd := (p.hasFDerivAt.comp 0 (hfa.comp 0 hψ')).fderiv
  rw [show fderiv ℂ (fun x => p (f (ψ x))) 0 = _ from hd] at key
  rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero] at key ⊢
  intro x hx
  exact congrArg Subtype.val (key ⟨x, by simp [hx]⟩ (by
    change p (fderiv ℂ f a x) = 0
    rw [hx, map_zero]))

theorem localStatement (n : ℕ) : LocalStatement n := by
  induction n with
  | zero =>
    intro E F _ _ _ _ _ _ hE _ f a _ _
    haveI : Subsingleton E := Module.finrank_zero_iff.mp hE
    exact Function.injective_of_subsingleton _
  | succ n ih =>
    rintro E F _ _ _ _ _ _ hE hF f a hf ⟨s, hs, hinj⟩
    haveI : CompleteSpace E := FiniteDimensional.complete ℂ E
    haveI : CompleteSpace F := FiniteDimensional.complete ℂ F
    haveI : Nontrivial E := Module.nontrivial_of_finrank_eq_succ hE
    refine injective_of_fderiv_ne_zero ih hE hF hf hs hinj fun h0 => ?_
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos n with rfl | hn
    · obtain ⟨eE⟩ : Nonempty (ℂ ≃L[ℂ] E) :=
        FiniteDimensional.nonempty_continuousLinearEquiv_of_finrank_eq (by simp [hE])
      obtain ⟨eF⟩ : Nonempty (F ≃L[ℂ] ℂ) :=
        FiniteDimensional.nonempty_continuousLinearEquiv_of_finrank_eq (by simp [hF])
      obtain ⟨c, rfl⟩ := eE.surjective a
      refine deriv_ne_zero_of_injOn (f := fun t => eF (f (eE t))) (a := c)
        ((eF.analyticAt _).comp (hf.comp (eE.analyticAt _)))
        (eE.continuous.continuousAt.preimage_mem_nhds hs)
        (fun x hx y hy hxy => eE.injective (hinj hx hy (eF.injective hxy))) ?_
      have := (eF.hasFDerivAt.comp c
        (hf.differentiableAt.hasFDerivAt.comp c eE.hasFDerivAt)).hasDerivAt.deriv
      refine this.trans ?_
      simp [h0]
    · obtain ⟨e⟩ : Nonempty (F ≃L[ℂ] E) :=
        FiniteDimensional.nonempty_continuousLinearEquiv_of_finrank_eq (hF.trans hE.symm)
      let A : E → E →L[ℂ] E := fun z => (e : F →L[ℂ] E).comp (fderiv ℂ f z)
      let h : E → ℂ := fun z => LinearMap.det (A z : E →ₗ[ℂ] E)
      have hh : AnalyticAt ℂ h a :=
        analyticAt_det (((ContinuousLinearMap.compL ℂ E F E (e : F →L[ℂ] E)).analyticAt _).comp
          hf.fderiv)
      have hW : ∀ᶠ z in 𝓝 a, AnalyticAt ℂ f z ∧ s ∈ 𝓝 z :=
        hf.eventually_analyticAt.and (eventually_mem_nhds_iff.mpr hs)
      have hZ : ∀ z, AnalyticAt ℂ f z ∧ s ∈ 𝓝 z → h z = 0 → fderiv ℂ f z = 0 := by
        intro z hz hz0
        by_contra hne
        have hi := injective_of_fderiv_ne_zero ih hE hF hz.1 hz.2 hinj hne
        exact LinearMap.det_eq_zero_iff_ker_ne_bot.mp hz0
          (LinearMap.ker_eq_bot.mpr (e.injective.comp hi))
      have ha : h a = 0 := by
        refine LinearMap.det_eq_zero_iff_ker_ne_bot.mpr ?_
        simp [A, h0]
      have hne : ¬ ∀ᶠ z in 𝓝 a, h z = 0 := fun hc =>
        not_injOn_of_fderiv_eq_zero hf (by filter_upwards [hW, hc] using hZ) hs hinj
      obtain ⟨b, hb, hb0, hhb, g, hg, hg0, hsub⟩ := exists_regular_of_zero hh ha hne hW
      have hgb : g b = 0 := hsub.self_of_nhds hb0
      have hfill := eventually_zero_of_subset hg hhb hg0 hb0 hsub
      obtain ⟨ψ, hψ, hψ0, -, hψg, t, ht, hψi⟩ :=
        exists_slice hg hg.differentiableAt.hasFDerivAt hg0
      haveI : Nontrivial (fderiv ℂ g b).ker := by
        have := Module.Dual.finrank_ker_add_one_of_ne_zero
          (f := (fderiv ℂ g b : E →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ)) (fun h => hg0 (ContinuousLinearMap.coe_injective h))
        exact Module.nontrivial_of_finrank_pos (R := ℂ) (by omega)
      have hT : Tendsto ψ (𝓝 0) (𝓝 b) := by
        have := hψ.continuousAt.tendsto
        rwa [hψ0] at this
      have hWb : ∀ᶠ z in 𝓝 b, AnalyticAt ℂ f z ∧ s ∈ 𝓝 z :=
        hb.1.eventually_analyticAt.and (eventually_mem_nhds_iff.mpr hb.2)
      refine not_injOn_of_fderiv_eq_zero (f := fun x => f (ψ x)) (hb.1.comp_of_eq hψ hψ0) ?_
        (inter_mem ht (hT hb.2)) fun x hx y hy hxy => hψi hx.1 hy.1 (hinj hx.2 hy.2 hxy)
      filter_upwards [hT.eventually hWb, hT.eventually hfill, hψg, hψ.eventually_analyticAt]
        with x h1 h2 h3 h4
      have := (h1.1.differentiableAt.hasFDerivAt.comp x h4.differentiableAt.hasFDerivAt).fderiv
      refine this.trans ?_
      simp [hZ _ h1 (h2 (h3.trans hgb))]

theorem bijective_fderiv_of_injOn :
    ∀ (n : ℕ) (U : Set (Fin n → ℂ)) (f : (Fin n → ℂ) → (Fin n → ℂ)), IsOpen U →
      DifferentiableOn ℂ f U → Set.InjOn f U → ∀ a ∈ U, Function.Bijective (fderiv ℂ f a) := by
  intro n U f hU hf hinj a ha
  have han : AnalyticAt ℂ f a := analyticAt_pi_iff.mpr fun i =>
    ReciprocityTheory.AnForm.Osgood.osgood n U (fun x => f x i) hU (differentiableOn_pi.mp hf i) a ha
  have hi := localStatement n _ _ (by simp) (by simp) f a han ⟨U, hU.mem_nhds ha, hinj⟩
  exact ⟨hi, LinearMap.injective_iff_surjective.mp
    (show Function.Injective (fderiv ℂ f a : (Fin n → ℂ) →ₗ[ℂ] (Fin n → ℂ)) from hi)⟩

end GlobalTheory.InjHol


[HUF_SRC_HOLOMORPHIC_FAMILY] Whole original holomorphic dependence for arbitrary pointwise choices and InGenus data

theorem _root_.GlobalTheory.T.isHolTo_modulipoint_exp_of_holomorphic_periods : ThetaUnifAV → Out.Laws → ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (Xo : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) (g : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)), 1 ≤ D.p + D.q → σ (baseKA l).ρ = H.rt0 → IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → ∀ (D' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → (D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))) (y : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X)) (e : ∀ b, (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G)), (D'.p = D.p ∧ D'.q = D.q ∧ ∃ (f : ℕ → D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (ν : ℕ → ℤ), (∀ n m : ℕ, 0 < n → 0 < m → ∀ a, ∃ e, f (n * m) a = f n a + n • e) ∧ (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → (∀ a, (∃ e, f n a = n • e) ↔ ∃ e, a = n • e) ∧ ∀ a', ∃ a e, a' = f n a + n • e) ∧ (∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∀ a a', (n : ℤ) ∣ D'.EF (f n a) (f n a') - ν n * D.EF a a') ∧ σ (baseKA l).ζ = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (ν l.A : ℂ) / l.A) ∧ t = f l.M') → IsOpen B → IsHolPeriods D' ty B P → (∀ b ∈ B, ((y b) ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ IsPeriod D' ty (P b) ∧ ((∀ z w, (e b) (z + w) = (e b) z * (e b) w) ∧ Function.Surjective (e b) ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => ((e b) z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃!
