[HUF_SRC_HOLOMORPHIC_CORE] Original coordinate/injectivity/local-lift proof of point/exponential holomorphy
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/IsHolToPointAndExp.lean#L226-L294
    intro n C R P₀ s ε hC hR
    refine isOpen_iff_mem_nhds.2 fun c hc => ?_
    refine Filter.Eventually.and (hC.mem_nhds hc.1) ((Filter.eventually_all_finset s).2 fun x hx => ?_)
    have : ContinuousAt (fun c => ‖R c x - P₀ x‖) c :=
      (((hR.2 x).continuousOn.continuousAt (hC.mem_nhds hc.1)).sub continuousAt_const).norm
    exact this.eventually_lt continuousAt_const (hc.2 x hx)
  have loc : ∀ b₀ ∈ B, ∃ B' : Set (Fin m → ℂ), b₀ ∈ B' ∧ B' ⊆ B ∧ IsHolTo B' y ∧
      IsHolTo (B' ×ˢ Set.univ) (fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => (e bz.1 bz.2).left) := by
    intro b₀ hb₀
    obtain ⟨C₀, y₁, -, -, -, -, hC₀, h0, hy0, hover, hhol, hinj, -⟩ :=
      hChart (KA l) σ Xo.X Xo.s D'.dim (y b₀) hsm (hU' b₀ hb₀).1
    obtain ⟨e₀, he₀⟩ : ∃ e₀, Uniformises Xo σ ty D' t (P b₀) (y₁ 0) e₀ := by
      rw [hy0]; exact ⟨_, hU' b₀ hb₀⟩
    obtain ⟨C₁, P₁, e₁, hC₁, h01, hsub, hP0, hP₁, he₁, hU₁⟩ :=
      hLift E H D l Xo σ g ty hpq hσ hty D' t D'.dim C₀ y₁ 0 (P b₀) e₀ hC₀ h0 hover hhol he₀
    have hU₁' : ∀ c ∈ C₁, Uniformises Xo σ ty D' t (P₁ c) (y₁ c) (e₁ c) := hU₁
    obtain ⟨Z, s, ε, hε, hZ, hZinj⟩ := hCoord E H D' g ty (P b₀) hty' (hP.1 b₀ hb₀)
    set C₂ := {c | c ∈ C₁ ∧ ∀ x ∈ s, ‖P₁ c x - P b₀ x‖ < ε} with hC₂def
    have hC₂ : IsOpen C₂ := near (P b₀) s ε hC₁ hP₁
    have h02 : (0 : Fin D'.dim → ℂ) ∈ C₂ := ⟨h01, fun x _ => by simpa [hP0] using hε⟩
    have hF : DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun c => Z (P₁ c)) C₂ :=
      hZ _ C₂ P₁ hC₂ ⟨fun c hc => hP₁.1 c hc.1, fun x => (hP₁.2 x).mono fun c hc => hc.1⟩ fun c hc => hc.2
    have hFinj : Set.InjOn (fun c => Z (P₁ c)) C₂ := by
      intro c hc c' hc' hcc
      obtain ⟨k, hk, -⟩ := hZinj _ _ (hP₁.1 c hc.1) (hP₁.1 c' hc'.1) hc.2 hc'.2 hcc
      exact hinj (hsub hc.1) (hsub hc'.1) (uniq (hU₁' c hc.1) (hU₁' c' hc'.1) k hk)
    obtain ⟨W, G, hW, hW0, hG, hGW⟩ :=
      exists_local_section hC₂ hF h02 (hOpen _ C₂ _ hC₂ hF hFinj 0 h02)
    set B₁ := {b | b ∈ B ∧ ∀ x ∈ s, ‖P b x - P b₀ x‖ < ε} with hB₁def
    have hB₁ : IsOpen B₁ := near (P b₀) s ε hB hP
    have hZP : DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun b => Z (P b)) B₁ :=
      hZ _ B₁ P hB₁ ⟨fun c hc => hP.1 c hc.1, fun x => (hP.2 x).mono fun c hc => hc.1⟩ fun c hc => hc.2
    set B₂ := B₁ ∩ (fun b => Z (P b)) ⁻¹' W with hB₂def
    have hB₂ : IsOpen B₂ := hZP.continuousOn.isOpen_inter_preimage hB₁ hW
    have hb₂ : b₀ ∈ B₂ := ⟨⟨hb₀, fun x _ => by simpa using hε⟩, by simpa [hP0] using hW0⟩
    have hβ : DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun b => G (Z (P b))) B₂ :=
      hG.comp (hZP.mono Set.inter_subset_left) fun b hb => hb.2
    have hβC : ∀ b ∈ B₂, G (Z (P b)) ∈ C₂ := fun b hb => (hGW _ hb.2).1
    have hk : ∀ b, ∃ k : (Fin g → ℂ) ≃ₗ[ℂ] (Fin g → ℂ), b ∈ B₂ → (∀ a, P₁ (G (Z (P b))) a = k (P b a)) ∧
        ∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, k (fun i => ty i (a : E) * z i) = fun i => ty i (a : E) * k z i := by
      intro b
      by_cases hb : b ∈ B₂
      · obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := hZinj _ _ (hP.1 b hb.1.1) (hP₁.1 _ (hβC b hb).1) hb.1.2 (hβC b hb).2
          (hGW _ hb.2).2.symm
        exact ⟨k, fun _ => hk⟩
      · exact ⟨LinearEquiv.refl _ _, fun h => absurd h hb⟩
    choose k hk using hk
    obtain ⟨B₃, hB₃, hb₃, hsub₃, hkd⟩ := exists_differentiableOn_transfer (Q := P)
      (Q' := fun b => P₁ (G (Z (P b)))) hB₂ (fun x => (hP.2 x).mono fun b hb => hb.1.1)
      (fun x => (hP₁.2 x).comp hβ fun b hb => (hβC b hb).1) hb₂ (hP.1 b₀ hb₀).2.2.2.1
      (fun b => (k b).toLinearMap) fun b hb x => (hk b hb).1 x
    refine ⟨B₃, hb₃, fun b hb => (hsub₃ hb).1.1, ?_, ?_⟩
    · refine isHolTo_congr (isHolTo_comp hhol hB₃ (hβ.mono hsub₃)
        fun b hb => hsub (hβC b (hsub₃ hb)).1) fun b hb => ?_
      exact uniq (hU' b (hsub₃ hb).1.1) (hU₁' _ (hβC b (hsub₃ hb)).1) (k b) (hk b (hsub₃ hb)).1
    · have hφ : DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => (G (Z (P bz.1)), k bz.1 bz.2))
          (B₃ ×ˢ Set.univ) :=
        (hβ.comp differentiableOn_fst fun bz hbz => hsub₃ hbz.1).prodMk hkd
      refine isHolTo_congr (isHolTo_comp he₁ (hB₃.prod isOpen_univ) hφ
        fun bz hbz => ⟨(hβC _ (hsub₃ hbz.1)).1, trivial⟩) fun bz hbz => ?_
      exact expo (hU' _ (hsub₃ hbz.1).1.1) (hU₁' _ (hβC _ (hsub₃ hbz.1)).1) (k bz.1)
        (hk _ (hsub₃ hbz.1)).1 (hk _ (hsub₃ hbz.1)).2 bz.2
  refine ⟨isHolTo_of_local fun b hb => ?_, isHolTo_of_local fun bz hbz => ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨B', h1, h2, h3, -⟩ := loc b hb
    exact ⟨B', h1, h2, h3⟩
  · obtain ⟨B', h1, h2, -, h4⟩ := loc bz.1 hbz.1
    exact ⟨B' ×ˢ Set.univ, ⟨h1, trivial⟩, Set.prod_mono h2 le_rfl, h4⟩

end GlobalTheory.HolPeriods


[HUF_SRC_FINITE_CLASSES] Original finite-image consequence for locally constant holomorphic families
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/FiniteImageOfLocallyConstantOnHolomorphicFamilie.lean#L71-L149
theorem finite_range {Y : Scheme.{0}} (p : Y ⟶ Sp ℂ) [LocallyOfFiniteType p] [QuasiCompact p] {I : Type*}
    (c : Pts p → I) (hc : IsLocallyConstant c) : (Set.range c).Finite := by
  have : CompactSpace Y := QuasiCompact.compactSpace_of_compactSpace p
  let 𝒰 := Y.affineOpenCover
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := isCompact_univ.elim_finite_subcover (X := Y)
    (fun i => ((𝒰.f i).opensRange : Set Y)) (fun i => (𝒰.f i).opensRange.isOpen)
    (fun y _ => Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨𝒰.idx y, 𝒰.covers y⟩)
  have key : ∀ {R : CommRingCat.{0}} (j : Spec R ⟶ Y) [IsOpenImmersion j],
      (Set.range (c ∘ PtsOver.map j rfl (𝟙 _))).Finite := by
    intro R j _
    have hl : IsLocallyConstant (c ∘ PtsOver.map j rfl (𝟙 _)) :=
      hc.comp_continuous (PtsOver.continuous_map _ _ _)
    have hft : LocallyOfFiniteType (j ≫ p) := inferInstance
    generalize c ∘ PtsOver.map j rfl (𝟙 _) = c' at hl
    generalize j ≫ p = q at c' hl hft
    obtain ⟨φ, rfl⟩ := Spec.map_surjective q
    let _ := φ.hom.toAlgebra
    have : Algebra.FiniteType ℂ R := (HasRingHomProperty.Spec_iff (P := @LocallyOfFiniteType)).1 hft
    exact finite_range_affine R c' hl
  refine (t.finite_toSet.biUnion fun i _ => key (𝒰.f i)).subset ?_
  rintro _ ⟨x, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨i, hi, hx⟩ := Set.mem_iUnion₂.1 (ht (Set.mem_univ (x.1 (IsLocalRing.closedPoint ℂ))))
  have hx' : x ∈ Set.range (PtsOver.map (𝒰.f i) rfl (𝟙 _)) := by
    rw [PtsOver.range_map, range_comp_of_isOpenImmersion]
    exact (le_preimage_iff x.1 _).2 hx
  obtain ⟨z, rfl⟩ := hx'
  exact Set.mem_biUnion hi ⟨z, rfl⟩

theorem isLocallyConstant_of_hol {Y : Scheme.{0}} (p : Y ⟶ Sp ℂ) (d : ℕ)
    (hp : SmoothOfRelativeDimension d p) {I : Type*} (c : Pts p → I)
    (H : ∀ (B : Set (Fin d → ℂ)) (y : (Fin d → ℂ) → Pts p), IsOpen B → IsHolTo B (fun b => (y b).1) →
      ∀ b₀ ∈ B, ∀ᶠ b in 𝓝 b₀, c (y b) = c (y b₀)) : IsLocallyConstant c := by
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
  intro x
  let h := overHomeomorph (C := ℂ) (Over.mk p)
  obtain ⟨W, σ, U, t, hW, h0, hσ, -, hhol, hco, hop, -⟩ := HolChart.exists_holomorphic_chart valAt'_rules
    existsUnique_point_of_ringHom exists_holomorphic_section_of_etale (Over.mk p) d hp (h.symm x)
  have hop' : IsOpen (σ '' W) := by
    refine TopologicalSpace.le_def.1 (le_generateFrom ?_) _ hop
    rintro _ ⟨V, f, O, hO, rfl⟩
    exact (isOpen_basicSet (C := ℂ) V f hO).preimage (isEmbedding_left (Over.mk p)).continuous
  have hx : h (σ 0) = x := by rw [hσ, Homeomorph.apply_symm_apply]
  have hev := H W (fun b => h (σ b)) hW hhol 0 h0
  have hxU : ⊤ ≤ x.1 ⁻¹ᵁ U := by rw [← hx]; exact (hco 0 h0).1
  have hT : Tendsto (fun (z : Pts p) (i : Fin d) => valR z.1 U (t i)) (𝓝 x) (𝓝 0) := by
    refine tendsto_pi_nhds.2 fun i => ?_
    have h1 := ((continuousAt_valR (t i) hxU).comp continuous_subtype_val.continuousAt).tendsto
    have h2 : valR x.1 U (t i) = (0 : Fin d → ℂ) i := by rw [← hx]; exact (hco 0 h0).2 i
    rw [← h2]
    exact h1
  have hN : h '' (σ '' W) ∈ 𝓝 x := (h.isOpenMap _ hop').mem_nhds ⟨_, ⟨0, h0, rfl⟩, hx⟩
  filter_upwards [hN, hT.eventually hev] with z hz hz'
  obtain ⟨_, ⟨w, hw, rfl⟩, rfl⟩ := hz
  have : (fun i => valR (h (σ w)).1 U (t i)) = w := funext (hco w hw).2
  rw [this, hx] at hz'
  exact hz'

theorem finite_image_of_locally_constant_on_holomorphic_families :
    (∀ (k : Type) [Field k] (σ : k →+* ℂ) (X : Scheme.{0}) (s : X ⟶ Sp k) (d : ℕ) (y₀ : Sp ℂ ⟶ X), SmoothOfRelativeDimension d s → y₀ ≫ s = spMap σ → ∃ (B : Set (Fin d → ℂ)) (y : (Fin d → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ X)) (U : X.Opens) (f : Fin d → Γ(X, U)) (n : ℕ) (r : Fin n → Γ(X, U)), IsOpen B ∧ (0 : Fin d → ℂ) ∈ B ∧ y 0 = y₀ ∧ (∀ b ∈ B, y b ≫ s = spMap σ) ∧ IsHolTo B y ∧ Set.InjOn y B ∧ ⊤ ≤ y₀ ⁻¹ᵁ U ∧ ∃ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε ∧ ∀ x : Sp ℂ ⟶ X, x ≫ s = spMap σ → ⊤ ≤ x ⁻¹ᵁ U → (∀ i, ‖valAt' x U (r i) - valAt' y₀ U (r i)‖ < ε) → x ∈ y '' B) → ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] (σ : k →+* ℂ) (X : Scheme.{0}) (s : X ⟶ Sp k) (d : ℕ) (I : Type 1) (cls : (Sp ℂ ⟶ X) → I), SmoothOfRelativeDimension d s → QuasiCompact s → (∀ (B : Set (Fin d → ℂ)) (y : (Fin d → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ X)), IsOpen B → (∀ b ∈ B, y b ≫ s = spMap σ) → IsHolTo B y → ∀ b₀ ∈ B, ∀ᶠ b in nhds b₀, cls (y b) = cls (y b₀)) → (cls '' {x | x ≫ s = spMap σ}).Finite := by
  intro _ k _ σ X s d I cls hs hq H
  let p := pullback.snd s (spMap σ)
  let q := pullback.fst s (spMap σ)
  have hp : SmoothOfRelativeDimension d p :=
    (smoothOfRelativeDimension_isStableUnderBaseChange d).of_isPullback
      (IsPullback.of_hasPullback s (spMap σ)) hs
  have : Smooth p := SmoothOfRelativeDimension.smooth d p
  have hover : ∀ z : Pts p, (z.1 ≫ q) ≫ s = spMap σ := fun z => by
    rw [Category.assoc, pullback.condition, ← Category.assoc, z.2, Category.id_comp]
  have hl : IsLocallyConstant fun z : Pts p => cls (z.1 ≫ q) := by
    refine isLocallyConstant_of_hol p d hp _ fun B y hB hy => ?_
    refine H B (fun b => (y b).1 ≫ q) hB (fun b _ => hover _) fun U f => ?_
    simp only [valAt'_eq_valR, valR_comp]
    exact hy (q ⁻¹ᵁ U) ((q.app U).hom f)
  refine (finite_range p _ hl).subset ?_
  rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
  refine ⟨⟨pullback.lift x (𝟙 _) (by rw [Category.id_comp]; exact hx), pullback.lift_snd _ _ _⟩, ?_⟩
  exact congrArg cls (pullback.lift_fst _ _ _)

end GlobalTheory.FiniteClasses


[HUF_SRC_THETA_SERIES] Actual analytic frame convention, IsAH and exact thetaChar series
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/IsLieBasis.lean#L69-L113
structure HolUnif (A : Over (Sp ℂ)) [GrpObj A] (L : LB A.left) (g : ℕ) where
  ex : (Fin g → ℂ) → (𝟙_ (Over (Sp ℂ)) ⟶ A)
  ex_add : ∀ z w, ex (z + w) = ex z * ex w
  ex_onto : Function.Surjective ex
  Λ : Submodule ℤ (Fin g → ℂ)
  ker : ∀ z, ex z = 1 ↔ z ∈ Λ
  discrete : DiscreteTopology Λ
  full : Submodule.span ℝ (Λ : Set (Fin g → ℂ)) = ⊤
  hol : ∀ (U : A.left.Opens) (f : Γ(A.left, U)), IsOpen {z | ⊤ ≤ (ex z).left ⁻¹ᵁ U} ∧
    DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun z => valAt' (ex z).left U f) {z | ⊤ ≤ (ex z).left ⁻¹ᵁ U}
  fr : L.ι → (Fin g → ℂ) → ℂ
  fr_hol : ∀ i, DifferentiableOn ℂ (fr i) {z | ⊤ ≤ (ex z).left ⁻¹ᵁ L.U i}
  fr_ne : ∀ i z, ⊤ ≤ (ex z).left ⁻¹ᵁ L.U i → fr i z ≠ 0
  fr_compat : ∀ i j z, ⊤ ≤ (ex z).left ⁻¹ᵁ (L.U i ⊓ L.U j) →
    fr i z = valAt' (ex z).left (L.U i ⊓ L.U j) (L.g i j).val * fr j z

namespace HolUnif

variable {A : Over (Sp ℂ)} [GrpObj A] {L : LB A.left} {g : ℕ} (u : HolUnif A L g)

def IsFn (s : L.Sec) (θ : (Fin g → ℂ) → ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∀ i z, ⊤ ≤ (u.ex z).left ⁻¹ᵁ L.U i → valAt' (u.ex z).left (L.U i) (s.f i) = θ z * u.fr i z

def IsRig (rig : LB.Gen (L.pull (eSec A))) : Prop :=
  ∀ j : (L.pull (eSec A)).ι, ⊤ ≤ (L.pull (eSec A)).U j →
    u.fr (j PUnit.unit) 0 = valAt' (𝟙 (Sp ℂ)) ((L.pull (eSec A)).U j) (rig.s j).val

def IsDiff (w : Fin g → ℂ) (v : epsR ℂ ⟶ A) : Prop :=
  ∀ (U : A.left.Opens) (h : ⊤ ≤ v.left ⁻¹ᵁ U) (f : Γ(A.left, U)),
    TrivSqZeroExt.snd (show DualNumber ℂ from
      (Scheme.ΓSpecIso (CommRingCat.of (DualNumber ℂ))).hom.hom ((v.left.appLE U ⊤ h).hom f)) =
      fderiv ℂ (fun z => valAt' (u.ex z).left U f) 0 w

def IsAH (Hm : Matrix (Fin g) (Fin g) ℂ) (χ : u.Λ → ℂ) : Prop :=
  Hm.IsHermitian ∧ (∀ z : Fin g → ℂ, z ≠ 0 → 0 < (z ⬝ᵥ Hm.mulVec (star z)).re) ∧ (∀ l, ‖χ l‖ = 1) ∧
    ∀ (l : u.Λ) i z, ⊤ ≤ (u.ex z).left ⁻¹ᵁ L.U i →
      u.fr i z = χ l * Complex.exp (Real.pi * (z ⬝ᵥ Hm.mulVec (star (l : Fin g → ℂ))) +
        Real.pi / 2 * ((l : Fin g → ℂ) ⬝ᵥ Hm.mulVec (star (l : Fin g → ℂ)))) * u.fr i (z + l)

end HolUnif

def thetaChar {g : ℕ} (τ : Matrix (Fin g) (Fin g) ℂ) (a b : Fin g → ℂ) (z : Fin g → ℂ) : ℂ :=
  ∑' n : Fin g → ℤ, Complex.exp (Real.pi * Complex.I * (((fun i => (n i : ℂ)) + a) ⬝ᵥ τ.mulVec ((fun i => (n i : ℂ)) + a)) +
    2 * Real.pi * Complex.I * (((fun i => (n i : ℂ)) + a) ⬝ᵥ (z + b)))



[HUF_SRC_OMEGA] Exact source symplectic form omegaStd, including its sign
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/ReciprocityTheory/ReductiveGroups/ArithmeticGeometry/BirationalGeometry/ToroidalCompactification/Clauses.lean#L14-L14
def omegaStd {h : ℕ} (a b : Fin h ⊕ Fin h → ℤ) : ℤ := ∑ i, (a (.inl i) * b (.inr i) - a (.inr i) * b (.inl i))


[HUF_SRC_THETA_SINGLE] Original individual normalized theta-characteristic interface
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/ExistsUniqueThetaCharOfUnif.lean#L11-L12
theorem GlobalTheory.existsUnique_thetaChar_of_unif : ThetaUnifAV → Out.Laws → (∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (B B' : Type) [CommRing B] [CommRing B'] (b : Base l B) (b' : Base l B') (j : B →+* B') (S S' : Scheme.{0}) (s : S ⟶ Sp B) (s' : S' ⟶ Sp B') (f : S' ⟶ S), j b.ρ = b'.ρ → j b.ζ = b'.ζ → f ≫ s = s' ≫ spMap j → ∀ o : Obj D l b s, ∃ o' : Obj D l b' s', Nonempty (ObjMap o' o f)) → (∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (B : Type) [CommRing B] (b : Base l B) (S : Scheme.{0}) (s : S ⟶ Sp B) (o : Obj D l b s), IsAbelianScheme o.t.G ∧ SmoothOfRelativeDimension (Module.finrank ℚ E / 2 * (D.p + D.q)) o.t.G.hom ∧ Out.Rigidified o.t.G o.t.PP.L ∧ Out.Sym o.t.G o.t.PP.L) → ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (Xo : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) (g : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)), 1 ≤ D.p + D.q → σ (baseKA l).ρ = H.rt0 → IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → ∀ (D' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → (D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))) (y : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X)) (e : ∀ b, (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G)) (bas : (Fin g ⊕ Fin g → ℤ) ≃ₗ[ℤ] D'.La) (sgn : ℤˣ) (h τ : (Fin m → ℂ) → Matrix (Fin g) (Fin g) ℂ), (∀ b ∈ B, ((y b) ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ IsPeriod D' ty (P b) ∧ ((∀ z w, (e b) (z + w) = (e b) z * (e b) w) ∧ Function.Surjective (e b) ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => ((e b) z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = ((e b) 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left)) ∧ (∀ z, (e b) z = 1 ↔ z ∈ ((P b)).range) ∧ (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, (e b) (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = (e b) z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a) ∧ (∀ (k : ℕ) (a a₂ : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k → (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a a₂ : ℂ) / k) → Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k ((e b) ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) a)) ((e b) ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) a₂)) (unitOn c)) ∧ (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = (e b) ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) (t a))) ∧ ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = (e b) ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) (t a)))) → (∀ a b, D'.EF (bas a) (bas b) = sgn * omegaStd a b) → (∀ b ∈ B, ((τ b).map Complex.im).PosDef ∧ ∀ k, (h b).mulVec (P b (bas (Pi.single (.inl k) 1))) = Pi.single k 1 ∧ (h b).mulVec (P b (bas (Pi.single (.inr k) 1))) = (τ b).mulVec (Pi.single k 1)) → ∀ b₀ ∈ B, ∃! cc : (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ), ∃ c₁ c₂, cc = (c₁, c₂) ∧ (∀ k, c₁ k = 0 ∨ c₁ k = 1 / 2) ∧ (∀ k, c₂ k = 0 ∨ c₂ k = 1 / 2) ∧ IsThetaFrame Xo.univ.t.PP {b₀} y (fun b z => (e b z).left) (fun b z => thetaChar (τ b) c₁ c₂ ((h b).mulVec z)) :=
  fun hT hLaws _ _ => GlobalTheory.existsUnique_thetaChar_at_point' hT hLaws


[HUF_SRC_THETA_GLUING] Original theta family, preconnected characteristic constancy and frame gluing
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/ModuliProofs/ExistsThetaFrameOfPreconnected.lean#L14-L168
theorem thetaChar_family {g m : ℕ} {B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)} {p q : Fin g → (Fin m → ℂ) → (Fin g → ℂ)}
    {h τ : (Fin m → ℂ) → Matrix (Fin g) (Fin g) ℂ} (hp : ∀ k, DifferentiableOn ℂ (p k) B)
    (hq : ∀ k, DifferentiableOn ℂ (q k) B)
    (hh : ∀ b ∈ B, ((τ b).map Complex.im).PosDef ∧ ∀ k, (h b).mulVec (p k b) = Pi.single k 1 ∧
      (h b).mulVec (q k b) = (τ b).mulVec (Pi.single k 1))
    {c₁ c₂ : Fin g → ℂ} (h₁ : ∀ k, c₁ k = 0 ∨ c₁ k = 1 / 2) (h₂ : ∀ k, c₂ k = 0 ∨ c₂ k = 1 / 2) :
    DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => thetaChar (τ bz.1) c₁ c₂ ((h bz.1).mulVec bz.2))
      (B ×ˢ Set.univ) ∧ ∀ b ∈ B, ∀ z, ∃ᶠ w in nhds z, thetaChar (τ b) c₁ c₂ ((h b).mulVec w) ≠ 0 := by
  have re : ∀ {c : Fin g → ℂ}, (∀ k, c k = 0 ∨ c k = 1 / 2) → ∃ a : Fin g → ℝ, c = fun i => (a i : ℂ) := fun {c} hc =>
    ⟨fun i => (c i).re, funext fun i => by rcases hc i with e | e <;> simp [e]⟩
  obtain ⟨a₁, rfl⟩ := re h₁
  obtain ⟨a₂, rfl⟩ := re h₂
  have hinv : ∀ b ∈ B, h b * Matrix.of (fun i k => p k b i) = 1 := fun b hb => by
    ext i k
    have := congrFun ((hh b hb).2 k).1 i
    rw [Matrix.one_apply, ← Pi.single_apply k (1 : ℂ) i, ← this]
    rfl
  have dh : ∀ i j, DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun b => h b i j) B :=
    differentiableOn_left_inverse (fun i k => differentiableOn_pi.1 (hp k) i) hinv
  have dτ : ∀ i k, DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun b => τ b i k) B := fun i k => by
    refine (DifferentiableOn.fun_sum (u := Finset.univ) fun j _ =>
      (dh i j).mul (differentiableOn_pi.1 (hq k) j)).congr fun b hb => ?_
    have := congrFun ((hh b hb).2 k).2 i
    rw [Matrix.mulVec_single_one] at this
    exact this.symm
  have fst : ∀ {f : (Fin m → ℂ) → ℂ}, DifferentiableOn ℂ f B →
      DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => f bz.1) (B ×ˢ Set.univ) := fun hf =>
    hf.comp differentiableOn_fst fun _ h => h.1
  refine ⟨CanTh.differentiableOn_thetaChar (T := fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => τ bz.1)
    (Z := fun bz => (h bz.1).mulVec bz.2) (fun i k => fst (dτ i k)) (differentiableOn_pi.2 fun i => ?_)
    (fun w hw => CanTh.mem_thetaDomain (hh _ hw.1).1) a₁ a₂, fun b hb => ?_⟩
  · simp only [Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct]
    exact DifferentiableOn.fun_sum fun j _ => (fst (dh i j)).mul (by fun_prop)
  · have dl : Differentiable ℂ (fun w : Fin g → ℂ => (h b).mulVec w) :=
      (LinearMap.toContinuousLinearMap (Matrix.mulVecLin (h b))).differentiable
    refine frequently_ne_zero_of_entire
      (θ := fun w => thetaChar (τ b) (fun i => (a₁ i : ℂ)) (fun i => (a₂ i : ℂ)) ((h b).mulVec w))
      ((CanTh.differentiable_thetaChar (CanTh.mem_thetaDomain (hh b hb).1) a₁ a₂).comp dl) fun h0 => ?_
    refine thetaChar_ne_zero (hh b hb).1 a₁ a₂ (funext fun w => ?_)
    have := congrFun h0 ((Matrix.of (fun i k => p k b i)).mulVec w)
    rwa [Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, hinv b hb, Matrix.one_mulVec] at this

theorem exists_thetaFrame_of_preconnected : Out.Laws → (∀ (k : Type) [Field k] (X : Scheme.{0}) (s
    : X ⟶ Sp k), Smooth s → IsReduced X ∧ IsNormalScheme X ∧ IsLocallyNoetherian X) → (∀ (E
    : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (B
    : Type) [CommRing B] (b : Base l B) (S : Scheme.{0}) (s : S ⟶ Sp B) (o
    : Obj D l b s), IsAbelianScheme o.t.G ∧ SmoothOfRelativeDimension (Module.finrank ℚ E / 2 * (D.p
    + D.q)) o.t.G.hom ∧ Out.Rigidified o.t.G o.t.PP.L ∧ Out.Sym o.t.G o.t.PP.L) → (∀ (E
    : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (Xo
    : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) (g : ℕ) (ty : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)), 1 ≤ D.p + D.q → σ (baseKA
    l).ρ = H.rt0 → IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → ∀ (D' : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (m : ℕ) (B
    : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → (D'.La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))) (y : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶
    Xo.X)) (e : ∀ b, (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G)) (bas : (Fin g ⊕ Fin g →
    ℤ) ≃ₗ[ℤ] D'.La) (sgn : ℤˣ) (h τ : (Fin m → ℂ) → Matrix (Fin g) (Fin g) ℂ), (∀ b ∈ B, ((y b) ≫
    Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ IsPeriod D' ty (P b) ∧ ((∀ z w, (e b) (z + w) = (e b) z * (e b) w) ∧
    Function.Surjective (e b) ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ (fun z => ((e b) z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt (y
    b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt (y
    b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = ((e b)
    0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left)) ∧ (∀ z, (e b) z = 1 ↔ z ∈ ((P
    b)).range) ∧ (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, (e b) (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = (e
    b) z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a) ∧ (∀ (k : ℕ) (a a₂ : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k → (c
    : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a a₂ : ℂ) /
    k) → Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k ((e b) ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) a)) ((e b) ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ •
    (P b) a₂)) (unitOn c)) ∧ (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞
    E) D.La), toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = (e b) ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) (t a))) ∧
    ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = (e b) ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) (t a)))) → (∀ a b, D'.EF (bas
    a) (bas b) = sgn * omegaStd a b) → (∀ b ∈ B, ((τ b).map Complex.im).PosDef ∧ ∀ k, (h
    b).mulVec (P b (bas (Pi.single (.inl k) 1))) = Pi.single k 1 ∧ (h b).mulVec (P b (bas (Pi.single
    (.inr k) 1))) = (τ b).mulVec (Pi.single k 1)) → ∀ b₀ ∈ B, ∃! cc : (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g →
    ℂ), ∃ c₁ c₂, cc = (c₁,
    c₂) ∧ (∀ k, c₁ k = 0 ∨ c₁ k = 1 / 2) ∧ (∀ k, c₂ k = 0 ∨ c₂ k = 1 / 2) ∧ IsThetaFrame Xo.univ.t.PP {b₀} y (fun b z => (e
    b z).left) (fun b z => thetaChar (τ b) c₁ c₂ ((h b).mulVec z))) → (∀ (X
    : Scheme.{0}) [IsLocallyNoetherian X] (P : Out.PolAV X) (x : X), ∃ (V : X.Opens) (θ
    : ∀ i, Γ(P.A.left, P.A.hom ⁻¹ᵁ V ⊓ P.L.U i)), x ∈ V ∧ θ ∈ secOn P.L (P.A.hom ⁻¹ᵁ
    V) ∧ (∀ y ∈ V, ∃ (a : P.A.left) (i : P.L.ι), P.A.hom a = y ∧ a ∈ P.A.left.basicOpen (θ
    i))) → (∀ (V V' : Type) [NormedAddCommGroup V] [NormedSpace ℂ V] [FiniteDimensional ℂ
    V] [NormedAddCommGroup V'] [NormedSpace ℂ V'] [FiniteDimensional ℂ V'] (W : Set (V × V')) (f θ F
    : V × V' → ℂ), IsOpen W → DifferentiableOn ℂ f W → DifferentiableOn ℂ θ W → (∀ w ∈ W, F w * θ w = f w) → (∀ b, DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun z => F (b,
    z)) {z | (b, z) ∈ W}) → (∀ w ∈ W, ∃ᶠ z in nhds w.2, θ (w.1,
    z) ≠ 0) → DifferentiableOn ℂ F W) → ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H
    : Host E) (D : Datum H) (l : Level H) (Xo : Moduli D l) (σ : KA l →+* ℂ) (g : ℕ) (ty
    : Fin g → (E →+* ℂ)), 1 ≤ D.p + D.q → σ (baseKA l).ρ = H.rt0 → IsTypeFn H D.p D.q ty → ∀ (D'
    : Datum H) (t : D.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] D'.La) (m : ℕ) (B : Set (Fin m → ℂ)) (P : (Fin m → ℂ) → (D'.La →+
    (Fin g → ℂ))) (y : (Fin m → ℂ) → (Sp ℂ ⟶ Xo.X)) (e : ∀ b, (Fin g → ℂ) → (Over.mk (y b) ⟶
    Xo.univ.t.G)) (bas : (Fin g ⊕ Fin g → ℤ) ≃ₗ[ℤ] D'.La) (sgn : ℤˣ) (h τ : (Fin m →
    ℂ) → Matrix (Fin g) (Fin g) ℂ), (∀ b ∈ B, ((y b) ≫ Xo.s = spMap σ ∧ IsPeriod D' ty (P b) ∧
    ((∀ z w, (e b) (z + w) = (e b) z * (e b) w) ∧ Function.Surjective (e b) ∧ IsHolTo Set.univ
    (fun z => ((e b) z).left) ∧ (∀ w, ∃! v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b)
    z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ
    ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = ((e b) 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left)) ∧
    (∀ z, (e b) z = 1 ↔ z ∈ ((P b)).range) ∧ (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, (e b) (fun k => ty k (a
    : E) * z k) = (e b) z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a) ∧ (∀ (k : ℕ) (a a₂ : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k → (c
    : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a a₂ : ℂ) /
    k) → Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k ((e b) ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) a)) ((e b) ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ •
    (P b) a₂)) (unitOn c)) ∧ (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞
    E) D.La), toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = (e b) ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) (t a))) ∧
    ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = (e b) ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) (t a)))) → (∀ a b, D'.EF (bas
    a) (bas b) = sgn * omegaStd a b) → (∀ b ∈ B, ((τ b).map Complex.im).PosDef ∧ ∀ k, (h
    b).mulVec (P b (bas (Pi.single (.inl k) 1))) = Pi.single k 1 ∧ (h b).mulVec (P b (bas (Pi.single
    (.inr k) 1))) = (τ b).mulVec (Pi.single k
    1)) → IsOpen B → IsPreconnected B → IsHolPeriods D' ty B P → IsHolTo B y → IsHolTo (B ×ˢ
    Set.univ) (fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => (e bz.1
    bz.2).left) → ∃ c₁ c₂ : Fin g → ℂ, (∀ k, c₁ k = 0 ∨ c₁ k = 1 / 2) ∧ (∀ k, c₂ k = 0 ∨ c₂ k = 1 / 2) ∧ IsThetaFrame Xo.univ.t.PP B y (fun b z => (e
    b z).left) (fun b z => thetaChar (τ b) c₁ c₂ ((h b).mulVec z)) := by
  intro laws hsm hobj hpt hsec _ E _ _ _ H D l Xo σ g ty h1 h2 h3 D' t m B P y e bas sgn h τ hU hbas hh _ hc hP hy hol
  have pt := hpt E H D l Xo σ g ty h1 h2 h3 D' t m B P y e bas sgn h τ hU hbas hh
  have th := fun (c₁ c₂ : Fin g → ℂ) h₁ h₂ => thetaChar_family (c₁ := c₁) (c₂ := c₂)
    (p := fun k b => P b (bas (Pi.single (.inl k) 1))) (q := fun k b => P b (bas (Pi.single (.inr k) 1)))
    (fun k => hP.2 _) (fun k => hP.2 _) hh h₁ h₂
  have he : ∀ b ∈ B, ∀ z, (e b z).left ≫ Xo.univ.t.G.hom = y b := fun b _ z => Over.w (e b z)
  have he0 : ∀ b ∈ B, (e b 0).left = y b ≫ eSec Xo.univ.t.G := fun b hb => by
    rw [((hU b hb).2.2.2.1 0).2 (zero_mem _)]
    rfl
  have := Xo.smooth
  have := (hsm _ _ Xo.s (SmoothOfRelativeDimension.smooth D.dim Xo.s)).2.2
  obtain ⟨o₁, o₂, o₃, o₄⟩ := hobj E H D l _ _ _ _ Xo.univ
  have sec : ∀ b₀ ∈ B, ∃ (V : Xo.X.Opens) (θ₀ : ∀ j, Γ(Xo.univ.t.G.left, Xo.univ.t.G.hom ⁻¹ᵁ V ⊓ Xo.univ.t.PP.L.U j)),
      ⊤ ≤ y b₀ ⁻¹ᵁ V ∧ ∀ b ∈ B, ⊤ ≤ y b ⁻¹ᵁ V → SpansFibre Xo.univ.t.PP (y b) V θ₀ := fun b₀ _ => by
    obtain ⟨V, θ₀, m₀, s₀, n₀⟩ := hsec Xo.X
      { A := Xo.univ.t.G, isAb := o₁, g := _, smoothDim := o₂, L := Xo.univ.t.PP.L, isLB := Xo.univ.t.PP.isLB
        rigid := o₃, sym := o₄, ample := Xo.univ.ample, ktriv := Xo.univ.ktriv } (y b₀ (default : PrimeSpectrum ℂ))
    exact ⟨V, θ₀, (top_le_preimage_iff_mem _ _).2 m₀, fun b _ hb =>
      exists_ratio_fibre laws.globalSectionsRank Xo.univ (y b) V θ₀ s₀ n₀ hb⟩
  choose! c hc₁ hc₂ using pt
  let C₀ : Set (Fin g → ℂ) := Set.pi Set.univ fun _ => {0, 1 / 2}
  have fin : (C₀ ×ˢ C₀).Finite := (Set.Finite.pi fun _ => Set.toFinite _).prod (Set.Finite.pi fun _ => Set.toFinite _)
  have hc₃ : ∀ b ∈ B, (∀ k, (c b).1 k = 0 ∨ (c b).1 k = 1 / 2) ∧ (∀ k, (c b).2 k = 0 ∨ (c b).2 k = 1 / 2) ∧
      ∃ fr, IsFibreFrame Xo.univ.t.PP (y b) (fun z => (e b z).left)
        (fun z => thetaChar (τ b) (c b).1 (c b).2 ((h b).mulVec z)) fr := fun b hb => by
    obtain ⟨c₁, c₂, e₀, r₁, r₂, r₃⟩ := hc₁ b hb
    have r := isThetaFrame_singleton_iff.1 r₃
    rw [e₀]
    exact ⟨r₁, r₂, r⟩
  have const := eq_of_finite_of_closed_fibres hc fin c
    (fun b hb => ⟨fun k _ => (hc₃ b hb).1 k, fun k _ => (hc₃ b hb).2.1 k⟩) (fun k hk b hb hcl => by
      have k₁ := fun i => hk.1 i trivial
      have k₂ := fun i => hk.2 i trivial
      obtain ⟨V, θ₀, hV₀, hV⟩ := sec b hb
      obtain ⟨fr', h'⟩ := (hc₃ b hb).2.2
      have r := exists_fibreFrame_of_mem_closure (e := fun b z => (e b z).left)
        (θ := fun b z => thetaChar (τ b) k.1 k.2 ((h b).mulVec z)) he hol (th _ _ k₁ k₂).1 (th _ _ k₁ k₂).2 hb hV₀ hV
        (T := {b' ∈ B | c b' = k}) (fun b' hb' => by have r := (hc₃ b' hb'.1).2.2; rw [hb'.2] at r; exact r) hcl
        ((th _ _ (hc₃ b hb).1 (hc₃ b hb).2.1).2 b hb) h'
      exact (hc₂ b hb k ⟨k.1, k.2, rfl, k₁, k₂, (isThetaFrame_singleton_iff (y := y) (e := fun b z => (e b z).left)
        (θ := fun b z => thetaChar (τ b) k.1 k.2 ((h b).mulVec z))).2 r⟩).symm)
  obtain ⟨k, k₁, k₂, hk⟩ : ∃ k : (Fin g → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ), (∀ i, k.1 i = 0 ∨ k.1 i = 1 / 2) ∧
      (∀ i, k.2 i = 0 ∨ k.2 i = 1 / 2) ∧ ∀ b ∈ B, c b = k := by
    rcases B.eq_empty_or_nonempty with h0 | ⟨b₀, hb₀⟩
    · exact ⟨0, fun _ => Or.inl rfl, fun _ => Or.inl rfl, fun b hb => absurd (h0 ▸ hb) (Set.notMem_empty b)⟩
    · exact ⟨c b₀, (hc₃ b₀ hb₀).1, (hc₃ b₀ hb₀).2.1, fun b hb => const b hb b₀ hb₀⟩
  exact ⟨k.1, k.2, k₁, k₂, isThetaFrame_of_fibreFrames (e := fun b z => (e b z).left)
    (θ := fun b z => thetaChar (τ b) k.1 k.2 ((h b).mulVec z)) he he0 hy hol (th _ _ k₁ k₂).1 (th _ _ k₁ k₂).2 sec
    fun b hb => by have r := (hc₃ b hb).2.2; rw [hk b hb] at r; exact r⟩

end GlobalTheory.ThetaFamily

v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left) ∧ (∀ v : epsPt (y b) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G, spMap (TrivSqZeroExt.fstHom ℂ ℂ ℂ).toRingHom ≫ v.left = ((e b) 0).left → ∃! w, IsDerivAt0 (fun z => ((e b) z).left) w v.left)) ∧ (∀ z, (e b) z = 1 ↔ z ∈ ((P b)).range) ∧ (∀ (a : 𝓞 E) z, (e b) (fun k => ty k (a : E) * z k) = (e b) z ≫ Xo.univ.t.act.act a) ∧ (∀ (k : ℕ) (a a₂ : D'.La) (c : ℂˣ), 0 < k → (c : ℂ) = Complex.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) * (D'.EF a a₂ : ℂ) / k) → Out.WeilIs Xo.univ.t.G Xo.univ.t.PP.L k ((e b) ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) a)) ((e b) ((k : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) a₂)) (unitOn c)) ∧ (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) D.La), toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.eta a = (e b) ((l.M : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) (t a))) ∧ ∀ a, toUnit _ ≫ Xo.univ.etaA a = (e b) ((l.A : ℂ)⁻¹ • (P b) (t a)))) → (∀ a b, D'.EF (bas a) (bas b) = sgn * omegaStd a b) → (∀ b ∈ B, ((τ b).map Complex.im).PosDef ∧ ∀ k, (h b).mulVec (P b (bas (Pi.single (.inl k) 1))) = Pi.single k 1 ∧ (h b).mulVec (P b (bas (Pi.single (.inr k) 1))) = (τ b).mulVec (Pi.single k 1)) → IsOpen B → IsPreconnected B → IsHolPeriods D' ty B P → IsHolTo B y → IsHolTo (B ×ˢ Set.univ) (fun bz : (Fin m → ℂ) × (Fin g → ℂ) => (e bz.1 bz.2).left) → ∃ c₁ c₂ : Fin g → ℂ, (∀ k, c₁ k = 0 ∨ c₁ k = 1 / 2) ∧ (∀ k, c₂ k = 0 ∨ c₂ k = 1 / 2) ∧ IsThetaFrame Xo.univ.t.PP B y (fun b z => (e b z).left) (fun b z => thetaChar (τ b) c₁ c₂ ((h b).mulVec z)) :=

[HUF_SRC_REMAINING_FRAMES] Retained FrameOK/FramesOK/CoverTri/FibTri on the same family
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/ExistenceOfLattices/NormConst.lean#L35-L126

def FrameOK {g : ℕ} (e : Fin g ≃ Σ τ : E →+* ℂ, Fin (Dw.mult τ))
    (F : FamilyUnif Xo (σ.comp (IntermediateField.inclusion c.le).toRingHom) (fun j => relab c σ (e j).1))
    (M : ModelsOf F r) (k : Fin F.n) (x : (M.X k).dom) (y : FrPt Xo c (CAt σ)) : Prop :=
  ∃ Pm : (F.Di k).La →+ (Fin g → ℂ),
        (∀ a j, Pm a j = perFr (M.X k) x.1 (M.φ k a : Fin r → E) (relab c σ (e j).1) (e j).2) ∧
        IsPeriod (F.Di k) (fun j => relab c σ (e j).1) Pm ∧ y.x = F.u k Pm ∧
        (∀ τ (i : Fin (Dw.mult τ)), IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (F.ex k Pm z).left) (Pi.single (e.symm ⟨τ, i⟩) 1)
          (y.u τ i)) ∧
        ((∀ τ' i', ‖dmin (M.X k).Gm x.1 τ' i'‖ = 1) →
          ∃ (Hm : Matrix (Fin g) (Fin g) ℂ) (χl : (Fin g → ℂ) → ℂ) (fr : Xo.univ.t.PP.L.ι → (Fin g → ℂ) → ℂ)
            (θ : (Fin g → ℂ) → ℂ) (Fd : Set (Fin g → ℂ)),
            Hm.IsHermitian ∧ (∀ z : Fin g → ℂ, z ≠ 0 → 0 < (hermForm Hm z z).re) ∧
            (∀ a b, (hermForm Hm (Pm a) (Pm b)).im = (F.Di k).EF a b) ∧
            (∀ j, DifferentiableOn ℂ (fr j) {z | ⊤ ≤ (F.ex k Pm z).left ⁻¹ᵁ Xo.univ.t.PP.L.U j}) ∧
            (∀ j z, ⊤ ≤ (F.ex k Pm z).left ⁻¹ᵁ Xo.univ.t.PP.L.U j → fr j z ≠ 0) ∧
            (∀ j j' z, ⊤ ≤ (F.ex k Pm z).left ⁻¹ᵁ (Xo.univ.t.PP.L.U j ⊓ Xo.univ.t.PP.L.U j') →
              fr j z = valAt' (F.ex k Pm z).left (Xo.univ.t.PP.L.U j ⊓ Xo.univ.t.PP.L.U j')
                (Xo.univ.t.PP.L.g j j').val * fr j' z) ∧
            (∀ j, ⊤ ≤ (F.ex k Pm 0).left ⁻¹ᵁ Xo.univ.t.PP.L.U j →
              fr j 0 = valAt' y.x ((Xo.univ.t.PP.L.pull (eSec Xo.univ.t.G)).U (fun _ => j))
                (Xo.univ.t.PP.rig.s (fun _ => j)).val) ∧
            (∀ a j z, ‖χl (Pm a)‖ = 1 ∧ (⊤ ≤ (F.ex k Pm z).left ⁻¹ᵁ Xo.univ.t.PP.L.U j →
              fr j z = χl (Pm a) * Complex.exp (Real.pi * hermForm Hm z (Pm a) +
                Real.pi / 2 * hermForm Hm (Pm a) (Pm a)) * fr j (z + Pm a))) ∧
            (∀ j z (pt : Sp ℂ ⟶ Gx y.x),
              pt ≫ gxFst y.x = (F.ex k Pm z).left →
              pt ≫ gxSnd y.x = 𝟙 (Sp ℂ) →
              ⊤ ≤ (F.ex k Pm z).left ⁻¹ᵁ Xo.univ.t.PP.L.U j →
              valAt' pt ((PPx y.x).U (fun _ => j)) (y.s.f (fun _ => j)) = θ z * fr j z) ∧
            MeasureTheory.IsAddFundamentalDomain Pm.range Fd MeasureTheory.volume ∧
            (∫ z in Fd, ‖θ z‖ ^ 2 * Real.exp (-Real.pi * (hermForm Hm z z).re)) /
              (MeasureTheory.volume Fd).toReal = 1)

def unfOf {g : ℕ} (e : Fin g ≃ Σ τ : E →+* ℂ, Fin (Dw.mult τ))
    (F : FamilyUnif Xo (σ.comp (IntermediateField.inclusion c.le).toRingHom) (fun j => relab c σ (e j).1))
    (M : ModelsOf F r) (un : ∀ k, (M.X k).dom → FrPt Xo c (CAt σ)) : Unf Xo c σ r where
  Comp := Fin F.n
  X := M.X
  un := un

def FramesOK {g : ℕ} (e : Fin g ≃ Σ τ : E →+* ℂ, Fin (Dw.mult τ))
    (F : FamilyUnif Xo (σ.comp (IntermediateField.inclusion c.le).toRingHom) (fun j => relab c σ (e j).1))
    (M : ModelsOf F r) (un : ∀ k, (M.X k).dom → FrPt Xo c (CAt σ)) : Prop :=
  (∀ k x, FrameOK e F M k x (un k x)) ∧
  (∀ k (x : (M.X k).dom) b (_hb : b ∈ Bor (M.X k).Ψ r) (h : rmul x.1 b ∈ (M.X k).dom),
    ∃ t : Ix Dw → (CAt σ)ˣ, FrBor t (un k x) (un k ⟨_, h⟩) ∧
      ∀ a n, Dom a n → toC (wchar (ex Dw a n) t) = bchar (laU (unfOf e F M un) a n k) b) ∧
  (∀ k, ∀ γ ∈ (M.X k).Γ, ∀ (x : (M.X k).dom) (h : lact γ x.1 ∈ (M.X k).dom),
    ∃ t : Ix Dw → (CAt σ)ˣ, FrBor t (un k x) (un k ⟨_, h⟩) ∧ ∀ a n, Dom a n → toC (wchar (ex Dw a n) t) = 1) ∧
  ∀ a n, Dom a n → ∀ f ∈ sec Xo c a n, ∀ k, DifferentiableOn ℂ (rdU (unfOf e F M un) f k) (M.X k).dom

def CoverTri {g : ℕ} (e : Fin g ≃ Σ τ : E →+* ℂ, Fin (Dw.mult τ))
    (F : FamilyUnif Xo (σ.comp (IntermediateField.inclusion c.le).toRingHom) (fun j => relab c σ (e j).1))
    (M : ModelsOf F r) : Prop :=
  ∀ y : FrPt Xo c (CAt σ), ∃ k, ∃ x : (M.X k).dom, ∃ Pm : (F.Di k).La →+ (Fin g → ℂ),
    (∀ a j, Pm a j = perFr (M.X k) x.1 (M.φ k a : Fin r → E) (relab c σ (e j).1) (e j).2) ∧
    F.u k Pm = y.x ∧
    ∀ v : ∀ τ, Fin (Dw.mult τ) → (Sp (DualNumber (CAt σ)) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G.left),
      (∀ τ (i : Fin (Dw.mult τ)), IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (F.ex k Pm z).left) (Pi.single (e.symm ⟨τ, i⟩) 1) (v τ i)) →
      ∀ τ (i : Fin (Dw.mult τ)), ∃ cf : Fin (Dw.mult τ) → CAt σ, IsUnit (cf i) ∧ (∀ j, i < j → cf j = 0) ∧
        IsCombAt y.x (v τ) cf (y.u τ i)

def FibTri {g : ℕ} (e : Fin g ≃ Σ τ : E →+* ℂ, Fin (Dw.mult τ))
    (F : FamilyUnif Xo (σ.comp (IntermediateField.inclusion c.le).toRingHom) (fun j => relab c σ (e j).1))
    (M : ModelsOf F r) : Prop :=
  ∀ k k' (x : (M.X k).dom) (x' : (M.X k').dom) (Pm : (F.Di k).La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))
    (Pm' : (F.Di k').La →+ (Fin g → ℂ))
    (v v' : ∀ τ, Fin (Dw.mult τ) → (Sp (DualNumber (CAt σ)) ⟶ Xo.univ.t.G.left)),
    (∀ a j, Pm a j = perFr (M.X k) x.1 (M.φ k a : Fin r → E) (relab c σ (e j).1) (e j).2) →
    (∀ a j, Pm' a j = perFr (M.X k') x'.1 (M.φ k' a : Fin r → E) (relab c σ (e j).1) (e j).2) →
    F.u k Pm = F.u k' Pm' →
    (∀ τ (i : Fin (Dw.mult τ)), IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (F.ex k Pm z).left) (Pi.single (e.symm ⟨τ, i⟩) 1) (v τ i)) →
    (∀ τ (i : Fin (Dw.mult τ)), IsDerivAt0 (fun z => (F.ex k' Pm' z).left) (Pi.single (e.symm ⟨τ, i⟩) 1)
      (v' τ i)) →
    (∀ τ (i : Fin (Dw.mult τ)), ∃ cf : Fin (Dw.mult τ) → CAt σ, IsUnit (cf i) ∧ (∀ j, i < j → cf j = 0) ∧
      IsCombAt (F.u k Pm) (v τ) cf (v' τ i)) →
    ∃ γ ∈ (M.X k).Γ, ∃ b ∈ Bor (M.X k).Ψ r, (⟨k', x'.1⟩ : Σ k, Frame (M.X k).Ψ r) = ⟨k, rmul (lact γ x.1) b⟩

def princ (l : Level H) (Gm : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) E) (Di : Datum H) (φ0 : Di.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] (Fin r → E)) :
    Subgroup (GL (Fin r) E) where
  carrier := {γ | IsUStab Gm (LinearMap.range φ0) γ ∧
    (∀ a ∈ l.Mb.map (H.bar : 𝓞 E →+* 𝓞 E) • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 E) Di.La), ∃ b : Di.La,
      (γ : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) E) *ᵥ (φ0 a) = φ0 a + (l.M : ℕ) • φ0 b) ∧
    ∀ a : Di.La, ∃ b : Di.La, (γ : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) E) *ᵥ (φ0 a) = φ0 a + (l.A : ℕ) • φ0 b}
  one_mem' := ⟨⟨fun x y => by simp, fun x => by simp⟩, fun a _ => ⟨0, by simp⟩, fun a => ⟨0, by simp⟩⟩
  mul_mem' := by
    intro γ γ' hγ hγ'
    have hΛ : ∀ b : Di.La, ∃ b', φ0 b' = (γ : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) E) *ᵥ φ0 b := fun b =>
      (hγ.1.2 (φ0 b)).1 (LinearMap.mem_range_self φ0 b)
    refine ⟨⟨fun x y => ?_, fun x => ?_⟩, fun a ha => ?_, fun a => ?_⟩
    · rw [Units.val_mul, ← Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, ← Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, hγ.1.1, hγ'.1.1]

theorem congr_of_dvd_modulus {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] {r : ℕ} {L : Type} [AddCommGroup L] [Module (𝓞 E) L]
    (Λ : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Fin r → E)) (φ : L ≃ₗ[𝓞 E] Λ) (γ : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) E) {m N : ℕ} (hd : m ∣ N)
    (h : ∀ x ∈ Λ, ∃ y ∈ Λ, Matrix.mulVec γ x - x = (N : E) • y) (a : L) :
    ∃ b : L, γ.mulVec (φ a : Fin r → E) = (φ a : Fin r → E) + m • (φ b : Fin r → E) := by
  obtain ⟨d, rfl⟩ := hd
  obtain ⟨y, hy, e⟩ := h _ (φ a).2
  refine ⟨φ.symm (d • ⟨y, hy⟩), ?_⟩
  rw [LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply, ← sub_eq_iff_eq_add', e, Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul, mul_smul]
  rfl

theorem exists_unf_hasPrinc : ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (H : ReciprocityTheory.ChartRestriction.G.Geo.Tor.Host E) (Dw : Datum H) (l : Level H) (Xo : Moduli Dw l) (c : Coeff l), ∀ (σ : c.F →+* ℂ) (r : ℕ) (ξ₀ : E) (Gm : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) E) (φ : Dw.La →ₗ[𝓞 E] (Fin r → E)), 1 ≤ Dw.p + Dw.q → GoodEmb c σ → cc ξ₀ = -ξ₀ → (∀ τ : E →+* ℂ, τ (H.rt : E) = H.rt0 → (τ ξ₀).im < 0) → (Function.Injective φ ∧ Submodule.span E (Set.range φ) = ⊤ ∧ (∀ a b, cc (Gm a b) = Gm b a) ∧ Gm.det ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ x y, ((Dw.EF x y : ℤ) : ℚ) = Algebra.trace ℚ E (-ξ₀ * herm Gm (φ x) (φ y))) → ∃ U : Unf Xo c σ r, IsUnf U ∧ HasPrinc U l.M' ∧ Nonempty U.Comp ∧ (∀ k la γ, (U.X k).χ la γ = 1) ∧ (∀ k, (U.X k).Gm = Gm ∧ (U.X k).ξ0 = ξ₀) ∧ (∀ k x, FromTest (K := c.F) (fun S _ _ => FrPt Xo c S) (fun φ y z => FrMap φ y z) (U.un k x)) ∧ ∀ ρ, FJ.LatZ U.X ρ ∧ (l.A = 1 → FJ.ExpoMatch U.X 1 ρ l.M) := by
  intro E _ _ _ H Dw l Xo c σ r ξ₀ Gm φ hpq hσ hξ hneg hφ
  have hN : N1c := GlobalTheory.T.cmModuli_consequence_of_abelianSchemeLaws
  have hH := GlobalTheory.T.hasse_norm_theorem_quadratic_extension
  have hB : Out.SecBaseChangeSt := GlobalTheory.lawsFieldsSeparately.2.1
  have hT : Out.SecTwistSt := GlobalTheory.lawsFieldsSeparately.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.1
  classical
  let e : Fin (Fintype.card (Σ τ : E →+* ℂ, Fin (Dw.mult τ))) ≃ Σ τ : E →+* ℂ, Fin (Dw.mult τ) :=
    (Fintype.equivFin _).symm
  obtain ⟨F⟩ := hN E H Dw l Xo (σ.comp (IntermediateField.inclusion c.le).toRingHom) _
    (fun j => relab c σ (e j).1) hpq hσ ((GlobalTheory.T.relabelEmbeddings_good_bijective_conj_compat E H l c σ hσ).2.2.2 Dw _ e)
  obtain ⟨M, hM, hX, hG⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_flagDomainModels_of_familyUniformisation hH E H Dw l Xo _ _ _ F r ξ₀ Gm φ hξ hneg hφ
  obtain ⟨un, hun⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_framedPoints_all_normalisations_familyUnif hB hT E H Dw l Xo c σ _ e F r M hσ hM hX
  have hred : IsReduced (CAt σ) := inferInstanceAs (IsReduced ℂ)
  refine ⟨unfOf e F M un, GlobalTheory.T.familyUniformisation_isComplexUniformisation hB E H Dw l Xo c σ _ e F r M un hσ hM hX hun, fun k γ hγ h => ?_,
    ⟨⟨0, F.n_pos⟩⟩, hM.chi, hG,
    fun k x => GlobalTheory.T.framedPoint_reduced_from_testRing hB E H Dw l Xo c (CAt σ) _, fun ρ => ⟨GlobalTheory.T.arithmeticGroup_cuspCentre_lattice E r ρ _ _, fun hA => ?_⟩⟩
  · exact (hM.gam k γ).2 ⟨hγ, fun a _ => congr_of_dvd_modulus _ (M.φ k) _ (Nat.dvd_lcm_left _ _) h a,
      fun a => congr_of_dvd_modulus _ (M.φ k) _ (Nat.dvd_lcm_right _ _) h a⟩
  · exact GlobalTheory.T.flagDomainModels_exponents_levelM E H Dw l Xo _ _ _ F r M ρ hM hA

end GlobalTheory.FrameUnif
